### [1] [EgoPass-Bench: Identifying Objects That May Affect the Wearer's Passage in Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24995)
*Hongyu Wang,Chuhan Li,Congjin Xu,Xuandong Zhao,Hua Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 这项研究提出了EgoPass-Bench基准测试，专门针对第一人称视角视频中的观察者相对于的交互理解。该基准测试评估了模型在运动感知、实体交互判断和相关实体发现任务上的表现。研究发现，尽管模型在运动感知上的表现优秀，但在交互理解上的表现却存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法未能充分评估和解释观测者与周围实体之间不断变化的交互关系。EgoPass-Bench旨在填补这一空白，提供一种针对真实世界第一人称视角视频的基准测试。

Method: 研究团队创建了EgoPass-Bench，包含284条手动审查的评价片段和2,201个任务问题，涵盖了运动感知、指定实体交互判断、无关实体发现等五个任务。

Result: 评估结果显示，最强模型在运动感知方面的表现优于交互理解。GPT-6 Astra模型在91.37%的情况下正确识别了目标物体的运动，但在66.50%的情况下正确识别了通行约束的变化。此外，模型在实体发现任务上普遍落后于两个人类基准。

Conclusion: 研究得出了准确捕捉运动并不能保证可靠发现相关实体的结论，并揭示了模型在运动感知与交互理解之间的差异。

Abstract: Understanding an egocentric environment requires reasoning about how surrounding entities affect the observer's actions. Existing evaluations of multimodal foundation models (MFMs) examine observer localization, motion trajectories, and action feasibility, but leave the discovery and interpretation of evolving observer-entity interactions insufficiently assessed. We introduce EgoPass-Bench, a benchmark for observer-relative interaction understanding in real-world egocentric video. EgoPass-Bench comprises 284 manually reviewed evaluation clips recorded using Ray-Ban Meta (Gen 2) smart glasses and 2,201 distinct questions across five tasks assessing motion perception, interaction judgments about specified entities, and relevant-entity discovery without target cues. Our evaluation of eight frame-based and six native-video configurations reveals a pronounced discrepancy between motion perception and interaction understanding. The strongest evaluated model, GPT-6 Astra, correctly recognizes object motion in 91.37% of 394 comparisons involving the same targets, but correctly identifies changes in passage constraints in only 66.50%. Across all 574 entity-discovery questions, it trails the two-person human baseline by $23.65 \pm 7.45$ percentage points (paired 95% CI), while exceeding the always-empty baseline (36.76%) by at least 18.9 percentage points. Further analysis reveals frequent inclusion of entities that neither pose a plausible physical conflict nor constrain passage, showing that accurate motion recognition does not ensure reliable relevant-entity discovery.

</details>


### [2] [Infer3D: Inverse Generative Modeling Enables Robust 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25061)
*Amani Kiruga,Mark Hamilton,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,William T. Freeman,Vincent Sitzmann,Yilun Du,Ayush Tewari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种逆生成建模框架Infer3D，通过恢复生成模型的潜在变量来匹配单眼输入，并结合冻结的3D提升网络来避免平铺输出，从而在多种控制合成和真实世界任务上展现出优于前馈网络的异常分布外重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的3D重建神经网络虽然高效，但在面对训练期间未见过的异常分布场景时表现较差。本文受到人类视觉生成特性启发，设计了一种逆生成建模框架，以增强模型在未知场景下的表现。

Method: Infer3D框架通过结合生成图像先验与冻结的3D提升网络，优化潜在变量以匹配单眼输入，同时防止生成平铺输出。

Result: 与在相同或更大数据集上训练的前馈网络相比，Infer3D在多种控制合成和真实世界任务中显示出了更高的异常分布外重建精度。

Conclusion: 本文提出的Infer3D框架为3D重建提供了新的方法，特别是在未知场景下的表现更加出色。

Abstract: Humans can understand the 3D structure of scenes even from highly unfamiliar or extreme viewpoints. Cognitive science suggests this generalization stems from a top-down generative inference strategy. In contrast, state-of-the-art 3D reconstruction neural networks typically rely on amortized, feedforward inference. While fast, this pattern-matching approach degrades when faced with out-of-distribution (OOD) scene conditions unseen during training. Inspired by the generative nature of human vision, we propose Infer3D, an inverse generative modeling framework that recovers the latent variables of a generative model to best match the monocular input. To prevent degenerate flat outputs, we couple the generative image prior with a frozen 3D lifting network that constrains the optimization space to a valid, multi-view-consistent 3D manifold. Across a variety of controlled synthetic and real-world tasks, Infer3D demonstrates superior OOD reconstruction accuracy compared to feedforward networks trained on equal or larger datasets. Furthermore, mirroring how human perception requires more time to process confusing stimuli, we introduce an adaptive inference strategy that expends more test-time compute on ambiguous OOD examples while efficiently routing in-distribution inputs. Project website: https://amanikiruga.github.io/infer3d-site/

</details>


### [3] [VisAlloc: Allocating Visual Supervision for On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25069)
*Haoran Xu,Tian Chen,Yuhe Wu,Yanbo Zhao,Junchi Liao,Hongyu Wang,Jiaze Li,Wentao Wang,Yanfeng Li,Rui Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisAlloc 提出了一种双层框架，结合了基于样本的视觉过滤和基于令牌的反事实重加权，以优化视觉推理。它使用固定配对教师过滤器选择正确回答的样本，并在令牌级别根据教师分布的差异提供额外监督，从而在多个基准测试中展示了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的自蒸馏方法可能存在稀释视觉引导信号的问题，因此提出了改进的方法来优化视觉推理。

Method: VisAlloc 使用一个固定配对教师过滤器选择样本，并通过计算学生生成响应的教师评分差异来重加权令牌，额外的监督旨在针对敏感的或教师分布不一致的令牌。

Result: 在八个基准测试中，4B和9B模型分别达到了 81.44 和 84.92 的宏平均值，比 Vision-OPD 再现方法分别提高了 0.97 和 0.52 个百分点。

Conclusion: VisAlloc 方法通过优化视觉推理中的样本和令牌级别的指导，展示了在多个评价标准上的优越性能。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers a promising approach to improving visual reasoning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). However, not all samples benefit equally from privileged visual guidance, and uniform token supervision can dilute visually grounded signals. We propose VisAlloc, a dual-level framework combining sample-level visual filtering with token-level counterfactual reweighting. A fixed paired-teacher filter retains samples answered correctly from an evidence-centered positive crop but incorrectly from a spatially displaced negative crop. At the token level, both EMA teachers score the same student-generated response, and their absolute log-probability differences measure view sensitivity. Additional supervision targets tokens admitted by sensitivity or teacher-distribution disagreement, with shared frozen-prior, template-bigram, and student--teacher divergence gates. Inverse-frequency redistribution preserves the total additional weight within each micro-batch, while the positive teacher remains the sole distillation target. Inference requires only the full-image student. Across eight benchmarks, our 4B and 9B models achieve macro-averages of 81.44 and 84.92, outperforming Vision-OPD reproductions by 0.97 and 0.52 points, respectively. Code on GitHub: https://github.com/Kaelyn01/VisAlloc

</details>


### [4] [Do Deep Stereo Models Learn Shape Priors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25113)
*Linbo Tang,Aneel Damaraju,Todd Zickler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过一个简单的形状空间中的合成立体数据集评估了当前最先进的立体模型边界估计的准确性，发现即使在局部证据较弱的情况下，这些模型依然存在系统性的边界错误。为此，研究提出了一种新的生成性立体模型，能够更准确地预测边界，同时保持与基础形状空间的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的深度立体模型在局部图像和视差线索弱的情况下，边界估计仍然存在系统性错误，这限制了立体视觉系统的能力。因此，需要引入新的模型来更好地结合区域分组证据与全局几何结构。

Method: 研究创建了一个基于简单形状空间的合成立体数据集，包括随机深度、倾斜、颜色和纹理的变化。实验使用了多个当前最先进的立体模型，并对比分析了它们的边界预测结果。

Result: 大多数最先进的立体模型在数据集上表现出系统性的边界错误，即使在微调或从头开始训练后也是如此。基于距离场预测物体边界的生成性立体模型在边界定位方面有所改善，并且生成的轮廓更接近于基本的形状空间。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了当前立体模型在局部证据较弱时边界预测能力的局限性，并提出了一个基于距离场的生成性立体模型来改善边界估计，未来的研究需要进一步探索生成模型在这类问题中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Localizing object boundaries is important for embodied visual systems, since small boundary errors can change the inferred geometry of physical surfaces. We study whether deep stereo models produce boundaries consistent with simple shape priors when local image and disparity cues are weak. We create a synthetic stereo dataset consisting of objects drawn from a highly constrained family of shapes, with randomized depth, slant, color and texture. Despite the simplicity of this shape space, we find that several state-of-the-art stereo models retain systematic boundary errors, even when finetuned or trained from scratch on the dataset. We then introduce a simple generative stereo model that jointly predicts disparity and a distance field representing object boundaries. The model improves boundary localization and produces contours that adhere more closely to the underlying shape space. These results expose limitations in the boundary predictions of current stereo architectures when local evidence is weak, and motivate further study of whether generative models can better combine regional grouping evidence with global geometric structure.

</details>


### [5] [TALD: Transferability-Aware Logit Disentanglement for Cross-Modal Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25126)
*Hui Li,Pengfei Yang,Xinyi Feng,Junhong Liu,Tianyang Zheng,Hui Zeng,Le Dong,Kaibing Zhang,Mengting Zhao,Quan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TALD 提出了一种两阶段框架，通过任务依赖的自我获益和跨模态独有性标准分解 logits，实现了可转移的隐分量分离，并通过自适应样本调节和学习到的通用模态分量监督最终学生模型，在多个基准测试中显著提高了目标模型的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决跨模态知识蒸馏中的负面转移问题，特别是当源模态监督和目标模态的判别特性存在冲突时。

Method: TALD 框架分为两阶段：首先通过残差解耦器将来自于教师和独立训练的目标模态参考学生的 logits 分解为通用模态和特定模态分量；接着通过自适应样本调节器调节分解的约束和样本特定的融合权重，并学习通用模态分量以适应性地监督最终的学生模型。

Result: 在五个跨视、听、文本的基准测试中，TALD 获得了最大的平均准确度提升和最高的正向转移频率。在十个迁移方向的十次评估中，TALD 优于无蒸馏训练的结果高出 84%，而基线方法仅高出最多 70%，同时提供有利的准确度与效率权衡。

Conclusion: TALD 通过改进跨模态知识蒸馏的方法，成功提高了目标模型的迁移性能，并优化了准确性和效率之间的权衡。

Abstract: Cross-modal knowledge distillation can incur negative transfer when source-modality supervision conflicts with the target modality's discriminative characteristics. We propose TALD, a two-stage framework for Transferability-Aware Logit Disentanglement in class-aligned logit space. Guided by task-dependent self-benefiting and cross-modal exclusivity criteria, a Residual Disentangler decomposes logits from a teacher and an independently trained target-modality reference student into modality-general and modality-specific components. A Sample-Adaptive Modulator regulates decomposition constraints and sample-specific fusion weights. The learned modality-general components are then adaptively fused to supervise the final student. Across five benchmarks spanning vision, audio, and text, TALD achieves the largest mean accuracy gain and highest positive-transfer frequency among the compared methods. Across ten transfer directions with five runs each, TALD improves over training without distillation in 84% of evaluations, compared with at most 70% for the baselines, while offering a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off. We plan to release the code.

</details>


### [6] [CoRelate: Coordinated Human-Object Motion Synthesis through Interaction Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25166)
*Zhiwen Qiu,Daisy Lin,Diyu Zhou,Jialin Wu,Rosita Ji,Saleh Kalantari*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoRelate 是一个通过显式交互关系和任务反馈协调人体和物体运动的扩散框架，提升了物体目标满意度和接触F1值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在达到目标后不保证接触，调整以维护接触可能干扰任务完成。CoRelate通过引入显式的交互关系和任务反馈改进了协作。

Method: CoRelate通过手部部分关系来指定参与的手、参考位置和参与区间。每个去噪步骤中，一个与生成器联合训练的临时精炼器结合关系偏差、任务残差和当前预测的运动特征，共同更新人体和物体在序列上的运动。生成后，时态运动校正通过优化局部运动片段来修补接触失败，同时保留物体运动轨迹和已保护的人体运动。

Result: 实验表明CoRelate在对象目标满意度、接触F1值和完全交互成功率方面优于对比基准。

Conclusion: 从关系条件和任务反馈中受益，验证了CoRelate的有效性和改进潜力。

Abstract: Human-object motion synthesis requires coordinated human participation as objects move toward specified goals. However, reaching object targets does not ensure that contact is established and maintained, while adjustments that improve contact may interfere with task completion. We introduce CoRelate, a diffusion framework that coordinates full-body human-object motion through explicit interaction relations and task feedback for rigid and articulated objects. We represent intended participation through hand-part relations that specify the participating hand, its part-relative reference position, and participation interval. These relations serve both as generation conditions and as references for measuring interaction realization. At each denoising step, a temporal refiner trained jointly with the generator combines relation deviations, task residuals, and motion features from the current prediction to jointly update human and object motion across the sequence. After generation, temporal motion correction addresses remaining contact failures by optimizing local motion segments while preserving object trajectories and protected human motion. Experiments demonstrate improved object-goal satisfaction, contact F1, and complete interaction success over the evaluated baselines. Ablation studies support the benefits of relation conditioning and the complementary roles of task and relation feedback.

</details>


### [7] [What a Calibrated Probability Hides: Belief-Level Post-Training for Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25168)
*Xinye Li,Zirui Song,Huaxing Liu,Xiang Wang,Shuai Li,Zixiang Xu,Yinjun Liu,Zhewen Zhang,Xiaojun Chang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BGPO 提出了一种优化策略，通过估计信念的盛行率并将预测分布与信念的盛行率结合，从而在预测中反映主观性与分歧。该方法在多个基准测试中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法区分预测不确定性与主观性，而BGPO旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: BGPO 通过提出信念列表，估计它们的盛行率，为每种信念预测选择分布。使用贝叶斯规则估计每种信念生成观测选择的可能性，并据此计算每个信念的奖励。对于罕见信念，策略在观察少量选择后提出列表，并在预测保留观察外的选择时表现良好。

Result: BGPO 在意见、对话安全、艺术作品引起的情绪和照片美学的基准测试中，实现了最低的条件、最差组和总体 Jensen-Shannon 散度，并且在文本和图像任务上改善了条件分歧。在合成图像任务上，BGPO 更准确地恢复了潜在群体。

Conclusion: BGPO 是一种能够更好地处理预测中主观性和分歧的优化策略。

Abstract: A calibrated decision model can predict how often each answer is chosen. The same prediction can reflect disagreement among people who are confident in their choices or uncertainty among people who share a belief. Disagreement calls for identifying whose perspective applies, while uncertainty calls for gathering more evidence. Standard post-training scores a single prediction against one observed choice at a time, learning the pooled distribution without distinguishing these two sources of uncertainty. We introduce Belief-Grouped Policy Optimization (BGPO). The policy proposes a list of beliefs, estimates their prevalence, and predicts a choice distribution for each. During training, BGPO uses Bayes' rule to estimate how likely each belief was to generate an observed choice. These estimates weight the choice's contribution to each belief's log-score reward. To discover rare beliefs, the policy also proposes lists after seeing a few observed choices and retains them only if they predict held-out choices well. At inference, the policy uses only the question and combines the belief-specific distributions by prevalence to produce a calibrated aggregate prediction. On benchmarks covering opinions, conversation safety, emotions elicited by artwork, and photo aesthetics, BGPO achieves the lowest conditional, worst-group, and overall Jensen-Shannon divergence across model initializations. It improves conditional divergence on both text and image tasks. On a synthetic image task with known beliefs, BGPO recovers the underlying groups more accurately than baselines trained with belief labels.

</details>


### [8] [MV-JEPA: Post-Training Video Encoders for Cross-View Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25172)
*Yangchen Zhao,Ali Payani,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过多视角预测后训练方法，在保留单视角推理能力的同时，增强跨视角实例和粗略时间对应关系，显著提高了JEPA视频编码器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准预训练方法未能充分利用相同对象或时刻的多视角摄像头视图，导致学习到的表示无法可靠地支持不同视角之间的匹配。

Method: 引入多视角预测后训练方法，共享编码器独立处理遮罩上下文视图，训练时预测器联合预测遮罩上下文区域的潜在标记和完全隔离视图。

Result: 经过少量9000次后训练更新后，MV-JEPA在多个数据集上的跨视角实例检索和粗略跨摄像头时刻检索性能显著提升，进一步在零样本动作阶段检索和冻结特征分类上取得成效。

Conclusion: 研究表明，多视角预测后训练方法能增强JEPA视频编码器的跨视角实例和粗略时间对应关系，而无需修改编码器架构或单视角推理接口，且保持了运动识别、图像识别和密集语义分割等性能。

Abstract: JEPA-based video encoders learn strong representations for video and image understanding through prediction in latent space. However, their standard pretraining predicts features within individual images or video clips, without explicitly exploiting paired camera views of the same object or moment. Consequently, the learned representations may not reliably support matching across different camera viewpoints. To solve this problem, we introduce multi-view predictive post-training to strengthen cross-view instance and temporal correspondence while retaining independent single-view inference. A shared encoder processes masked context views independently, and a training-time predictor jointly predicts latent tokens for masked context regions and a fully held-out view. We alternate cross-view prediction with single-view masked prediction on general image and video data to help retain the encoder's pretrained capabilities. After only 9,000 post-training updates on a single GPU, MV-JEPA improves cross-view instance retrieval and coarse cross-camera moment retrieval on various datasets, with further gains in zero-shot action-phase retrieval and frozen-feature classification on AE2. Frozen-probe evaluations further indicate that post-training largely preserves motion recognition, image recognition, and dense semantic segmentation performance on the evaluated benchmarks. Together, these results demonstrate that multi-view predictive post-training can strengthen instance and coarse temporal correspondence in JEPA video encoders without modifying the encoder architecture or single-view inference interface.

</details>


### [9] [Phantom Success, Silent Failure: Auditing the Success Checkers of Simulated Manipulation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25179)
*Zirui Song,Jiadong Zhang,Zheng Lu,Xiaosong Ma,Xiaojun Chang,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimAudit 通过模拟和视觉验证，发现了多个任务中由机械臂模拟完成指令时产生的缺陷，包括虚假成功和隐性失败，并建议未来基准测试需要对不完全执行和专家演示的有效替代方案进行测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模拟操作基准依赖于手动编写的检查器，而这些检查器可能无法正确评估学习策略的表现。需要引入一种方法来验证这些检查器的有效性。

Method: SimAudit 使用基于大规模语言模型的方法，通过模拟环境重新创造任务场景，然后运行原始检查器，利用多视角渲染来验证执行行为是否满足指令。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0、RoboCasa365 和 RoboDojo 上，发现 183 个模拟测试案例中的预测检查器结果。通过视觉验证和去重后，共发现了 49 个任务中的 61 个独特缺陷，包括 40 个虚假成功和 21 个隐性失败。缺陷类型在不同环境中有所不同，但大多数集中在不完全执行接受和完整执行拒绝上。

Conclusion: 这些发现表明需要在未来的基准测试中评估不完全执行和专家演示的有效替代方案。需采用更严格的评估准则来验证这些检查器的有效性。

Abstract: Simulated manipulation benchmarks measure policy performance using hand-written success checkers that label each episode as a success or failure. Passing an expert demonstration shows that a checker accepts one way of completing the task. Learned policies, however, may stop after partial progress or complete the task in a different way. A checker may then accept an incomplete execution (phantom success) or reject an execution that satisfies the instruction (silent failure). In this paper, we introduce SimAudit, an LLM-based pipeline that audits success checkers against task instructions. SimAudit analyzes the instruction and checker code to identify scenarios where the two may disagree, recreates those scenarios in simulation, and runs the original checker. Multi-view renderings are then used to verify whether the observed behavior satisfies the instruction. Across RoboTwin 2.0, RoboCasa365, and RoboDojo, 183 simulated test cases produced the predicted checker verdict. Visual verification and deduplication yielded 61 distinct defects across 49 tasks, including 40 phantom successes and 21 silent failures. Most defects in RoboCasa365 accepted incomplete executions, most in RoboDojo rejected valid executions, and both error types were common in RoboTwin 2.0. These findings suggest that validating success checkers requires testing both incomplete executions and valid alternatives to expert demonstrations, and motivate more rigorous evaluation protocols for future benchmarks.

</details>


### [10] [Identity World Model: Recognizing People in an Imagined Latent World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25204)
*Dingqiang Ye,Kartik Narayan,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种身份世界模型IDWorld，旨在通过想象身份在不同条件下的特征来实现鲁棒的人体识别，相较于像素级的想象，该模型保持了相近的准确率但速度提高了11倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大型视觉模型虽然能快速识别全身人体，但在应对实际变化时仍不够稳健，常依赖于易变因素如着装、纹理、姿态或视角。因此，本文提出了一种新的方法，通过在一个变化条件下想象身份如何被表示，而非简单地忽略这些因素，来增强识别的鲁棒性。

Method: IDWorld使用了一个隐含世界模型来预测身份在不同条件下的特征。模型在共享的、标准化条件下运行，如相同的服装、固定视角或裸体轮廓，而不渲染任何像素。对于每对查询-库，IDWorld会自适应地融合每种想象的条件，以获得最佳结果。

Result: IDWorld在CCPG和CCGR-MINI上达到了最先进的结果，优于MEVID上的比较方法，并且在CASIA-B*和SUSTech1K上的泛化能力最强。其性能在CCVID上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 身份世界模型IDWorld提供了一条实现稳健识别的简单而有效的方法，代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Whole-body human recognition has advanced rapidly with large vision models. However, existing methods remain fragile under real-world variations, as they tie identity to transient cues such as clothing, texture, pose, or viewpoint. We argue that a robust recognizer should not merely learn to ignore these cues, but imagine how a person would be represented if they changed. To this end, we propose IDWorld, an identity world model: just as a latent world model predicts the features of a scene after an action, IDWorld predicts how a person would be represented under a changed condition. In a single forward pass, IDWorld imagines these counterfactual features under shared canonical conditions, such as the same outfit, a fixed view, or a bare silhouette, without rendering any pixels. Compared with explicit imagination in pixels, this latent imagination keeps nearly the same accuracy while being over 11x faster, at 5.9 ms per frame. Since each imagined condition has its own strengths, IDWorld then fuses them adaptively, weighting each condition for every probe-gallery pair. Extensive experiments show that IDWorld achieves state-of-the-art results on CCPG and CCGR-MINI, outperforms the compared methods on MEVID, and generalizes best to CASIA-B* and SUSTech1K, with the best mAP on CCVID. These results suggest identity world models offer a simple yet effective path to robust recognition. Code will be released.

</details>


### [11] [Recurrent Vision Language Models Solve the Shell Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25210)
*Dingqiang Ye,Jiacong Xu,Wen Zhang,Yiqun Mei,Vishal M. Patel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为RecurrentVLM的方法，通过使预训练的VLM中的每个注意力层在时间上递归，实现了对隐藏物体状态的准确追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLMs）在跟踪视频中的隐藏物体方面存在挑战，尤其是在处理序列依赖性强的情景时容易失效。

Method: RecurrentVLM通过递归机制让每个注意力层不断重新计算其输出，从而在时间维度上保持状态的一致性。

Result: RecurrentVLM在VET-Bench和MET-Bench上达到了100%和99.6%的准确率，在50次交换的游戏中也实现了99%的高精度，且仅需最多30次交换的训练。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，递归VLMs可能成为追踪视频中未观察到的物体状态的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Tracking a hidden object through a video is effortless for humans, yet it remains a bottleneck for vision language models (VLMs). In the shell game, where a ball is hidden under one of several identical cups that are then swapped, VLMs that answer directly stay near chance, thinking models fail on long games, and fine-tuning learns only the lengths it has seen. We argue that the limit is serial computation: each swap must be composed with the last result, and a VLM of fixed depth has only a fixed number of state transitions for it. To address this, we propose RecurrentVLM, which makes every attention layer of a pretrained VLM recur over time by computing the keys and values of finished frame groups from the layer's own output. With a single answer token, RecurrentVLM reaches 100% on VET-Bench and 99.6% on MET-Bench, and 99% on 50-swap games after training on at most 30 swaps. We also observe several interesting phenomena: length generalization emerges suddenly at an aha moment; without recurrence, each swap costs an attention layer; the ball's state forms in the first four attention layers; and recurrence keeps the latest state at the cost of details of earlier moments. These results suggest that recurrent VLMs are a potential solution for tracking the states of unobserved objects in video. Code will be released.

</details>


### [12] [Beyond Restoration: Content-Degradation Factor Unfolding for Real-World Concealed Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25239)
*Rihan Zhang,Fengyang Xiao,Yuhan Chen,Sina Farsiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入因子展开网络（FUN），将内容因素R作为直接语义证据，以及将退化因素L用于生成退化相关变化的空间可靠性图，实现了退化的因子化，以增强视觉感知。该方法在多任务下的稳定表现表明其泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于RGB分割会将内容线索和退化线索混淆，而基于修复的方法主要关注图像恢复和任务交互，因此需要一个能够明确分离内容证据和退化影响的方法，以提高退化条件下可靠视觉感知的能力。

Method: 该方法引入了一种新的网络——因子展开网络（FUN），此网络通过利用内容因子R作为分割的直接语义证据，以及退化因子L来捕获退化相关的变化并生成空间可靠性图，来实现内容退化的分离。此外，该方法还提出了一种统一的能量函数，将观察形成与可靠性加权的前景-背景分离结合在一起，实现了从粗到细的网络结构。

Result: 该研究证明了FUN在干净和退化条件下以及各种分割任务中的广泛适用性和鲁棒性，展示了其优越的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究探索了一种全新的退化因子化方法，通过增强对退化内容的感知，以实现更可靠的视觉感知。

Abstract: Reliable visual perception under degradation requires distinguishing task-relevant content from degradation-induced variations, especially in concealed object segmentation (COS), where foreground cues are inherently weak. The central challenge is not merely improving visibility, but identifying reliable content evidence amid degradation-induced variations. RGB-space segmentation often entangles content and degradation cues, while restoration-based methods primarily focus on image recovery and task interaction. This motivates explicit factorization to distinguish content evidence from degradation effects and assess its reliability. Retinex provides a spatially aligned basis for this separation but remains illumination-centric. We therefore extend it to content-degradation formation with task-asymmetric factor roles. The Factor Unfolding Network (FUN) uses the content factor R as the sole source of direct semantic evidence for segmentation, while the degradation factor L captures degradation-related variations and generates a spatial reliability map to calibrate this evidence. A unified energy couples observation formation with reliability-weighted foreground-background separation, and its alternating updates yield a coarse-to-fine network without clean-image supervision. In this way, FUN factorizes degradation for perception rather than removing it. Extensive experiments across clean and degraded conditions and diverse segmentation tasks demonstrate robust performance and generalization. Code will be released.

</details>


### [13] [Branch Caching After Step Distillation in Four- and Eight-Step Image Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25248)
*Mingyu Liang,Dingkun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过固定支路缓存替换部分注意力和MLP评估，节省了生成器的去噪步骤时间，并保持了学生模型的噪声进度。在特定尺寸下，通过几个支路类型的重用，去噪时间显著减少，提高了图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索是否可以将提取的图像生成器重新利用去噪过程中的特征，以缩短生成过程，同时保持图像质量。

Method: 研究了一个固定分支缓存，替换了特定的注意力和MLP评估。使用同一初始噪声下的完整轨迹和原始缓存轨迹，为每种分支类型和重用位置提供了一个标量修正。评估结果表明，支路缓存和修正可以大幅减少去噪时间。

Result: 在1024²尺寸下，一个内部重用可以使四步Qwen-Image-Lightning去噪时间缩短至0.977秒，八步Lightning和八步Hyper-FLUX分别加速1.51倍和1.53倍。此外，修正后的图像质量优于单纯的重用，特别是在Lightning的学生模型上表现为0.66和0.87 dB的提升。在Hyper-FLUX上，PSNR提高了0.01 dB，LPIPS分数有所改善。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，固定分支缓存可以有效减少去噪时间并保持图像质量，特别是在特定模型和任务中表现出明显的改进。

Abstract: Can a distilled image generator save time by reusing features between its few remaining denoising steps? We study a fixed branch cache that replaces selected attention and MLP evaluations while preserving the student's noise schedule. One full trajectory and one raw-cache trajectory from the same initial noise supply a scalar correction for each branch type and reuse position. At $1024^2$, one internal reuse reduces four-step Qwen-Image-Lightning denoising time from 1.279 to 0.977 seconds. Three reuses yield $1.51\times$ speedup on eight-step Lightning and $1.53\times$ on eight-step Hyper-FLUX. We evaluate image quality on all 200 DrawBench prompts. With the reuse positions held fixed, correction improves mean same-seed image fidelity over raw reuse by 0.66 and 0.87 dB on the two Lightning students. On Hyper-FLUX the PSNR difference is 0.01 dB, while LPIPS improves from 0.1625 to 0.1546. These results measure the effect of branch caching and correction while retaining each student's original sampling grid.

</details>


### [14] [Keel: A Frontier Model Teaches a Vision-Language Model by Rewriting Its Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25257)
*Jiajun Fan,Neil He,Bangji Yang,Qinglong Zheng,Ziwen Wang,Chaoran Cheng,Changyi Yang,Hanqi Mao,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Keel方法通过修改Vision-Language模型的权重来解决其在图像任务中产生的循环问题，这种方法无需训练，保持模型大小和每_token成本，并且在多个图像基准测试中提高了模型的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的补救方法往往只针对解码部分对视觉语言模型进行改进，而Keel方法通过直接编辑模型权重来解决模型中存在的循环问题，从而更有效地解决问题。

Method: Keel方法通过让一个前端多模态LLM（称为编辑器）充当代理，识别并修正模型权重中的问题，并通过失败 census 和图像显示工具反馈修正效果给编辑器，选择能明显减少错误但不影响未见问题准确性的权重修改。

Result: Keel方法在Qwen3.5-0.8B和Qwen3.5-2B两个模型上，在视觉数学到GUI接地的六个图像基准测试中显著降低了循环问题，并提高了模型在平均准确率上的表现，特别是在大规模的Qwen3.5-2B模型上，增加了在GUI接地任务上的准确性。

Conclusion: Keel方法证明了可以通过直接修改权重来有效解决问题，并且这种修改能够保持模型的性能，在多个任务上优于其他无训练的基线方法。

Abstract: Can a frontier model repair a vision-language model (VLM) by rewriting its weights? Small reasoning VLMs such as Qwen3.5 need such a repair: in thinking mode they fall into loops on many image questions, repeating themselves until the token limit, spending the whole budget and losing the answer. The usual remedies act on the decoding, leaving the looping model as it was, or must be told what to change. Yet the cause is written in the weights. Keel, the first agentic weight-editing method for VLMs on image tasks, finds and removes it there. Told only the symptom, an editor, a frontier multimodal LLM acting as an agent, locates the cause, edits the weights and tests them, with a failure census and instruments showing it the failing images and where the VLM attends; among several editors, the harness keeps only edits that clearly reduce the failure without lowering accuracy on questions no editor saw. The repair needs no training, keeps the model's size and per-token cost, and applies to already-edited weights. The editors expose where the loops live: from the original weights, independent editors on both models trace them to the attention outputs of the middle layers, and the smaller VLM stops consulting the image inside a loop. On Qwen3.5-0.8B and Qwen3.5-2B, across six image benchmarks from visual mathematics to GUI grounding, the edit removes most loops on every benchmark and shortens answers, and the edited models are significantly more accurate than the thinking models on average; the larger one gains most on GUI grounding, a task absent from the practice questions. Because the repair lives in the weights, it combines with budget forcing where held-out questions favor it: on the best available weights, Keel has the best average accuracy on both models, significantly above every training-free baseline we compare on the larger one.

</details>


### [15] [Real Cameras are Radiometric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25265)
*Ties Harder,Felix Seegräber,David Nakath,Hendrik Sauer,Kevin Köser,James Pritts*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种辐射光度相机模型，该模型将任何中心几何相机模型提升为辐射光度的模型，通过直接在传感器相对照度上进行逆渲染来进行校准。这种校准方法不依赖特征检测，减少了投影误差，并且能够进行多视角辐射光度重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在提出一种新的辐射光度相机模型，克服几何校准方法依赖特征检测所带来的偏置问题，提高相机校准的精度，并推广应用场景。

Method: 提出的辐射光度相机模型保持了几何相机模型的投影特性，并额外添加了有限光圈、自由曲率焦面和通用的光斑图案函数，通过标准校准目标的相对照度进行逆渲染进行校准。

Result: 与几何校准方法相比，提出的辐射光度校准不依赖特征检测，能在测试集中获得较低的投影误差。首次使用焦面扩展薄透镜的失焦模型到任何中心相机，并将其用于多视角辐射光度重建。

Conclusion: 辐射光度相机模型为扩展生成场景模型提供了可能性，尤其是在多视角场景下对光和衰减等现象进行准确估计。

Abstract: We propose the radiometric camera, a model that lifts any central geometric camera model to a radiometric one. The lifted model keeps the projection of the geometric model and adds a finite aperture, a free-form focus surface and a generic vignetting function. We calibrate it by inverse rendering directly on the relative irradiance of the sensor, from the same images of standard calibration targets used in geometric calibration. Geometric calibration absorbs the bias of its feature detections. The proposed radiometric calibration uses no features, and our radiometric cameras have lower reprojection errors on held-out test sets. The focus surface extends defocus modeling from the thin lens to any central camera, and we use it in a first multi-view radiometric reconstruction, which recovers the in-focus reflectance of a texture from defocused images. The vignetting is estimated jointly and accurately reproduces a flat field measured through a diffuser. Because every standard camera model lifts to a radiometric one, the radiometric camera naturally and directly extends generative scene models beyond multi-view geometry to multi-view radiometric ones, where phenomena such as lighting and attenuation can be accurately estimated.

</details>


### [16] [Accord: Universal Online Metric Localization and Dense Mapping from one Camera and an IMU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25279)
*Jaafar Mahmoud,Arthur Movsesyan,Cuong Huynh,Mikhail Iumanov,Sergey Zagoruyko,Sergey Kolyubin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Accord通过将关键帧窗口与由滤波器姿态三角化而来的元3D点锚定，并利用可见图像之间的双向密集深度一致性来估计并纠正每个图像的比例误差，从而建立一个准确的在线稠密度量地图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决单一相机和IMU的密集度量映射问题，针对现有方法中度量尺度保证不足的问题，Accord提出了多视图一致性（Accord）的概念，以确保密集的度量级地形图。

Method: Accord使用视觉惯性里程计（VIO）滤波器发布元姿态，深度基础模型根据这些姿态预测短关键帧窗口的深度，并利用因子图融合结果子图。

Result: 在五种公开的相机-IMU设置和新的Phone-rig/GoPro数据集上，Accord在每个平台上5cm的均方分数上表现出最优秀的性能，或者说第二优的成绩。

Conclusion: Accord不仅在稠密视觉里程计方面实现了显著的性能提升，还通过开放源代码和数据集的方式促进了视觉SLAM领域的研究。

Abstract: Dense metric mapping from a single camera and IMU remains challenging: visual-inertial odometry (VIO) provides metric motion but sparse geometry, whereas feed-forward depth models predict dense structure without ensuring metric scale. We present Accord, which constructs an accurate online dense metric map from any camera with an IMU. A VIO filter publishes metric poses, a depth foundation model conditioned on them predicts depth for short keyframe windows, and a factor graph fuses the resulting submaps. Recent work has explored several approaches to this problem. What is essential is the multi-view agreement (Accord), which is considered in SLAM as a source of geometric self-consistency. Accord anchors each keyframe window to metric 3D points triangulated from the filter poses, then exploits bidirectional dense depth agreement between co-visible images to estimate and correct per-image scale errors while preserving the metric scale established by the anchors. Context keyframes further improve depth prediction, and the resulting submaps are fused in a factor graph. We evaluate a single mapping configuration across five public camera-IMU setups and a new Phone-rig/GoPro dataset. With nothing tuned per dataset/setup and every map aligned only by Accord's own trajectory, Accord achieves consistently best/second-best mean F-score at 5 cm on every platform. Accord is open-source along with the phone-rig dataset for the benefit of the research community

</details>


### [17] [Persistent Identity in Person Tracking: Online Enrollment and Registered Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25283)
*Ratha Siv,Matei Mancas,Bernard Gosselin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究扩展了PoseTReID，引入了在线外观库匹配(Gallery)和残差冲突监护，使用提供的地面真实值(ground-truth)框。在六段SportsMOT片段中，StrongSORT在单轨器中领先，并超过两个Gallery变体。在十一个视频中，Gallery AIN和COPE超过单轨器。注册SVM在完整结果种优于两组，达到最高AIN评分。Pose gating效果不明确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出了一种改进的PoseTReID方法，通过引入在线外观库匹配（Gallery）和残差冲突监护，以提高多人跟踪和识别的准确性。

Method: 该方法利用了在线外观库匹配和残差冲突监护技术，使用了提供的地面真实性值(person boxes)进行评估。在几个公开的数据集上进行了测试。

Result: 在六段SportsMOT片段中，StrongSORT超越了单轨器。而在十一个视频中，Gallery AIN和COPE超过了单轨器。注册SVM在完整结果中表现最好，获得了最高的AIN分数。尽管Gallery和SVM在某些视频中表现不如SORT，但整体上它们仍然提供了更好的结果。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入在线外观库匹配和残差冲突监护，对PoseTReID进行改进，虽然在个别视频中效果不如 SORT，但整体表现显著优于原始方法。这表明新方法在一个广泛的测试集上提供了良好的性能。

Abstract: We extend PoseTReID with online appearance-bank matching (Gallery) and a residual-conflict guard for registered SVM recognition. All methods use supplied ground-truth (GT) person boxes. We average all-box assessment IDF1/HOTA equally across videos. On six SportsMOT clips, StrongSORT leads standalone trackers (79.44/84.72%) and exceeds both Gallery variants. Over eleven videos, Gallery AIN (83.29/85.44%) and COPE (84.66/86.38%) exceed standalone trackers with complete results. This lead depends on two retrospectively added GTA development compilations and is absent on the original nine videos. Registered SVMs lead both cohorts among complete results, with AIN at 95.72/94.82% over eleven videos. They use every GT crop from approximately 20% of frames, including future appearances. On MOT20-03, registered AIN reached 96.80% IDF1 and assigned the correct enrolled name to 90.72% of assessment GT observations. Gallery and standalone trackers receive no per-video labelled enrollment. Both identity routes restore identities across internal title cards, but Gallery trails SORT on three videos (four in HOTA) and both SVMs trail SORT on MOT16-04/MOT20-03. Under six-clip persistent-only scoring, native Gallery gains 2.39 percentage points in mean IDF1 over a matched control, with lower correct-P coverage and 5.02 times as many encoder attempts. Pose gating is mixed; the guard's metric effect is not isolated.

</details>


### [18] [CalibCache: Cache-to-Full Feature Dynamics Calibration for Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25296)
*Mingyu Liang,Dingkun Xu,Jingwei Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CalibCache是一种轻量级的离线校准方法，通过校准特征加速度，提高基于缓存的扩散变换器在图像生成过程中的质量。在FLUX.1-dev和Qwen-Image上，CalibCache在20步采样时，以2.86倍的去噪加速比，将LPIPS降低至0.429，并使DiT-XL/2的FID降低至10.37。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散变压器虽然能够生成高质量的图像，但迭代采样过程代价高昂。CalibCache旨在通过校正缓存特征和完整计算特征之间的系统性差异，来改善基于缓存的生成质量，减少计算时间。

Method: CalibCache通过使用中心加权最小二乘法来离线拟合特征加速度映射。这种方法仅需要每种设置下的一对完整计算和基于缓存的轨迹，便可应用于采样过程中的特征调整，而无需额外的完整网络评估或在线优化。

Result: CalibCache在FLUX.1-dev和Qwen-Image上的DrawBench测试中表现出色，特别是在20步采样间隔N=3,4,5时，相对于TaylorSeer具有更高的生成质量和速度。具体来说，对于FLUX.1-dev，LPIPS从0.563降至0.429，加速比2.86；对于DiT-XL/2，则将FID从16.39降至10.37。

Conclusion: CalibCache提供了一种有效的解决方案，通过轻量级的离线校准机制，在保证图像质量和生成速度的同时，大幅减少了基于缓存扩散器的计算成本。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers generate high-quality images, but iterative sampling remains computationally expensive. Feature caching reduces the number of full network evaluations, yet its approximation error can become more severe under short sampling schedules, where fewer full-compute steps and a coarser sampling grid can make history-based extrapolation less reliable. In this paper, we propose CalibCache, a lightweight offline calibration method that corrects systematic discrepancies between cached and full-compute feature dynamics. Specifically, CalibCache calibrates feature acceleration, defined as the second-order finite difference across sampling steps. Using only a single pair of full-compute and naive-cache trajectories at each setting, CalibCache fits scalar acceleration maps offline by centered least squares. These maps are then applied during sampling without additional full-network evaluations or online optimization. Across FLUX.1-dev and Qwen-Image on DrawBench and DiT-XL/2 on ImageNet-256, CalibCache consistently improves fidelity or generation quality over TaylorSeer under 20-step sampling at cache intervals $N=3,4,5$. At $N=5$, it reduces FLUX.1-dev LPIPS from $0.563$ to $0.429$ while achieving a $2.86\times$ denoising speedup over full-compute sampling, and lowers DiT-XL/2 FID from $16.39$ to $10.37$ relative to TaylorSeer.

</details>


### [19] [ScaleDiag: Diagnosing the Limits of Test-Time Scaling in GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25297)
*Chen Liu,Zhen Fang,Xin Yu,Dadong Wang,Ling Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究推出了一个称为ScaleDiag的控制性诊断框架，该框架通过分别独立调整目标缩放、上下文、目标特定信息和选择策略，来分离视觉输入、指令信息和预测采样对GUI定位的影响，揭示了现有缩放策略的实际效果范围远小于普遍认为的范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的GUI定位方法虽然通过视觉缩放、指令缩放和采样缩放有所改进，但其针对的瓶颈尚不清楚，且效果是否存在饱和现象也不明确，为此引入了ScaleDiag框架。

Method: ScaleDiag框架通过五个基准测试和八种视觉-语言模型的评估，分别独立控制目标缩放、指令信息、预测采样策略的变化，以研究不同方法的影响。

Result: 研究结果表明，视觉缩放受目标占有情况而非固定裁剪比例的控制，指令缩放更多依赖于目标识别信息而不是描述丰富度，重复采样则表明正确坐标容易生成但难以选择，几何一致性几乎不能提高准确性。

Conclusion: 综合而言，测试时的缩放受到不同的操作范围限制，而不是单纯从更多视觉关注、更丰富指令或更多样本中受益。

Abstract: GUI grounding maps a screenshot and a natural language instruction to the coordinates of the intended interface element. Existing approaches improve grounding through visual scaling, instruction scaling, and sampling scaling, yet it remains unclear which grounding bottlenecks these strategies address and where their gains saturate. To address this gap, we introduce ScaleDiag, a controlled diagnostic framework that isolates the effects of visual input, instruction information, and prediction sampling on GUI grounding by independently varying target scale and surrounding context, target-specific information and auxiliary descriptions, and candidate coverage and selection, respectively. We evaluate ScaleDiag across five benchmarks and up to eight vision-language models and find that each scaling strategy operates within a much narrower regime than commonly assumed. (1) Visual scaling is governed by target occupancy rather than a fixed crop ratio, with full-screen errors typically rescued once the target occupies only 0.32% of the cropped view, while further zooming eventually becomes harmful. (2) Instruction scaling is driven by information that identifies the target rather than description richness itself, as even ground-truth-derived attributes such as color, size, and shape are often neutral or harmful, while appearance and spatial descriptions account for over 90% of the achievable gain. (3) Repeated sampling exposes a different limitation, as correct coordinates are frequently generated but remain difficult to select, leaving 5.1--11.0 points of oracle headroom while geometric consensus provides almost no improvement. Together, these results show that test-time scaling is constrained by distinct operating regimes rather than benefiting monotonically from more visual focus, richer instructions, or additional samples. Our code is available at https://github.com/YenanLiu/ScaleDiag.

</details>


### [20] [VoxForge: A Unified Foundation Model for Dense 3D Features Across MRI, CT and PET](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25309)
*Rushil Anirudh,Jayaraman J. Thiagarajan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VoxForge 是一种单编码器模型，结合了 3D 结构、自我蒸馏和跨模态课程等最新技术，能够从开放许可的 MRI 和 CT 数据中学习可转移的特征。在 MRI 和 CT 的分割、分类和配准任务，以及对 PET 的任务评估中，VoxForge 在每个任务组中都达到了最佳或并列最佳的表现，能够同时支持跨模态的局部和全局任务，且标注效率极高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的一般模型和专业模型各有局限性和问题， VoxForge 的创新之处在于它提供了一种单一的编码器，能够从开放许可的数据中学习到可迁移的特征，并能够在多种任务和模态中表现出色。

Method: VoxForge结合了3D架构、自我蒸馏和跨模态课程等先进技术，从开放许可的MRI和CT数据中学习特征，并在不进行微调的情况下，评估其在分割、分类和配准任务以及PET任务中的表现。

Result: VoxForge 在各种任务中达到了最优或并列最优的表现，特别是在标签效率方面表现突出，仅需顶级模型十分之一的标签数量就能达到相同的性能。

Conclusion: VoxForge为处理医学影像数据提供了有潜力的解决方案，为未来的医学影像分析领域开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: A unified 3D medical foundation model promises reusable, frozen features that serve a wide variety of tasks such as segmentation, classification and registration on MRI, CT and PET as well as specialist models do. Generalist models still need separate backbones for localized and global tasks, specialist models are confined to a single modality, with the concern that spanning modalities under-emphasizes fine anatomy, and both typically rely on end-to-end fine-tuning to be competitive. We present VoxForge, a single encoder that integrates recent advances in 3D architecture, self-distillation and cross-modal curricula to learn transferable features from permissively licensed MRI and CT volumes. We evaluate it strictly frozen, the test a reusable foundation model must pass, against state-of-the-art generalist and specialist models on segmentation, classification and registration across MRI and CT, and on PET, which it never sees. VoxForge has the best or tied-best average rank in every task group, serves localized and global tasks across modalities at the level of state-of-the-art specialists, and is extremely label-efficient, matching top-performing models on TotalSegmentator CT with a tenth of the labels.

</details>


### [21] [MoGloss: Joint Motion and Pseudo-Gloss Pretraining for American Sign Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25333)
*Rui Hong,Jana Košecká*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种创新的双阶段预训练方法，该方法首先学习重构掩蔽运动，然后进行伪手词单位和运动的联合掩蔽预测。基于此策略构建的翻译模型在How2Sign、ASL STEM Wiki、OpenASL数据集上优于Uni-Sign和SHuBERT等最先进的方法，同时在减少翻译数据时，这种联合预训练的收益增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于手语翻译和识别可以从各种预训练方法中受益，因此本研究旨在开发一种新的策略，以改善现有方法的性能。

Method: 本文提出了一种新型的双阶段预训练方法，首先通过学习重构掩蔽运动来进行预训练，然后进行伪手词单位和运动的联合预测。同时，为更好地进行手语识别与翻译任务，建立了一个新的数据集，并采用统一的训练和测试分割方法，对比了新的预训练策略与现有最佳方法的性能。

Result: 基于新预训练策略构建的翻译模型在多个手语数据集（How2Sign、ASL STEM Wiki、OpenASL）上取得了比Uni-Sign、SHuBERT和YouTube-ASL基线更好的结果。此外，基于相同编码器构建的分类器在ASL Citizens数据集的孤立手语识别任务上也取得了最先进的准确率。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一种新的预训练策略，适用于手语翻译任务，并在多个数据集上实现了更优的性能，同时随着可获得的翻译数据减少，该方法的表现有了显著提升。

Abstract: Sign language translation and recognition have been shown to benefit from various pretraining approaches. We propose a novel two-stage pretraining approach for the motion encoder, which first learns to reconstruct masked motion, followed by joint masked prediction of pseudo-gloss units and motion. Pseudo-glosses serve as an intermediate structured representation of natural language text and are obtained from the captions by a large language model. We curate a corpus of about 1,100 hours of American Sign Language from five public datasets, estimate 2D pose for all clips and define an evaluation protocol. We then reproduce state-of-the-art translation methods and evaluate them using the same train and test splits. We propose a translation model built on our pretraining strategy, and this model outperforms the previous state-of-the-art methods Uni-Sign and SHuBERT, as well as the YouTube-ASL baseline, on the How2Sign, ASL STEM Wiki and OpenASL datasets. Furthermore, the gain from the joint pretraining using motion and pseudo-gloss grows as translation data shrinks. A classifier built on the same encoder also achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on ASL Citizen isolated sign recognition and outperforms our reproduction of Uni-Sign on WLASL2000.

</details>


### [22] [VideoRNG: Relightable Neural Gaussians from Generative Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25338)
*Bowen Xue,Saeed Hadadan,Fabrice Rousselle,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Zahra Montazeri,Miloš Hašan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoRNG 是一种无需先验几何模型或材料模型即可生成可重新照明的 3D 资产的方法，通过两阶段过程生成结构化的 RGBA 运动照相机序列和百万级各向异性高斯体素，从而实现复杂的外观和结构的逼真渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技术在生成逼真 3D 资产时受到表面网格和紧凑的物理基础材料的限制，难以表达由精细几何和复杂光照传输引起外观的物体，而现有基于高斯插值的方法通常将固定的光照计算在内。

Method: VideoRNG 采用预训练的视频扩散骨干网络微调的两阶段方法。首先，视频生成器生成结构化的 RGBA 运动照相机序列，包括预知的姿态、相机和光照。然后，使用预训练的视频扩散骨干网络初始化的前馈大型重建模型将选定的运动照相机帧的视频-VAE 潜在帧直接映射到百万级的各向异性高斯体素，并学习其神经外观特征。共享的方向性解码器和随机高斯光线跟踪器将这些体素转换为在新视角和光照下的辐射。

Result: VideoRNG 能够生成复杂的 3D 资产，如毛发、羽毛和蜘蛛网，这些资产可以交互式渲染、重新照明，并集成到场景中。

Conclusion: 通过引入深度锚定外观训练和百万高斯体素的高效重建方法，VideoRNG 实现了复杂和高逼真度的 3D 资产的生成与渲染。

Abstract: Generating realistic 3D assets remains constrained by classical representations: surface meshes and compact physically based materials struggle to express objects whose appearance arises from fine geometry or complex light transport, while existing Gaussian-splatting generators typically bake fixed illumination into appearance. We introduce VideoRNG, a two-stage method that generates complete, relightable 3D assets from a text prompt or a single image without prescribing a mesh or analytic material model. A video generator fine-tuned from a pretrained video diffusion backbone first produces a structured RGBA turntable sequence under a known pose, camera, and illumination. A feed-forward large reconstruction model initialized from a pretrained video diffusion backbone then maps the video-VAE latent frames of selected turntable frames directly to millions of anisotropic Gaussians with learned neural appearance features. A shared directional decoder and stochastic Gaussian ray tracer turn this representation, which we call ray-traced Relightable Neural Gaussians (rtRNG), into radiance under novel viewpoints and illumination. To preserve high-frequency appearance while learning geometry, we introduce depth-anchored appearance training, and we make million-Gaussian reconstruction practical through foreground token selection and cost-aware distributed sampling. VideoRNG generates complex assets such as fur, feathers, and spider webs that can be interactively rendered, relit, and integrated into scenes.

</details>


### [23] [SceneAnything: Agentic Reconstruction of Large-Scale Mesh Scenes from a Single Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25340)
*Bowen Xue,Saeed Hadadan,Fabrice Rousselle,Zahra Montazeri,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Milos Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneAnything 是一种自动化系统，能够从视频中重建三维网格场景，支持基于文本的场景构建。系统通过软件框架协调场景构建者、预训练的视觉模型和图形工具，实现精细的场景重建与视觉比较，提高了场景的一致性和创新性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的直接从视频重建三维场景的方法常常存在布局、比例和照明等方面的错误，本文提出的 SceneAnything 旨在解决这一问题，通过自动化系统提高三维场景重建的准确性与质量。

Method: SceneAnything 包含一个软件框架来协调场景构建者、预训练视觉模型和图形工具，通过共享的相机、深度和分割估计来确定场景组成部分的构建。具体方法包括：场景所有者规划环境，编写 Blender 程序，根据可见性和贴合度选择构建层级，完成未见区域并标记为推断，使用适应的光照和支持的运动组装场景，最后对程序进行自动化检查和独立视觉对比。

Result: 在 100 个场景的评估中，SceneAnything 方法在原来的视角上表现优于直接由人工重建的方法，并且在新颖视角的内容相似度方面也优于其他视频转三维的方法。

Conclusion: 文章提出的方法显著提高了从视频重建三维场景的可靠性和精度，并证明该系统能够很好地辅助文本到场景的创作过程。

Abstract: Reconstructing an editable 3D scene from video requires matching visible structure while plausibly completing unseen surfaces. Agents can write scene-construction programs, but direct reconstruction attempts often produce errors in layout, scale, and lighting. We introduce SceneAnything, an automated system for reconstructing mesh scenes from real and generated videos, enabling text-to-scene construction when paired with video generation. A software harness coordinates agents, pretrained vision models, and graphics tools through an explicit reconstruction protocol. Shared camera, depth, and segmentation estimates ground component construction: a scene-owner agent plans the environment and briefs authors, who write parameterized Blender programs. A visibility- and fit-based level-of-detail rule selects the construction level, while unseen regions are completed and marked as inferred. Assembled scenes use fitted lighting and supported motion, and revisions undergo programmatic checks and independent visual comparisons. Accepted programs and lessons are reused across scenes. We evaluate on 100 scenes; subset comparisons show improved original-view fidelity over direct-agent reconstruction and higher novel-view content similarity than the compared video-to-3D methods. We will release the harness, agent instructions, reusable libraries, and scenes.

</details>


### [24] [Search or Scoring? Diagnosing Why Visual World-Model Planners Fail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25347)
*Haoxuan Jia,Yang Liu,Shang Luo,Chenhao Wu,Zixiang Ni,Yingguang Yang,Feng Zhang,Chongyang Zhang,Hao Zheng,Kefu Xu,Congjing Ran,Jifeng Zhu,Bin Chong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究通过分析优化器的候选动作来区分搜索失败和评分失败，并提出解决方案，发现不同的视觉世界模型在不同任务中失败的原因不同，从而提出相应的改进措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高视觉世界模型在特定任务环境下的性能，通过分析该模型中的错误类型及其对应优化策略，使视觉世界模型能够在遇到不同类型的失败时采用适当的解决方案。

Method: 通过执行优化器的候选动作以评估它们的实际表现，并使用错误模型来区分搜索失败和评分失败。根据不同的失败模式，提出了调整搜索范围和利用示范窗口数据两种策略进行改进。

Result: 研究发现，对于PushT和Wall模型，搜索失败更为常见，扩大搜索范围能够显著降低实际结果的错误成本；而对于Metaworld和RoboCasa模型，评分失败更为重要，利用示范窗口数据比单纯依靠模型预测更为有效。

Conclusion: 该研究为不同类型的失败提供了识别机制和相应的改进措施，有助于提高视觉世界模型在复杂任务环境下的稳健性和适应性。

Abstract: Visual world models such as DINO-WM and JEPA-WM plan with an optimizer that searches for actions whose predicted outcome has the lowest cost, its distance to a goal image. A plan can thus fail in two ways: the optimizer never tries a good action that the cost would prefer (a search failure), or the cost, computed on predictions, ranks a bad action first (a scoring failure). The two need different remedies, more search or a better model, but a planner does not record which one occurred. We tell them apart by executing the optimizer's candidates, scoring a known solution with the planner's own cost, and comparing costs on predicted and real outcomes. An error model explains when each arises and so how to use retrieved demonstration windows (DemoProp): start the search from them (a warm start) when prediction errors are small, and average them without the model when large errors make the optimizer pick overrated actions. Across four simulated environments and three world models, on short tasks whose goals the planner can represent, PushT and Wall fail mostly by search, while Metaworld and RoboCasa fail by scoring because the cost, sound on real outcomes (AUROC 0.94 on Metaworld), is at chance on predictions (0.54). The diagnosis tells which remedy pays off: a four times larger search lowers the real-outcome cost only where failures come from search (clearly for DINO-WM), and where they come from scoring, averaging the windows without the model solves 127 of 168 new Metaworld tasks, against at most 87 when the model chooses among them. Within PushT, degrading only the model flips which use is better, and our rule, frozen in advance, follows the flip and solves 678 of 960 new episodes, against 530 for the best fixed use.

</details>


### [25] [Fold the Loop: Decoding-Time KV Memory for Looped Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25369)
*Kun Zhou,Wenmian Yang,Zhensheng Wang,Yanzeng Li,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过对视觉键值（KV）状态进行压缩，FoldCache 提高了循环视觉-语言模型的推理效率，同时保持了与完整缓存相近的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的循环视觉-语言模型通过重复应用共享权重的模块来增强视觉推理能力，但其解码记忆力随递归深度增加而增加，限制了模型处理的图像大小和批次大小。FoldCache 旨在减少这一限制，提高模型效率。

Method: FoldCache 通过在每一步的模块内分组视觉键值状态，并在解码期间让同一组内的步骤共享这些键值状态，从而压缩数据。这种压缩仅限于模块内部，而问题和答案的键值状态仍保持独特性。分组的精细度控制压缩的程度，同时还可以结合令牌逐出和量化等技术进一步减少内存使用。

Result: 实验表明，FoldCache 可将前缀键值的记忆容量减少约一半，同时在五项开放问题基准上达到与完整缓存相近的准确性。这使得在相同的 GPU 内存预算下，批次处理大小可提高至 1.7 倍，使用更激进的 FoldCache-Lite 版本则可提高至 2.6 倍。

Conclusion: FoldCache 提供了一种有效的方法来减小循环视觉-语言模型的解码内存使用，同时保持良好的预测效果，适用于大规模图像处理任务。

Abstract: Looped vision-language models (VLMs) deepen visual reasoning by repeatedly applying weight-tied modules. However, their decoding memory grows with recurrent depth and limits the image size and batch size that fit in a fixed memory budget. Each recurrent step produces its own key-value (KV) states, so the cache holds several copies of the same image. We find that, after an exact prefill, earlier steps still need access to visual information but can reuse the final visual KV within the same module. We propose FoldCache, which compresses visual KV along the recurrent axis while preserving the full prefill computation. FoldCache partitions recurrent steps within each module into groups and retains the final visual KV of each group for its members to share during decoding. Sharing stays within module boundaries, while question and answer KV remain separate for each step. The grouping granularity controls the degree of compression, and folding can be combined with token eviction and quantization to further reduce memory use. On LoopVL, FoldCache approximately halves prefix KV memory while matching full-cache accuracy on five open-ended benchmarks. These memory savings support batch sizes up to 1.7 times those of a full cache under the same GPU memory budget, and up to 2.6 times with the more aggressive FoldCache-Lite.

</details>


### [26] [A Unified Framework for Image Manipulation Localization Under Multi-Type Weak Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25371)
*Songlin Li,Hui Ma,Zhiqing Guo,Dan Ma,Gaobo Yang,Xin Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniIML 提出了一种教师-学生框架，将不同类型的弱标注统一映射，以指导 Segment Anything Model (SAM) 生成候选掩模，并通过 Ordinal Safe Consensus 过程和基于弱标注的直接监督来优化学生模型，从而提高了弱监督图像操纵定位的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的弱监督图像操纵定位方法通常依赖于特定的标注训练策略，限制了模型使用多样标注的能力，从而限制了训练数据的规模和后续进展。

Method: UniIML 采用了教师-学生框架，其中包括将不同类型的弱标注统一样本化处理，用于指导 SAM 生成候选掩模。此外，引入 Ordinal Safe Consensus（OSC）过程，以选出最合适的掩模。通过基于软伪标签和可靠性的加权蒸馏，学生模型能在不同类型的弱注释下进行统一训练。

Result: 实验结果显示 UniIML 在弱监督图像操纵定位方面达到了比最先进的方法更优越的平均性能。

Conclusion: UniIML 框架为解决弱监督图像操纵定位问题提供了一种新的思路，通过整合多元弱标注信息，利用 OSC 方法优化候选掩模选择，实现了优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: Existing weakly supervised image manipulation localization (IML) methods typically rely on annotation-specific training strategies, hindering the joint use of diverse annotations within a single model. This limits the scale of usable training data and constrains further progress. We propose UniIML, a teacher-student framework that maps diverse weak annotations into a unified representation to guide the Segment Anything Model (SAM) in generating multiple candidate masks. However, SAM is designed for general-purpose segmentation and has limited sensitivity to manipulation traces; its predicted quality scores alone may therefore fail to reliably identify the candidate that best delineates the manipulated region. We introduce Ordinal Safe Consensus (OSC), which first filters candidates using trusted weak-annotation constraints encoded in the unified representation. It then ranks the remaining candidates separately by consistency with weak annotations, SAM-predicted quality scores, and cross-view stability, aggregating these rankings for an initial selection. To assess how changes in the evaluation criteria affect candidate selection, OSC repeats ranking and selection while omitting one criterion at a time. The most frequently selected candidate is adopted as the final mask, and its relative selection frequency quantifies selection stability. We construct soft pseudo-labels from the selected candidate's predicted probabilities and estimate pixel-level supervision weights based on selection stability, pixel-level agreement among candidates, and predictive certainty. The student receives direct supervision in regions defined by weak annotations and learns from soft pseudo-labels elsewhere through reliability-weighted distillation, enabling unified training across weak annotation types. Experiments show that UniIML achieves superior average performance compared with state-of-the-art IML methods.

</details>


### [27] [Text Lives in the Guidance: Preventing Silent Text Rewriting in Cached Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25382)
*Kun Zhou,Wenmian Yang,Zhensheng Wang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究发现，缓存能够提高 DiT 的效率，但会导致文本准确性降低，尤其是生成的文本在缓存中被重新生成时更为明显。提出了一种名为 GuideCache 的方法，通过仅在预测指导误差最大的标记上重新计算，可以在保持较低计算量的同时恢复文本的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在使用扩散变换器（DiTs）生成长文本时，缓存已被证明可以提高性能，但由于缓存方法通常仅依赖于图像级指标，这可能会导致生成文本的准确性下降。因此，研究者发现了这一问题并提出了一种新的方法来解决。

Method: 研究者的方法首先通过 Z-Image 数据集测量不同缓存、采样步骤和文本生成方法的技术挑战，接着追踪缓存预测的错误，并表明这种错误主要来自条件预测和无条件预测之间的差异。最后，他们提出了一种名为 GuideCache 的技术，通过仅在预测指导误差最大的标记上重新计算，以恢复完整计算量的文本准确性。

Result: 在 Z-Image 和 Qwen-Image 数据集上，GuideCache 以 2.4 倍的较低计算量恢复了满计算量的提示准确性，并且在某些情况下生成的文本质量与 ToCa、TeaCache 和 FasterCache 等其他方法相当或更好。

Conclusion: 这一工作表明，对于文本生成模型的加速方法应依据生成文本的质量进行评估，而不仅仅是依靠图像级别的指标。GuideCache 作为一种有效的缓存优化方法，能够在保持高效率的同时输出高质量的文本。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers (DiTs) now render long, legible text in images, and feature caching is a standard way to accelerate them. Caches are judged by image-level metrics, which do not check whether the rendered text is still correct. We find that caching silently rewrites this text. On Z-Image, TaylorSeer at 2.9x fewer FLOPs lowers word accuracy from 62% to 46%, while ImageReward and CLIP Score barely change. Sampling with fewer steps at the same compute keeps almost all of this text, so the loss comes from how the cache predicts the skipped steps. Decomposing the cache error along classifier-free guidance (CFG), we trace this loss mainly to the guidance term, the difference between the conditional and unconditional predictions, which caches extrapolate worst on text. Correcting the guidance inside the text alone recovers most of the lost text. We therefore propose GuideCache, which recomputes both CFG branches only on the tokens with the largest predicted guidance error, using the guidance terms that the cache already stores. At 2.4x fewer FLOPs, GuideCache restores full-compute prompt fidelity on Z-Image and Qwen-Image, and on Z-Image it renders text as well as or better than ToCa, TeaCache, and FasterCache while staying much closer to the full model's output. GuideCache works with caches of any order, and on top of fewer sampling steps it keeps more text than step reduction at a higher speedup. These results suggest that acceleration methods for text-rendering models should be evaluated on the text they render.

</details>


### [28] [Zero to Four Views: Unified Panorama Generation and Preference RL for Unobserved Regions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25389)
*Rui Hong,Xugang Ye,Jana Košecká,Kai Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种能够从文本描述和0到4个视角图生成全景图的模型。该模型使用预训练的文本到图像的矩形流变压器进行微调，并在第二阶段通过Uncovered-Region GRPO进行强化学习以优化未被视角图覆盖的部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全景图生成模型大多依赖于文本描述或单张输入图像，本文提出的模型利用多视角图作为有条件输入，能够生成更丰富的全景图效果。

Method: 模型首先通过两阶段微调进行训练。第一阶段将视角图及其相机槽位置ID加入到标记序列中，并通过使用Uncovered-Region GRPO进行第二阶段的优化，从而提高未被视角图覆盖的区域。

Result: 使用一个视角图时，该模型在Laval Indoor数据集上超越了之前最先进的模型360Anything，在室内SUN360子集上表现不一。增加更多视角图时，第二阶段优化显著提升了整体结果。

Conclusion: 作者通过改进现有的文本到图像模型并引入新的强化学习方法，成功解决了全景图生成的问题，为未来相关研究提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Panoramas can serve as shared scene backgrounds for multi-shot video generation, but most existing panorama generators take text alone or a single input image. We present a single model that generates a full equirectangular panorama from a text caption and K in {0,...,4} perspective conditioning views taken by a four-camera rig at one viewpoint. We fine-tune a pretrained text-to-image rectified-flow transformer in two stages. In the first stage, each conditioning view is appended to the token sequence together with the ID of its camera slot, and the number of views varies during training, so one set of weights serves every input setting. In the second stage, we improve the region not covered by any conditioning view. Supervised training computes its loss on noisy latents of the ground truth and never scores a panorama the model has generated and decoded, so we propose Uncovered-Region GRPO (UR-GRPO), a reinforcement-learning recipe that rewards the candidates a vision-language model prefers in pairwise comparisons on crops around this region. Because the slot ID alone does not tell the model which way the rig points, we also offer an optional variant that writes each view's camera direction into its position encoding, which lets the user decide where each view appears in the output. With one conditioning view, our model outperforms the previous state of the art, 360Anything, trained on the same data and evaluated with the same code, on Laval Indoor and gives mixed results on an indoor subset of SUN360; the second stage improves the results for every number of conditioning views.

</details>


### [29] [HOPD: Historical On-Policy Distillation for SAR MLLM Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25391)
*Yuxuan Li,Derrick Goh Xin Deik,Zheng-Peng Duan,Qianru Sun,Weijie Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOPD通过历史在线策略蒸馏，利用历史数据指导学生模型的生成，即使在持续 SAR 解释中遇到数据和地理变化等挑战时也能保持先前任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法在持续 SAR 解释中面临挑战，如数据变化和地理偏移。HOPD 提出了一种基于历史在线策略蒸馏的方法，以应对这些挑战，同时保持先前任务的性能。

Method: HOPD 方法包括重塑 LoRA 状态作为持久性教师，存储每个状态以保留稳定的历史参考。原视角教师引导增强历史学生视图，同时保持空间坐标，并将这种知识合并到单个部署适配器中。

Result: HOPD 在 SARVQA-CL 上的表现优于其他方法，实现了最高最终平均分 54.51，并且在各种任务上都超过了回溯-SFT 方法，获得了收益。

Conclusion: HOPD 证明了在持续 SAR 适应中，可以通过借鉴源获取历史作为监督来保持早期任务性能。

Abstract: Continual SAR interpretation requires multimodal large language models to learn incoming sources while retaining earlier task abilities. This is difficult when acquisition and geographic shifts alter visual evidence, regionally concentrated categories bias answers, and historical images are scarce. We address this problem with Historical On-Policy Distillation (HOPD), which reuses source-acquisition LoRA states as persistent teachers for one evolving student. Archiving each state before consolidation preserves a stable source reference. The teachers supervise student-generated prefixes, transferring historical behavior to the student's changing generation contexts. To enrich sparse replay, original-view teachers guide augmented historical student views while preserving spatial coordinates. This knowledge is consolidated into a single deployed adapter that requires no source identity. On SARVQA-CL, our source-incremental evaluation protocol, HOPD achieves the highest final mean score of 54.51 among the compared methods, exceeding the strongest baseline by 2.96 points. It also improves the final source-macro score in every evaluated task over shared-memory Replay-SFT, with gains across source orders. These results support source-acquisition history as supervision for retaining earlier task performance during continual SAR adaptation. Code and data: https://github.com/zcablii/HOPD.

</details>


### [30] [Decision Boundaries Matter: Graph-Regularized Residual Adaptation for Cross-Domain Fake Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25406)
*Shichuang Xie,Qishuai Ding,Tong Qiao,Liquan Shen,Guorui Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GraRA通过仅使用未标记的目标数据对源决策边界进行细化，仅需要32个优化系数，不增加额外的推理分支，显著提升了未见领域的fake图像检测准确性和AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 假图像检测器在未见过的领域中可能因决策边界不匹配而失效，本文提出的方法旨在利用未标记的目标数据来纠正这些边界，从而提高未见领域的检测性能。

Method: GraRA方法首先构建了一个基于不确定性加权的目标残差子空间，并确保其与源分类方向正交。然后通过源得分锚点生成软标签并进行目标图扩散，同时使用局部一致性正则化边界内的残差学习。最终将学到的修正合并入源分类器。

Result: 在OpenMMSec多个未见过的领域中，GraRA方法的准确率达到了84.36%，AUC达到了91.34%，分别比源检测器提高了4.33和3.50个百分点。

Conclusion: GraRA方法通过巧妙利用未标记的目标数据来纠正源决策边界，表现出显著的性能提升，并仅需要少量的优化参数。

Abstract: Despite learning transferable features, fake image detectors may struggle on unseen domains due to mismatched decision boundaries. Our analysis shows that features extracted by the frozen forensic encoder remain discriminative, but the source-trained decision boundary does not fully exploit this information. However, correcting this boundary without source data or target labels remains challenging, as unreliable source predictions may lead to incorrect adaptation. To address this issue, we propose Graph-Regularized Residual Adaptation (GraRA), which refines the source decision boundary using only unlabeled target data. To determine which classifier directions to update, GraRA constructs a compact residual subspace from uncertainty-weighted target features and keeps it orthogonal to the source classification direction. To guide these updates, it uses source-score anchors to generate soft labels through target-graph diffusion, while neighborhood consistency regularizes residual learning within the constrained subspace. The learned correction is merged into the source classifier, requiring only 32 optimized coefficients and no additional inference branches. On diverse unseen OpenMMSec domains, GraRA achieves 84.36\% accuracy and 91.34\% AUC, improving over the source detector by 4.33 and 3.50 percentage points, respectively. Further analysis shows that reliable source predictions and locally consistent neighborhoods help guide residual learning, allowing GraRA to correct ranking errors that a simple threshold shift cannot address. \textcolor{cvprblue}{Code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [31] [Watertight Mesh Optimization of Textured Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25414)
*Nathaniel Burgdorfer,Philippos Mordohai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MODL 是一种直接优化水密三角网几何与视点依赖外观的框架，通过Laplacian预条件优化和曲率自适应重新网格化，实现高质量的新型视图合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修复现有方法在三维资产重建中难以平衡多个目标和忽视内部质量的问题，提出直接优化水密三角网的框架 MODL。

Method: MODL 结合了Laplacian预条件优化以实现平滑的顶点更新，并通过曲率自适应重新网格化保留细节和良好的三角形形状。优化目标为多视角归一化交叉相关约束，但无需进行分割、几何监督或alpha混合。

Result: 在物体重建数据集上的评估表明，MODL 在新型视图合成方面具有竞争力，同时生成几何上准确且具有优越内部网格质量的模型。

Conclusion: 直接网格优化框架 MODL 在摄影师现实渲染、几何重建和实用 3D 资产创建方面有巨大的潜力。

Abstract: When reconstructing high-quality 3D assets from images, existing approaches often excel at one objective while compromising another, and frequently overlook the intrinsic quality of the reconstructed surfaces. We present MODL, a framework that jointly optimizes geometry and view-dependent appearance directly on a watertight triangle mesh. Our approach combines Laplacian-preconditioned optimization for smooth vertex updates with curvature-adaptive remeshing to preserve fine details and well-shaped triangles. The triangle mesh is optimized subject to multi-view normalized cross-correlation constraints, but without segmentation, geometric supervision or alpha-blending. Evaluations on object reconstruction datasets demonstrate that MODL achieves competitive novel-view synthesis while producing geometrically accurate models with superior intrinsic mesh quality. Our results highlight the potential of direct mesh optimization to unify photorealistic rendering, geometric reconstruction, and practical 3D asset creation.

</details>


### [32] [MIR-Depth: Multi-Range Refinement for Single-View RGB-D Depth Completion of Transparent Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25417)
*Xinzhe Fu,Qi Shao,Yuanfei Xie,Taoyu Wu,Zhihao Cao,Long Hu,Hong Liang,Xianzhi Li,Anh Nguyen,Baoru Huang,Rongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MIR-Depth 通过构建3D全局逆深度体和基于先验初始深度的局部体积，引入选择性循环细化和空间门控修正，实现了单张RGB-D图像中透明物体表面深度的准确恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人抓取和透明物体操作中，需要从单张RGB-D图像和对象掩码中恢复准确的可见表面深度。然而，镜面反射会导致深度缺失，折射则会干扰背景深度，使得原始测量不可靠成为表面锚点。

Method: MIR-Depth 方法包括构建3D全局逆深度体和基于先验初始深度的局部体积，采用选择性循环细化和多尺度 ConvGRU 更新逆深度，以及应用学习的像素级空间门控修正全分辨率残差。

Result: MIR-Depth 在多个数据集（TransCG，TODD，TRansPose，ClearPose，HouseCat6D）上的实验表明，其误差低于 ReMake，DFNet，BP-Net 和 PSD。现场机器人评估证实了其在6-自由度抓取姿态预测中的准确表面几何。

Conclusion: MIR-Depth 提供了一种有效的单视图逆深度估计方法，适用于机器人透明物体的抓取和操作，该方法在多个实验和实际应用中表现优越。

Abstract: Recovering accurate visible-surface depth from a single RGB-D image and an object mask is essential for robotic grasping and manipulation of transparent objects. Specular reflections cause missing depth, while refraction corrupts nonzero returns with background depth, making raw measurements unreliable as surface anchors. Under a fixed sampling budget, refining an initial single-view prediction within a single search interval either misses large refraction offsets or sacrifices fine local resolution. To resolve this single-view trade-off, MIR-Depth constructs Coordinate-Aware Multi-Range Hypotheses: a 3D-aggregated global inverse-depth volume and depth-axis-filtered pixel-wise local volumes around a prior-guided initial depth. Selective Recurrent Refinement then introduces out-of-bounds lookup masking and validity-masked softmax fusion to iteratively update inverse depth from these volumes using a multi-scale ConvGRU. Finally, Spatially Gated Correction applies a learned per-pixel spatial gate to the full-resolution residual, preserving accurate initial geometry without imposing raw sensor readings as hard constraints. Experiments on TransCG, TODD, TRansPose, ClearPose, and HouseCat6D show that MIR-Depth achieves lower error than ReMake, DFNet, BP-Net, and PSD. Real-robot evaluations confirm accurate surface geometry for 6-DoF grasp pose prediction. Code and pretrained models will be publicly available.

</details>


### [33] [Bilateral Evidence Selection and Reasoning for Mammography Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25418)
*Zhengbo Zhou,Dooman Arefan,Shandong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种名为BESR的方法，用于提高双侧医学图像的病变识别能力，在有限的视觉标记预算下实现了更好的证据保留和诊断性能，并降低了预填充延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗视觉语言模型需要在高分辨率影像中识别小而微妙的病变，虽然视觉标记压缩可以提高效率，但单独保留显着区域可能会丢弃必要的诊断上下文。BESR方法通过双侧引导标记选择器和双侧奖励，连接证据存储与比较评估，旨在改善双侧病变识别。

Method: BESR方法包括双侧引导标记选择器和双侧奖励机制。选择器在共享标记预算下，共同优先考虑病变外观及其局部和双侧上下文，而奖励机制鼓励通过保留的比较进行有益的评估。

Result: 在来自CBIS-DDSM和VinDr-Mammo的双侧数据集上进行的实验显示，BESR方法在有限的视觉标记预算下提高了证据保留和诊断性能。进一步的推理基准测试表明，预填充延迟降低。

Conclusion: BESR方法通过有效利用双侧证据提高了医疗视觉语言模型的诊断性能，特别是在资源受限的情况下。

Abstract: Medical vision--language models (VLMs) often need to identify small, subtle lesions within high-resolution images containing extensive normal anatomy and redundant visual information. Visual-token compression improves efficiency, but preserving salient regions alone can discard the context needed for diagnostic interpretation. This context can extend across images: in bilateral mammography, the opposite breast provides a patient-specific anatomical reference. Yet retaining such evidence does not ensure that the model uses it effectively. We propose Bilateral Evidence Selection and Reasoning (BESR), which connects evidence preservation with comparative assessment through a bilateral-guided token selector and a bilateral reward. Under a shared token budget, the selector jointly prioritizes lesion appearance and its local and bilateral context, while the reward encourages assessments that benefit from the preserved comparison. Experiments on a bilateral dataset constructed from CBIS-DDSM and on VinDr-Mammo demonstrate improved evidence retention and diagnostic performance under constrained visual-token budgets. Inference benchmarking further shows reduced prefill latency.

</details>


### [34] [DiLE: Discrimination-Aware Latent-Space Learning for Universal Multimodal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25419)
*Ben Kang,Junda Wang,Yuxuan Yuan,Hang Li,Yuhan Liu,Xin Chen,Wanting Geng,Tong Lin,Pei Fu,Dong Wang,Jian Luan,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DiLE 提出了一种新的框架，通过结合任务引导和策略性检索反馈来改善嵌入的区分性证据，在四个模型配置中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型虽然在多模态嵌入理解上有所提升，但并未确保嵌入能保留足够的区分性信息。为解决这一问题，DiLE 引入了一种新框架。

Method: DiLE 对嵌入空间进行学习，使用可学习的查询编码任务相关信息，通过响应指令和辅助答案生成监督优化表示。同时设计了共享-私有专家适应机制，用于集成跨任务知识共享和特定任务表示学习。引入了基于策略的负样本精炼方法，学生模型检索混淆候选，通过逆 KL 散度目标使嵌入相似性分布与教师的细粒度相关性分布对齐。

Result: 在 MMEB-v2、UVRB 和 MRMR 数据集上，DiLE 方法在四种模型配置中均达到最佳性能。

Conclusion: DiLE 通过策略性手段增强了嵌入区分性，展示了其跨模态和任务的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models have greatly improved input understanding in universal multimodal embedding models. However, this understanding does not ensure that their embeddings preserve the evidence needed to distinguish relevant items from confusable alternatives. To preserve essential discriminative evidence in embeddings, this paper proposes DiLE, a discrimination-aware latent-space learning framework that combines task guidance with on-policy retrieval feedback. Specifically, a few learnable queries are used to encode task-relevant information in a compact latent space. Reasoning instructions and auxiliary answer-generation supervision shape these representations to support both task understanding and retrieval discrimination. To address task-specific discriminative needs, we design shared-private expert adaptation that integrates cross-task knowledge sharing with task-specific representation learning. Building on this, the on-policy hard-negative distillation is introduced: the student retrieves confusable candidates, and a reverse KL divergence objective aligns its embedding-based similarity distribution with the teacher's fine-grained relevance distribution over these candidates. Candidates are periodically refreshed using updated student snapshots, tying teacher supervision to the student's evolving retrieval behavior and confusions. Across four model configurations that span two backbone generations, DiLE achieves state-of-the-art performance on MMEB-v2, UVRB, and MRMR, demonstrating its effectiveness across modalities and tasks.

</details>


### [35] [Raptor: Rejection-aware Adaptive Tree based on Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25436)
*Qishu Hu,Haozhao Wang,Zicong Hong,Yufeng Zhan,Yuanqing Xia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Raptor 是一种无需训练的框架，通过拒绝反馈共同调节候选覆盖率和编写努力，显著加速了 vision-language 模型的解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于树结构的推测性解码可以加速 vision-language 模型，但面对长视觉上下文时，其推测计算成本增加，导致候选覆盖率和冗余扩展成为重要考虑因素。

Method: Raptor 通过提取拒绝感知信号来表征推测性进展、前沿覆盖率和冗余扩展，指导分步更新树的深度、分支宽度和编写预算，并控制执行以限制不必要的写作并适时恢复扩展。

Result: Raptor 在两个 vision-language 模型上的实验证明了其有效性，相比自回归解码实现了 2.39 倍的平均加速。

Conclusion: Raptor 通过拒绝反馈有效地调节了候选覆盖率和编写努力，能够显著提高 vision-language 模型的解码效率。

Abstract: Tree-structured speculative decoding accelerates vision-language models (VLMs) by verifying multiple candidate continuations in parallel. However, long visual contexts increase the cost of speculative computation, making candidate coverage and redundant expansion important considerations beyond tree topology adaptation. We propose Raptor, a training-free framework that uses rejection feedback to jointly regulate candidate coverage and drafting effort. Our key insight is that acceptance length alone cannot distinguish insufficient candidate coverage from excessive deep expansion, and these conditions require different allocation decisions. Raptor therefore extracts complementary rejection-aware signals to characterize speculative progress, frontier coverage, and redundant expansion. These signals jointly guide bounded, momentum-smoothed updates of tree depth, branching width, and drafting budget, together with execution control that limits unnecessary drafting and progressively restores expansion when appropriate. Extensive experiments with two VLMs across seven multimodal benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of Raptor, achieving up to 2.39x average speedup over autoregressive decoding.

</details>


### [36] [Wearable Logo, Silent Hijack: Stealthy Cross-Modal Semantic Attacks on Visible--Infrared Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25437)
*Qike Zhang,Jiaju Han,Xuelian Shi,Xiang Chen,Dehe Zhao,Banglong Nong,Wenhao Zheng,Lei Wang,Jiahuan Long,Jiujiang Guo,Chengyin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LoSA的框架，可以在保持徽标轮廓不变的情况下，通过优化其内部颜色布局和位置，来欺骗可见-红外视觉语言模型，同时保持视觉自然性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过利用日常用品上常见徽标的形状和语义含义，探究其作为跨模态成像模型的携带器，实现同步的定向语义攻击的可能性。

Method: LoSA框架采用轮廓场和低频图频谱模式来结构化内部色彩布局，使用固定的外观适配器连接可见光和红外视觉，并通过粒子群优化联合优化共享设计参数。

Result: LoSA在四个数据集上的一致表现优于基准模型，在同时实现Top-1定向攻击成功率方面表现出色，进一步实验、视觉自然性评分和生成描述/问答转移分析验证了其效果和局限。

Conclusion: 研究成果表明，实际环境中的熟悉视觉符号可以作为跨模态成像模型的形状保持携带器来实现同步定向语义攻击。

Abstract: Logos appear naturally on clothing and daily necessities, where their recognizable shapes and semantic associations make them familiar visual cues. This familiarity helps them blend into real-world environments and remain visually inconspicuous. We ask whether an existing logo can serve as a carrier for coordinated targeted semantic attacks on visible-infrared (VIS-IR) vision-language models (VLMs) without changing its original outline. This setting requires expressive adversarial variation within a fixed shape while accounting for different appearance characteristics and model responses across modalities. We propose Logo-guided Semantic Attack (LoSA), a score-based black-box framework that optimizes a logo's interior appearance and placement while preserving its silhouette. LoSA uses a contour field and low-frequency graph spectral modes to structure the interior color layout, a frozen appearance adapter to couple VIS and IR appearances, and particle swarm optimization to jointly optimize shared design parameters. Experiments across four datasets demonstrate that LoSA consistently outperforms the evaluated baselines in simultaneous Top-1 targeted success. Cross-architecture experiments, visual naturalness ratings, and captioning/VQA transfer analyses further characterize its effectiveness and limitations. These findings reveal that familiar visual symbols from real-world environments can serve as shape-preserving carriers for coordinated targeted semantic attacks across imaging modalities.

</details>

*Xin Zhang,Tujie Xu,Zhiyi Chen,Xiaodong Zhang,Sicheng Yang,Minglei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为VisualTalk-Bench的新基准，评估财务和中外教育领域中基于问题的虚拟角色驱动视频解释。它涵盖了内容事实性、语音与视觉一致性、视觉质量、动作流畅度以及角色内容指向等几个方面。尽管端到端视频生成器可能难以将手势对准特定的视觉目标，但Avatar-O Agent在一项涉及四个代理的研究中获得了最高的视觉引导评分、感知指向准确性和解释效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视频生成和头像合成技术的进步，通过视觉解释回答用户问题有了新的方式。当前大多数基准忽略了评估虚拟角色是否正确指向讨论的内容。因此，研究者开发了VisualTalk-Bench来填补这一空白，特别是在金融和教育领域。

Method: VisualTalk-Bench 包含800个经过精心策划的参考视频和100个独立评估请求。研究者还创建了Avatar-O Agent，这是一种模块化的参考实施，具有明确的交互规划。通过比较不同代理的表现，研究者旨在探索视频生成与特定视觉目标对齐的挑战。

Result: 实验结果显示，内容准确性与视觉质量并不能确保准确的指向。在由26名参与者参与的比较研究中，Avatar-O Agent在视觉引导、感知指向准确性和解释效果方面得分最高。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，即使高质量的视频生成也不能自动保证生成内容指向的准确性。研究还强调了模块化参考实施和明确交互规划在准确指向方面的重要性。未来研究需要更好地解决这一挑战。
