We compare a frozen DINOv3 model pretrained on 493 million satellite images, with only a linear classifier trained on top, against an ImageNet ResNet-50 that is fine-tuned end to end. On the official split, fine-tuning wins clearly (macro F1 0.764 vs. 0.685). However, 28.8% of the official test patches overlap a training or validation patch, and copying the label of the nearest such patch is correct for 83.6% of them. We therefore build a spatial split that holds out whole 2 km squares with a 200 m buffer. On this split the comparison becomes inconclusive (0.648 vs. 0.640, paired 95% CI of the difference [-0.054, 0.016]), and the learning curves of the two models overlap at every label budget from 343 to 33,126 training patches. On the five rarest classes, the frozen model achieves a higher mean F1 (0.566 vs. 0.445), although the confidence interval for this difference includes zero ([-0.05, 0.23]). Diagnostics show that the frozen features mainly miss differences between closely related species, such as the two native oaks. Choosing the input size and layer on validation data (224 px, block 16) raises the frozen model to 0.662 on the spatial split. A comparison that looks decisive on the official split is thus inconclusive under geographically separated evaluation. We release the spatial split and our code.

</details>


### [110] [SpatialMem: Building Quantitative Spatial Reasoning Agents with Hierarchical Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25925)
*Zinan Zheng,Nuo Chen,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为SpatialMem的记忆框架，该框架使视觉语言模型（VLM）能够积累工具使用经验，并利用这些记忆来协调视觉专家解决空间推理问题。SpatialMem通过两层记忆结构和双重状态轨迹优化过程来实现这一目标，有效提升了空间推理能力和基线模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前方法在空间推理中过于依赖固定且僵化的工具管道，难以应对多样化的场景和任务需求。本文提出了一个记忆框架SpatialMem，旨在解决现有空间推理基准数据集存在的质量问题和覆盖范围不足的问题，以增强VLM的通用性和灵活性。

Method: SpatialMem采用了一种基于记忆的框架，其中包括任务记忆和场景记忆，用于捕捉工具协调和选择。还引入了双重状态轨迹优化过程，用于保留成功的工具经验并改善不理想的体验。

Result: 该方法在MetricBench、Q-Spatial和SpatilRGPT-Bench等基准测试中达到了最先进的技术水平，与最佳VLM基线相比，在MetricBench上提升了32%的表现。此外，与无记忆的代理相比，在MetricBench上获得了9.1%的绝对收益。

Conclusion: 本文提出的SpatialMem方法展示了在空间推理领域中使用记忆框架的有效性，并为提高VLM处理多样空间场景和任务的能力奠定了基础。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have shown remarkable capabilities in qualitative spatial reasoning, but remain limited in quantitative spatial reasoning. Current work leverages the VLM agent to invoke external vision tools to perform computation. However, these methods usually bind each functional requirement to a single predefined vision specialist, implicitly resulting in fixed and rigid tool pipelines. In practice, addressing diverse spatial scenarios and tasks demands a broader diversity in pipeline orchestration and vision specialist selection to achieve robust spatial reasoning. However, such diversity leads to a combinatorial execution space, making it challenging for the agent to orchestrate the optimal pipelines and specialists for different scenarios.
  In this work, we propose SpatialMem, a memory framework that enables a VLM agent to accumulate tool-use experience and leverage that memory to orchestrate vision specialists to solve the spatial reasoning problem. SpatialMem builds a two-level memory structure, with task memory capturing tool orchestration and scene memory capturing tool selection. It further introduces a dual-state trajectory optimization process to preserve successful tool experiences and refine suboptimal ones. Moreover, existing spatial reasoning benchmarks suffer from quality issues and limited coverage of scene types and tasks. To address these gap, we propose MetricBench, a benchmark covering both indoor and outdoor scenes and three quantitative spatial reasoning tasks. Our method achieves state-of-the-art results on MetricBench, Q-Spatial, and SpatilRGPT-Bench, outperforming best VLM baselines by 32% on MetricBench. Additionally, the memory design yields a 9.1% absolute gain over a memory-free agent on MetricBench.

</details>


### [111] [Why Flat Drafts Stay Flat: Region-Mean Latents Govern Texture in Flow-Based Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25930)
*Zihan Gu,Jiangcan Jia,Hua Zhang,Lulu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 基于VAE的扁平化颜色块草案在预训练流式生成器下能保持其扁平外观，研究发现在VAE潜在空间中，图像区域的扁平化系统地调整了其潜在均值，通过对这些区域的均值场进行控制交换，可以恢复纹理或保持扁平性。通过第二阶编码扩展解释了曲率如何将零均值扰动转化为均值偏移，并通过分析条件高斯流模型展示了区域噪声均值化的近单位终点敏感性。这些结果为无训练渲染提供了编码和生成行为连接，同时支持在纹理、空间保真度和语义恢复方面的干预策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨预训练流式生成器在处理扁平化颜色块草案时的保真度问题，并探索其潜在机制。

Method: 通过分析VAE潜在空间中的行为、控制不同区域之间的场交换以及引入一种新的第二阶编码扩展，研究揭示了潜在均值调整对生成纹理的影响机制。

Result: 发现图像区域的扁平化会导致其潜在均值偏移，并且可以通过加回潜在空间中的均值字段来恢复纹理或保持扁平性。此外，提出了一种第二阶编码扩展和一种可解析的条件高斯流模型，以解释这种行为机制，并提供了一种在编码前进行像素抖动、均值场缩放和早期边界保持的无训练渲染方法。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，通过显式地指导区域均值，可以在保持形状的同时恢复图像的颜色和纹理，为图像生成提供了一种新的、高效的处理方式。

Abstract: Flat color-block drafts specify layout and regional color while leaving interior detail unspecified, yet pretrained flow-based generators can preserve their flat appearance even under photorealistic prompts. We trace this behavior to a cross-scale mechanism linking fine pixel variation to regional means in variational autoencoder (VAE) latent space. Flattening photo regions systematically shifts their latent means, with offsets concentrated along a shared channel direction within each backbone. Controlled field exchanges in FLUX.1-dev and Qwen-Image establish the effect of this displacement on texture generation: adding the photo-derived regional mean field to a flat latent restores texture, whereas adding its complementary field alone largely preserves flatness. A second-order encoding expansion explains how encoder curvature can convert zero-mean perturbations into mean shifts. An analytically solvable conditional Gaussian flow shows how regional noise averaging enables near-unit endpoint sensitivity at high signal-to-noise ratios. This mechanism motivates training-free rendering through pixel dither before encoding, mean-field scaling, and early boundary holding. Paired low-rank adaptation (LoRA) with draft conditioning improves content recovery and produces photo-like regional means under the standard velocity objective. Explicit regional mean guidance remains effective after adaptation, continuously controlling texture and improving color fidelity. These findings connect encoding shifts to persistent generation behavior and support interventions evaluated for texture, spatial fidelity, and semantic recovery.

</details>


### [112] [AGSR: Aperture-Guided Super-Resolution for Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25934)
*Ayman Alkhateeb,Hadi Amirpour,Christian Timmerer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AGSR（多孔径引导超分辨率）在3DGS基础上通过逐步融合多孔径渲染结果实现超分辨率，提高了细微结构的保真度，同时保持了实时性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS方法通过投影协方差减弱了场景的细微结构，提出了AGSR以通过在推理时收缩这些协方差来缓解这一问题，从而提出新的渲染框架。

Method: AGSR框架在多孔径渲染中使用不确定性引导的特征聚合和几何条件，从传输和深度线索中提取特征。它作为预训练的3DGS模型的插件增强。

Result: AGSR在Mip-NeRF 360基准测试中优于轻量级图像空间超分辨率基线，特别是在底层高斯表示差时。与基线相比，AGSR能够显着提高细微结构的保真度。

Conclusion: AGSR通过引入不确定性指导的特征聚合和几何条件，改进了3DGS的超分辨率能力，提供了实时性能和细微结构保留之间的良好平衡。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time novel view synthesis, but Gaussian rasterization attenuates fine-scale scene structure through the projected covariance of each primitive. We show that contracting these projected covariances at inference time can mitigate the effective bandwidth limitation induced by finite Gaussian footprints, motivating a multi-aperture rendering framework for super-resolution.
  Based on this insight, we propose AGSR (Aperture-Guided Super-Resolution), a lightweight inference-time framework that progressively fuses multi-aperture renderings using uncertainty-guided feature aggregation and geometric conditioning from transmittance and depth cues. AGSR operates as a plug-in enhancement for pre-trained 3DGS models.
  Experiments on the Mip-NeRF 360 benchmark show that AGSR outperforms lightweight image-space super-resolution baselines, with gains increasing as the underlying Gaussian representation deteriorates. The results provide evidence that covariance contraction exposes bandwidth-sensitive structural cues attenuated by native Gaussian rasterization. AGSR achieves these improvements while maintaining real-time performance with up to 139k parameters.

</details>


### [113] [TwinD: Generalizing Deepfake Detection from Blending to Entire Face Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25935)
*Andrii Yermakov,Jan Cech,Mario Fritz,Jiri Matas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TwinD 提出了一种新的面部深伪造检测器，通过在FaceForensics++伪造数据集和两种类型的双胞胎数据上进行训练，实现了在融合数据集和整个面部合成数据集上的均方 AUROC 分数分别提高到 96.3% 和 95.6%。这项技术在没有使用面部伪造数据集的情况下，仅使用双胞胎数据训练，也能达到 94.3%的均方AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的面部深伪造检测器在混合伪造内容上表现良好，但在整个面部合成内容上的表现较差。TwinD 的目的是通过新型训练方法改善检测器在新数据上的表现。

Method: TwinD 利用由真实帧重建的重建双胞胎和将修改过的帧与原始帧混合的图块双胞胎两种新类型的训练数据来改善检测。重建双胞胎用于捕捉解码器的神经痕迹，而图块双胞胎用于发现融合痕迹。这些新数据集被用来训练新的检测器，而无需使用传统的面部深伪造数据集。

Result: TwinD 在三项非融合集中取得了优异的性能，达到了 96.3% 的均方 AUROC，而在整个面部合成数据集上也达到了 95.6% 的分数，高于之前的最佳状态。此外，在仅使用双胞胎数据进行训练的情况下，模型仍能达到 94.3% 的均方 AUROC。

Conclusion: TwinD 提出的新技术在检测多种面部伪造痕迹方面具有优越性，为对抗新型深伪造技术问题提供了新的解决方案。未来的研究可以探索这些方法在其他领域的应用，例如在社交媒体和视频内容审核中的应用。

Abstract: Facial deepfake detectors are usually trained on blending fakes, where a generated face is pasted back into a real frame. Recent generators synthesize the entire face and leave no traces of blending. On a benchmark of 19 datasets released from 2019 to 2026, the best prior facial deepfake detector reaches a mean AUROC of 96.2% on ten blending datasets, but none exceeds 78.1% on five datasets of entire face synthesis (EFS). AI-generated content (AIGC) detectors do not close this gap. We propose TwinD, a detector trained on FaceForensics++ fakes and two kinds of twins: fakes made from a real frame. A reconstruction twin is a real frame reconstructed by an autoencoder, with the real pixels put back in a random region. It targets the neural traces of a decoder. A blob twin blends an altered copy of a real frame with the frame itself, targeting blending artifacts. It needs no generator and no face-shaped mask. Together, these twins provide two complementary forensic cues that generalize to unseen manipulations. TwinDreaches a mean AUROC of 96.3%, 8.9 points above the prior state-of-the-art, with 98.1% on the blending datasets and 95.6% on the EFS datasets. Trained on twins alone, without a deepfake dataset, single models still reach 94.3% on average over three seeds. For reproducibility, the code, trained model, and training data are at: https://github.com/yermandy/TwinD

</details>


### [114] [Point Dynamics as Proxy for Robotic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25946)
*Dong An,Tianyu Liu,Haoran Ding,Wen Yang,Anqing Duan,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric P. Xing,Guangyi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种新的方法，通过将视频帧的第一帧转化为3D点，并利用学习到的点动力学模型模拟点的响应，进而渲染产生的3D点形成像素对齐的视频，实现动力学预测与视觉渲染的分离，从而改善了操纵物体的运动一致性，提高了机器人世界模型的真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人世界模型在预测未来帧时，对象的行为常不一致，这是由于模型在条件约束下，对动力学的隐式理解，无法准确模拟物体和场景的交互。因此，该研究引入了3D点动力学作为链接动力学预测与视觉渲染的桥梁，期望通过明确区分动力学预测和视觉渲染，改善模型的预测效果。

Method: 研究方法包括将第一帧提升为3D点，通过学习到的点动力学模型预测这些点在机器人动作下的响应，再将这些点模拟的结果渲染回像素对齐的视频。并在这个过程中，与不同的视频渲染器进行了实验证明方法的有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明，与其他方法相比，使用点动力学代理可以减少对象运动错误8.6%至6.2%，在真实双臂机器人上也观察到了8.1%的改善率。此外，模拟运动更加精确的视频也表现出更加准确的对象运动，直接编辑3D点也能引导视频的变化。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了点动力学作为动力学预测与视觉渲染之间的中介可以提高机器人感知世界的准确性，动力学建模的进步有望带来更真实的世界模型。

Abstract: Robotic world models predict future video frames from the current observation and a sequence of robot actions. While recent models follow the given actions closely, manipulated objects often collapse or move inconsistently with the action. We attribute this to the conditioning: an action, or a rendering of the robot, specifies where the robot goes but not how the scene responds, leaving a single video model to infer the interaction dynamics implicitly while also rendering appearance. We therefore separate dynamics prediction from visual rendering, with 3D point dynamics as the proxy between them. Specifically, we lift the first frame into 3D points, simulate how they respond to the robot's motion with a learned point dynamics model, and render the simulated points back into pixel-aligned videos that any video model can read. In controlled experiments on the DROID dataset with two video renderers of different architectures, this point dynamics proxy reduces object motion error by 8.6% and 6.2% without degrading image quality, and by 8.1% on a real bimanual robot. Moreover, the video follows the simulation: more accurate dynamics yield more accurate object motion, and editing the points steers the video. This suggests that advances in dynamics modeling can translate into more faithful robotic world models.

</details>


### [115] [MorphTwin: Enriching Deformable Material Responses from Videos for Morphable and Interactive Physical Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25963)
*Haoqin Hong,Ding Fan,Fubin Dou,Tianyu Huang,Yuzhuo Chen,Zhemeng Zhang,Hanxiao Jiang,Congcong Zhu,Gerhard Petrus Hancke,Jingrun Chen,Rynson WH Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MorphTwin 通过丰富当地变形模式，实现了对象物理特性的独立控制，同时保持了标准弹簧力和显式模拟，增强了重建的准确性和交互速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物理孪生方法在处理交互物理对象时，受限于弹簧基孪生中的体积和形状响应耦合，影响了新力作用下的正确响应。MorphTwin 提出了解决方案，通过在线校准的方法，独立控制并提高了物理对象的重建精度。

Method: MorphTwin 通过在一个物理孪生模型中引入更多的局部变形模式，使变形控制更加精细独立，还采用离线校准过程来调整每个图的刚度，保留了标准弹簧力并进行显式模拟。

Result: 在合成场景的逆设置下，与 PhysTwin 相比，MorphTwin 减少了泊松比的平均绝对误差75.8%，年轻模量的平均相对误差22.1%。在真实世界实验中，MorphTwin 提高了重建精度，并在交互速度下比 PhysTwin 降低了高达14.4%的未来跟踪误差。

Conclusion: MorphTwin 拓展了视频中交互物理对象重建的能力，通过增强其物理参数的控制和准确性，在模拟和实际应用中表现出了优越性。

Abstract: Reconstructing interactive physical twins from video requires accurately capturing how objects deform while maintaining efficient simulation. We observe that reproducing motion in a video does not guarantee correct responses to new forces when the physical representation restricts how an object can deform. In spring-based twins, coupling between volume and shape responses creates such a limitation. We present MorphTwin, which enriches local deformation modes to capture and independently control these responses while retaining standard spring forces and explicit simulation. To support interpretable material editing and transfer, we use offline calibration to map shared relative response changes to stiffness updates on each reconstructed graph. Under identical inverse settings on synthetic scenes, MorphTwin reduces the mean absolute error of Poisson's ratio by 75.8% and the mean relative error of Young's modulus by 22.1% compared to PhysTwin. We introduce an egocentric stereo and RGB-D benchmark covering interactions with objects of varying stiffness. Real-world experiments demonstrate improved reconstruction accuracy and up to 14.4% lower future tracking error than PhysTwin at interactive speeds.

</details>


### [116] [Sample-efficient end-to-end Optical Music Recognition through Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25975)
*Eric Ayllon,Francisco J. Castellanos,Antonio Rios-Vila*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在资源匮乏场景下使用主动学习来减少手动注释的负担，并评估了不确定性与多样性的采样策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决大规模手动注释的高成本问题，研究提出在资源匮乏场景下采用主动学习策略，以选择信息量大的样本进行标注。

Method: 研究比较了基于不确定性（置信度）和多样性的采样策略，并在乐谱行级别和整页乐谱数据上进行了评估。

Result: 基于置信度的采样策略在多种情况下的表现与理想选择相媲美，并表明每种场景都有定制化的策略最优。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，主动学习可以帮助提高光学音乐识别模型的适应性，同时减少标注工作量。

Abstract: End-to-end approaches to Optical Music Recognition (OMR) have recently shown promising results, but their performance often degrades on unseen data. A common solution is to pretrain and fine-tune models with annotated samples from the target domain, improving adaptation to new distributions. However, this process typically requires extensive manual annotation to yield competitive models, making it costly and time-consuming. In this work, we explore Active Learning to reduce this burden by selecting informative samples for labeling in low-resource scenarios, where the amount of manual annotation that can be performed is limited. We evaluate both uncertainty- and diversity-based sampling strategies on staff-level and full-page corpora. Results show that confidence-based sampling performs comparably to oracle selection (based on true error), and that each scenario benefits from a tailored strategy.

</details>


### [117] [Multi-View Transformer with Geometry-Guided Multi-Modal ControlNet for 3D Virtual Try-On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25979)
*Sarthak Batra,Armin Mustafa,Adrian Hilton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GS-VTON-MVT 提出了一种三阶段框架，结合了隐式和显式的 3D 理解，用于一致的 3D 桶装尝试。框架包括多视图填补变压器、几何指导多模态 ControlNet 和姿态自适应个性感知注意机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 3D 虚拟试穿 (VTON) 流水线在训练视图的编辑上依赖于人工选择的参考视图和固定的注意力融合权重，这导致了一致性问题，如纹理漂移、模糊的印花和新视角中的不合理几何形状。

Method: GS-VTON-MVT 框架包括三个阶段：(i) 多视图填补变压器：通过全视图交叉注意力联合编辑一组训练视图，学习跨视图对应关系而不是强加它们；(ii) 几何指导多模态 ControlNet：在服装图像、从当前 3DGS 状态渲染的深度图和表面法线的指导下条件化扩散编辑器，补充在视觉证据最弱处的显式结构；(iii) 姿态自适应个性感知注意力：通过基于相机姿态的临近性动态替换固定的当前视图和参考视图特征之间的融合权重。

Result: 相比通用的 3D 编辑器和先前的 3D 试穿方法，GS-VTON-MVT 在 3D-VTONBench 指标上取得了更好的 FID、KID、LPIPS 基于跨视图一致性以及用户偏好。

Conclusion: GS-VTON-MVT 填补了现有的 3D 虚拟试穿方法中的一致性缺口，提供了更高质量和更一致的 3DGS 试穿结果。

Abstract: Editing a 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) capture of a person so that they wear a new garment requires every rendered view to agree on the same garment details. Existing 3D virtual try-on (VTON) pipelines, however, edit training views largely independently and fuse them with hand-designed heuristics such as manually chosen reference views and fixed attention-blending weights. The resulting inconsistencies are baked into the 3D representation and surface as texture drift, blurred prints, and implausible geometry in novel views. We present GS-VTON-MVT, a three-stage framework that combines implicit and explicit 3D reasoning for consistent 3DGS try-on. (i) A multi-view inpainting transformer jointly edits a set of training views with full cross-view self-attention, so that cross-view correspondence is learned rather than imposed. (ii) A geometry-guided multi-modal ControlNet conditions the diffusion editor on the garment image together with depth and surface normals rendered from the current 3DGS state, supplying explicit structure where visual evidence is weakest. (iii) A pose-adaptive persona-aware attention replaces the fixed blending weight between current-view and reference-view features with weights derived from camera-pose proximity. On the 3D-VTONBench benchmark, GS-VTON-MVT improves FID, KID, LPIPS-based cross-view consistency, and user preference over general-purpose 3D editors and prior 3D try-on methods.

</details>


### [118] [Fixing the Twin, Not the Detector: Sim-to-Real Roadside LiDAR Detection from Digital-Twin Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26035)
*KuanLin Chen,Feng-jui Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究通过修正模拟孪生环境与实际现场之间的差异，使用单一8GB GPU训练的CenterPoint模型，在路侧LiDAR检测竞赛中获得了较好的成绩，着重于数据预处理和标签处理方面的改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境中的LiDAR数据标注成本高昂，而数字孪生可以提供生成的标签，但孪生环境与实际现场存在差异，这些差异可能影响检测效果。因此，研究如何通过修正这些差异提高基于生成数据训练的检测器在实际环境中的性能。

Method: 研究开发了数据预处理和标签处理管道，方法包括调整孪生环境中的坐标、点密度和框标注格式以匹配实际测试传感器；生成孪生环境缺失的行人、骑行者及未标注的街道杂乱，重建实际大小和点密度对象；通过非学习的方式将未标注的真实帧点密度调整到每个单元格的密度；在固定路侧传感器的假定下，利用结构化信息和点统计特征进行后续处理。

Result: 研究使用单一8GB GPU训练的CenterPoint模型，取得了Urbantwin Sim2Real LiDAR挑战赛LUMPI赛道第1名（0.5541联合得分）和V2X-Real赛道第5名（0.8102）的好成绩。数据预处理显著提升了检测模型在测试集上的表现，有四分之一到三分之二的验证提升成果在测试集上得到了保留。

Conclusion: 研究展示了通过仔细调整模拟孪生环境以及利用固定路侧传感器特性，可以有效地提高基于生成数据训练的路侧LiDAR检测器的性能。

Abstract: Roadside LiDAR detectors are expensive to train on real labels, and digital twins promise to replace these labels, but a detector trained on a twin meets a different sensor, different objects and different annotation conventions on the real site. We describe the pipeline that placed first in the LUMPI track of the UrbanTwin Sim2Real LiDAR Challenge (IEEE BigData 2026 Cup, 0.5541 combined score) and fifth on its V2X-Real track (0.8102). Every detector is a CenterPoint model trained only on synthetic data, on a single 8 GB laptop GPU. Most of the gain came from correcting differences between the twin and the real site rather than from the detector. We align the twin's coordinates, point density and box convention with the test sensor, generate the pedestrians and riders that the twins lack, plus street clutter as unlabeled negatives, and rebuild twin objects at real size and real point density. A non-learned resampling to the per-cell point density of unlabeled real frames lifts LUMPI realism from 0.589 to 0.947, against 0.949 between real frames. Label-free post-processing then exploits what a fixed roadside sensor guarantees: static structures recur in every frame, and real objects have characteristic point statistics. Reporting the validation and test gain of each component side by side, we find that corrections of systematic sim-to-real differences transfer to the test set, typically keeping a quarter to three quarters of their validation gain, while choices that fit the few objects of a validation set do not.

</details>


### [119] [Streaming-AAA: Streaming AAA Game Video Generation at High Resolution, High Frame Rate, and Low Latency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26040)
*Zhihao Hu,Jianqi Ma,Jun Zhang,Jiayang Bai,Haifeng Shi,Yanxiang Zhuang,Zhongqian Sun,Weidong Zhang,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Streaming-AAA 提出了一种新的无限生成 AAA 级别游戏视频的范式，能够在保持高画质和流畅帧率的同时，实现低延迟的实时交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在保证视觉保真度、高帧率和低交互延迟方面存在局限性，导致用户体验不佳。Streaming-AAA 的目标是解决这些问题，以实现更优的用户交互体验。

Method: Streaming-AAA 通过重构 VAE 架构进行高效的高分辨率解码、使用扩散强制进行预训练以延长时间和依赖捕捉并保持持久记忆，以及通过后训练的蒸馏和内存压缩提高推理效率并维持视觉质量和时间一致性。

Result: Streaming-AAA 实现了1080p分辨率下每秒43帧的视频生成，具有仅198毫秒的端到端延迟交互。实验表明，该方法在视觉保真度、帧率、延迟和长期稳定性方面显著优于先前的可控视频生成方法。

Conclusion: Streaming-AAA 针对当前可交互式 AAA 级游戏视频生成中存在的问题，提供了一套新的方法和架构，有助于改善交互体验，实现了同时保持高质量视觉效果和低延迟的实时交互。

Abstract: Controllable streaming video generation offers a promising route toward interactive game simulation, yet existing methods remain far from practical gameplay. They often fail to jointly achieve high visual fidelity, high frame rates, and low interaction latency, resulting in poor responsiveness and degraded user experience. We present Streaming-AAA, a new paradigm for controllable infinite generation of AAA-quality game videos. Our system generates 1080p gameplay videos at 43 FPS with 198 ms end-to-end latency interaction on merely NVIDIA A800 GPUs, while supporting long-horizon play without severe temporal degradation or collapse. Streaming-AAA is built upon three key designs. First, we redesign the VAE architecture for efficient high-resolution decoding, enabling real-time synthesis at 1080p. Second, we pretrain the diffusion model using diffusion forcing and significantly extend the temporal training horizon, allowing the model to capture long-term dependencies and maintain persistent memory. Third, we perform post-training distillation and memory compression to improve inference efficiency while retaining visual quality and temporal consistency. Experiments show that Streaming-AAA substantially improves visual fidelity, frame rate, latency, and long-term stability over prior controllable video generation approaches, enabling a significantly more playable experience for interactive AAA game video generation.

</details>


### [120] [PaveSplat: Resolution-Robust Feed-Forward Gaussian Splatting with Residually Corrected Surface-Aligned Covariances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26045)
*Janghun Kim,Hyun-Cheol Kim,Eunbin An,Myeonghun Oh,Hyon-Gon Choo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PaveSplat通过结合解析表面协方差和自适应优化的姿态残差，改进了像素对齐的前向Gaussian splatting方法，显著提高了不同分辨率下的渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于表面元素的方法在深度推导的帧不合适的区域难以正确地重新定向表面元素，导致在高分辨率下渲染质量下降。PaveSplat提出了一种解决方案，将解析表面协方差作为初始值，并添加姿态残差来优化表面元素的方向，从而在不同分辨率下提供更高质量的渲染。

Method: PaveSplat采用了前向的Gaussian splatting方法，通过深层神经网络预测每个像素一个原素。它保留了解析表面协方差作为初始值，并且引入了一个性别初始化的单位四元数残差，该残差仅在优化渲染效果时调整表面元素的方向。这种结合解析和学习的方式提高了渲染质量。

Result: 在RealEstate10K基准测试上，PaveSplat在所有尺度上都获得了最高的PSNR、SSIM和LPIPS。对于零样本的DL3DV基准测试，它在所有尺度上都获得了最高的PSNR，并在SSIM和LPIPS上排名首位或次席。在高分辨率重新渲染下的覆盖孔洞分析中，PaveSplat比基线方法拥有更少未覆盖的像素。

Conclusion: PaveSplat通过引入姿态残差优化表面元素的方向，显著增强了像素对齐的Gaussian splatting方法在不同分辨率下的渲染质量，为高分辨率下的真实感渲染提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: We present PaveSplat, a feed-forward Gaussian splatting method that adds a learned residual to the analytic surface-aligned covariance, keeping surfels paving the visible surface across rendering resolutions. Pixel-aligned feed-forward Gaussian splatting predicts one primitive per pixel from a few images in a single pass, and its rendering quality depends on how well these primitives cover the surface. Recent surfel-based methods compute the surface-aligned covariance analytically from predicted depth. However, this covariance has no way to correct the surfel orientation where the depth-derived frame is ill-posed. The surfels then cover the surface loosely; at the training resolution each surfel's one-pixel minimum footprint covers the gaps, but they show up as holes when the same prediction is re-rendered at a higher resolution. To address this, we keep the analytic covariance as an initialization, with its axes aligned to the surface tangents, and add an identity-initialized unit-quaternion residual that reorients a surfel only where this improves rendering, turning surfels that the analytic frame mis-aims back onto the surface. On RealEstate10K, PaveSplat achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, and LPIPS among single-Gaussian-per-pixel methods at every scale (1x-8x). The native-resolution gain is small; the benefit appears when the same prediction is rendered at higher display resolutions without re-running the network. On zero-shot DL3DV, it leads PSNR within this group at every scale and ranks first or second on SSIM/LPIPS. A coverage-hole analysis shows fewer uncovered pixels than both baselines under high-resolution re-rendering.

</details>


### [121] [Less Than Meets the Eye: Action-Set Identifiability and Principled Abstention for 3D Physical Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26056)
*Reshad Ul Karim,Abrar Samin,Sammam Mahdi,Syeda Maliha Tabassum,Md Rashad Al Hasan Rony*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了通过有限的相机运动来区分镜像、其镜像反射以及屏幕上显示的相同视图但在不同深度的表面的方法。提出了一种名为Intervene3D的技术，能够选择一种运动来最大化区分假设的能力，并提供最合适的物理表面。在合成和实际实验中展示了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在通过有限的相机运动来辨别镜像、镜像反射和屏幕上显示的相同视图但实际上位于不同深度的物体的实际物理表面，以提高虚拟和增强现实系统中的表面估计准确性。

Method: 研发了一种名为Intervene3D的技术，能够对图像和初始几何估计进行处理，生成未来视角，并通过最大化区分假设的能力来选择动作，确保仅在能够区分残留假设的情况下才报告机制和表面。提出了一种适应性测试来剔除无法解释的观察数据。实验使用匹配的合成基准和真实显示器实验来验证该方法的有效性。

Result: 在合成基准中，不启用弃权的情况下，能够回答76.4%的不可解析场景，而启用弃权时，没有场景得到明确答案。在现实的屏幕实验中，执行的一次运动使十二个网络汇总的中值表面误差降低了31.5%，且网络在标注面板内部比外部有更大误差的图像占33.4%，在Mirror3D帧中将实际物理表面置于玻璃背后的比例为36.9%。

Conclusion: 研究证明了Intervene3D技术和适应性测试的有效性，通过有限的相机运动显著提高了表面估计的准确性，并能处理复杂的镜像场景。

Abstract: A corridor, its mirror reflection, and its image on a display can look identical, yet their physical surfaces lie at different depths. We test whether bounded camera motions distinguish these explanations. Intervene3D takes an image and an initial geometry estimate, predicts the view under each hypothesis and candidate motion, and selects a motion by maximin separability. It reports the mechanism and first physical surface only if the available motions separate the hypotheses still in contention. Otherwise it abstains. An adequacy test also rejects observations that no hypothesis explains. On a matched synthetic benchmark with exact landmarks, disabling abstention gives confident answers on 76.4% of unresolvable scenes. With abstention, none receives a confident answer under exact or noisy landmarks. At 100% accuracy on answered scenes, maximin selection answers 61.0% of scenes and summed selection 43.1%. The adequacy test lowers wrong answers on strongly curved mirror scenes from 56.8% to 12.4%. Real display experiments compare two hypotheses using one frozen depth-network estimate. The second image supplies photometric evidence and is never passed through the depth network. One executed motion reduces the pooled median surface error of twelve networks by 31.5%. These networks have larger error inside the annotated panel than outside on 33.4% of illusion-benchmark images. They place the first physical surface behind the glass on 36.9% of Mirror3D frames.

</details>


### [122] [One-to-Many: Diverse and Bidirectional Image-to-Image Translation via Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26058)
*Sho Shimotsumagari,Seiichi Uchida,Shumpei Takezaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: O2MFlow通过一种条件化的流模型实现了从单一源到多种目标的生成，并在反向翻译中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图像到图像翻译任务只提供一种目标，但现实任务中可能存在多种有效的目标。因此，需要一个模型能够在给定单一源的情况下生成多种有效目标。

Method: O2MFlow采用了条件化的 Rectified Flow (RF) 模型，在单一运输框架中实现了从单一到多种的目标生成和反向翻译。它通过在流的动力学中条件化出现的潜空间，实现了通过固定的潜空间生成映射及通过采样潜空间生成多样输出的目标。

Result: O2MFlow在三个基准测试中实现了数据集特定的前向多样性和精确度权衡，并在评估方法中获得了最低的反向LPIPS-Dist，以及两个基准测试中的最低反向FID。

Conclusion: 通过引入条件化的流模型，O2MFlow能够在现实任务中更好地适应多种有效目标的生成。

Abstract: Many paired image-to-image translation tasks admit multiple valid target appearances, while training provides only one target per source. We propose One-to-Many Flow (O2MFlow), a latent-conditioned Rectified Flow (RF) model for one-to-many generation and backward translation (target$\rightarrow$source) within a single transport framework. Conditioning the RF dynamics on an appearance latent turns transport into a family of latent-indexed bijections: a fixed latent gives a mapping that is inverted by time-reversed integration, and sampled latents produce diverse outputs for the same input. We formalize reusable appearance variations as Shared Latent Transport and show that Kullback--Leibler (KL) regularization directly controls the source--latent distribution gap between training and generation. Across three benchmarks, O2MFlow offers dataset-dependent forward diversity--fidelity trade-offs and the lowest backward LPIPS-Dist among evaluated methods, with the lowest backward FID on two benchmarks.

</details>


### [123] [Domain-Aware Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy Spacecraft Pose Estimation under Sim-to-Real Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26065)
*Nidhal Eddine Chenni,Arunkumar Rathinam,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究在SPEED+数据集上展示了基于合成图像校准的姿态估计方法，在实际图像上的覆盖保证丧失，通过引入两种转译模型，使基于合成图像校准的姿态估计方法在实际图像上的覆盖保证得以恢复，减少了未检测错误，推动了姿态估计方法在航天中的可信部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何在实际图像与合成图像之间进行有效校准，以确保基于合成图像校准的姿态估计方法在实际工作中的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 引入两种转译模型，将合成图像转译为与实际图像类似但保留几何形状的图像，用于校准姿态估计方法。

Result: 通过引入转译模型，基于合成图像校准的姿态估计方法实际图像上的覆盖保证可以在0.1的错误覆盖率以上恢复，并显著减少了未检测到的错误，将失败率从43.5%降低到最多3.3%。

Conclusion: 证明了通过将合成图像转译为实际图像外观的方法可以恢复基于合成图像校准的姿态估计方法的实际应用中的可靠性，接近实现姿态估计方法在航天中的可信部署。

Abstract: Autonomous rendezvous and proximity operations require not only an accurate estimate of the pose of the target spacecraft, but also a reliable indication of when this estimate can be trusted. Conformal prediction provides such an indication with a formal guarantee, by producing pose uncertainty sets that contain the true pose with a specified probability. Such a guarantee, however, assumes that calibration and test data follow the same distribution, an assumption that is violated in orbital perception, where labelled calibration images are available only from synthetic renderings while operational images are real. In this work, we show on the SPEED+ dataset that conformal pose estimation calibrated on synthetic images loses its coverage guarantee on real images, and that many of the resulting failures are silent: the predicted pose set appears valid but excludes the true pose. To address this, we calibrate on synthetic images translated to the appearance of real images. Since translation alters appearance while preserving geometry, the synthetic pose labels remain valid and no labelled real images are required. Experiments with two translation models show that this calibration restores coverage above the specified level for miscoverage levels of 0.1 and above, and reduces silent failures from up to 43.5% to at most 3.3% of the test images, bringing conformal pose estimation a step closer to trustworthy deployment in space.

</details>


### [124] [DfD-GS: Defocus Image Prior for Metric-Scale Single-View Depth Estimation and View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26072)
*Shiori Ueda,Rinto Yagawa,Hideo Saito,Shohei Mori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的基于单视角焦点与非焦点图像的前馈框架DfD-GS，用于恢复场景的米尺度几何结构，通过利用焦散模糊作为物理的米尺度深度线索，该方法在合成和真实数据集上优于单图像基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于单视角观测的几何恢复问题，提高视图合成的可靠性，减少尺度模糊和数据集依赖偏差的影响。

Method: 首先从焦点与非焦点图像对中估计基于焦散的米尺度视差图，然后利用这个估计值调整和细化单视角深度先验到米尺度，预测可见和隐藏的3D高斯体以实现消隐感知的视图合成。

Result: 在合成数据集Booster和Middlebury上，DfD-GS在米尺度深度估计和新型视图合成方面优于单图像基线，尽管在WildRGB-D上的深度RMSE更大，AbsRel和LPIPS较小。还显示了在真实数据集中保持稳定米尺度的能力。

Conclusion: DfD-GS方法在单视角几何恢复方面提供了新的解决方案，通过利用焦散模糊作为深度线索，提高了视图合成的质量和稳定性。

Abstract: Recovering metric-scale geometry from observations at a single viewpoint remains fundamentally ill-posed, limiting the reliability of novel view synthesis. Recent methods rely on learned monocular depth priors, which provide strong structural estimates but can suffer from scale ambiguity and dataset-dependent biases. We propose DfD-GS, a feed-forward framework that predicts a metric-scale 3D Gaussian scene representation from a single-view all-in-focus--defocused image pair. The key idea is to exploit defocus blur as a physical metric depth cue. DfD-GS first estimates a defocus-based metric disparity map from the all-in-focus--defocused image pair and the corresponding defocus strength. This estimate is used to align and refine a monocular depth prior to metric scale. Visible and hidden 3D Gaussians are then predicted for occlusion-aware novel view synthesis. Experiments on synthetic-defocus datasets constructed from Booster and Middlebury show that DfD-GS outperforms single-image baselines in metric depth estimation and novel view synthesis. On WildRGB-D, DfD-GS achieves lower AbsRel and LPIPS but higher depth RMSE than the single-image baselines. Additional analyses show that DfD-GS maintains a more stable metric scale under field-of-view changes. Qualitative results on a real dataset show recovery of the main scene structure under optical defocus.

</details>


### [125] [Frequency-Synchronized Boundary Coupling for Training-Free Multi-Prompt Long Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26098)
*Yipan Xu,Shuyong Gao,Jiyuan Fu,Keliang Yin,Jiayu Chen,Qianyu Guo,Yang Liu,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreqSync 提出了一种训练免费的框架来解决现有转换策略中的频率无关边界耦合问题，通过频率同步的源条件注意（FS-SCA）与残差裁剪频谱缝合（RCSS）相结合，实现了平滑过渡和清晰提示对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前技术在从文本生成长视频时面临挑战，尤其是过渡效果不理想，缺乏平滑性和连续性。频谱同步源条件注意（FS-SCA）与残差裁剪频谱缝合（RCSS）结合的 FreqSync 框架旨在解决这些缺陷。

Method: FreqSync 框架通过引入频率同步的源条件注意和残差裁剪频谱缝合，允许相邻片段共享稳定的基本频率结构和运动线索，同时保持高频特定提示细节在目标主导下。

Result: 实验表明，FreqSync 在过渡平滑度和提示对齐方面达到了最先进的效果，并且保持了强大的感知视频质量。

Conclusion: FreqSync 框架为从多提示生成连贯的长视频提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Text-to-video generation has made remarkable progress with diffusion models and transformer-based video architectures, yet generating coherent long videos from evolving textual descriptions remains challenging. Training long-video models from scratch requires substantial computation and large-scale multi-event data, while training-free multi-prompt generation must activate new prompt semantics without breaking temporal continuity. We identify a frequency-agnostic boundary coupling problem in existing transition strategies: weak coupling causes abrupt changes, flicker, and background jumps, whereas overly strong or coarse coupling may over-preserve source semantics and produce ghosting or duplicated subjects around prompt boundaries. To address this problem, we propose FreqSync, a training-free framework that formulates prompt transitions as frequency-selective boundary coupling. FreqSync combines Frequency-Synchronized Source-Conditioned Attention (FS-SCA) with Residual-Clipped Spectral Stitching (RCSS), enabling adjacent segments to share stable low- and mid-frequency structure and motion cues while keeping high-frequency prompt-specific details target-dominant under explicit residual constraints. Extensive experiments show that FreqSync achieves state-of-the-art transition smoothness and prompt alignment while maintaining strong perceptual video quality, demonstrating its effectiveness for coherent multi-prompt long video generation.

</details>


### [126] [HiUni: Harmonizing Understanding and Generation in Shared Visual Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26107)
*Jiahao Hu,Jingdong Du,Yichen Zhang,Yi Song,Zhenyu Zhang,Jiaxin Niu,Ruihang Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Yiqian Zhang,Yong Huang,Zefeng Zhang,Zheyuan Gu,Shuohuan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiUni 提出了一种新的统一多模态模型设计，通过重用语言对齐的 ViT 和密集 Transformer 桥接来直接扩展预训练的 VLM，同时在输出端保留特定任务的计算。该模型引入了 Pixel Diffusion Head 和 HiGate 路径以应对直接优化像素空间和合成所需的空间细节的技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态模型通常将视觉表示和骨干网络分离开来，以适应视觉理解和生成的不同需求，但这种分离限制了统一性。HiUni 试图通过重新设计预训练的视觉语言模型来实现视觉理解与生成的统一。

Method: HiUni 方法包括重用同一种语言对齐的 Vision Transformer 和密集 Transformer 作为双模态任务的基础，并将任务特定的计算限制在输出阶段。为此，引入了 Pixel Diffusion Head 来优化像素空间和 HiGate 路径以提供合成所需的细粒度空间细节。

Result: 实验结果表明，HiUni 在图像生成和编辑中取得了与预训练视觉语言模型相当的平均理解分数，同时实现了竞争性的性能。

Conclusion: 研究结论是，单一密集骨干网络可以在承担视觉理解和生成任务时不进行特定任务的骨干网络计算，从而简化了统一多模态建模的路径。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models aim to support visual understanding and generation within a single framework. However, prevailing approaches often separate their visual representations or backbone computation to accommodate the different requirements of these two tasks, which limits the degree of unification. In this paper, we present HiUni, which revisits this design choice and explores whether a pretrained vision language model can be directly extended into a unified model with extensive parameter sharing. Specifically, HiUni reuses the same language-aligned ViT and dense Transformer backbone for both capabilities, while confining task-specific computation to the output side. Yet such a design introduces two key challenges. Generation in pixel space is difficult to optimize, and semantic abstraction in pretrained ViT discards fine spatial details required for synthesis. To address these challenges, HiUni introduces a \emph{Pixel Diffusion Head} tailored to direct pixel-space optimization and a lightweight \emph{HiGate} pathway that provides fine-grained spatial details near the output stage. Extensive experiments show that HiUni achieves a similar average understanding score to the pretrained VLM while attaining competitive performance on image generation and editing. Controlled comparisons and analyses further demonstrate that a single dense backbone can effectively support both capabilities without task-specific backbone computation, highlighting a simpler path toward unified multimodal modeling.

</details>


### [127] [Learning Reconstruction Errors as Spatiotemporal Evidence for AI-Generated Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26116)
*Jiahua Jiang,Jinglai Li,Jianru Zhang,Jin Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解决方案，通过学习和利用重建误差作为时空证据的统一体来解决AI视频检测中的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI视频的广泛传播引发了关于虚假信息、欺诈及社会风险的担忧，促使发展有效的检测方法。

Method: 该方法通过联合优化一个重建器和下游分类器来生成更具有检测AI视频信息的重建误差，同时通过三维卷积直接在连续重建误差中捕获时空证据，而不依赖原始视频特征流。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在30多种现有AI视频生成器上均取得了与基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法提供了更有效的AI生成视频检测途径，具有较低的架构复杂度。

Abstract: The proliferation of AI-generated videos has raised growing concerns about misinformation, fraud, and potential societal risks, creating an urgent need for detection. Reconstruction-error-based methods have shown strong empirical performance for AI-generated image detection by exploiting discrepancies between an input and its reconstructed version. Their extension to video detection, however, faces two challenges: (i) a fixed reconstructor is not optimized for the detection objective and may yield weak discriminative signals; and (ii) an additional raw-video feature stream is often introduced to model temporal dependencies, increasing architectural overhead. To address these challenges, we develop a detector that learns and exploits reconstruction errors as a unified source of spatiotemporal evidence. First, we jointly optimize a reconstructor and the downstream classifier to produce reconstruction errors that are more informative for detecting AI-generated videos. Second, we show that consecutive reconstruction errors alone carry temporal evidence, and thus a 3D convolution over them captures spatiotemporal evidence without a raw-video stream. Extensive experiments on over 30 existing AI-video generators demonstrate competitive performance against baselines.

</details>


### [128] [Better Retrieval, Not Better Binding: Pretraining Exposure in Egocentric Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26120)
*Yuqi Guo,Michael Wray*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Ego-ExBind 提供了一种诊断协议，用于分析预训练曝光和动词-名词绑定在自观点视频-文本检索中的作用，通过实验证明了零样本检索与感知到的动词-名词配对曝光之间的正相关关系，并提出通过调整曝光输入可以改善检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的视频-语言模型（VLMs）在零样本检索方面表现出色，但在组合理解方面存在局限性，因此需要一种新的诊断协议来分析和改善这个问题。

Method: Ego-ExBind 通过构建从 EgoClip 预训练语料库推导出的曝光记录账簿，并通过受控的名词置换和动词置换负样本进一步探究名词和动词绑定通道。

Result: 研究发现零样本检索与感知到的动词-名词配对曝光正相关，并且增加配对曝光会导致名词置换差距增大而较小程度上减小动词置换差距。

Conclusion: 通过对曝光输入进行重新加权可以在不改变名词-动词组成部分理解的情况下改善视频-文本检索性能，并且这种改进取决于样本级别的曝光对应关系，Ego-ExBind 提供了一个框架来评估未来自观点 MIR 的改进是否反映在组合理解上还是预训练熟悉度上。

Abstract: Video-language models (VLMs) achieve strong performance on video-text retrieval and are increasingly used for egocentric video understanding. Yet, despite gains in zero-shot retrieval, they have shown limitations in compositional understanding, and it remains unclear whether they understand compositional relationships between verbs and nouns. In this work, we introduce Ego-ExBind, a compositional diagnostic protocol for analyzing pretraining exposure and verb-noun binding in egocentric video-text retrieval. Ego-ExBind consists of an Exposure Ledger built from the EgoClip pretraining corpus used by EgoVLPv2, which defines Seen Composition, Unseen Composition, and Unseen Atom splits for EPIC-KITCHENS-100 multi-instance retrieval (MIR). We further probe noun and verb binding channels through controlled noun-swap and verb-swap negatives. We find that zero-shot retrieval is positively associated with pair exposure within seen compositions, even after controlling for the marginal frequencies of verbs and nouns. However, higher pair exposure is associated with larger noun-swap margins and slightly smaller verb-swap margins. This asymmetry motivates us to test whether exposure reweighting can improve retrieval with little change in the measured swap margins. To isolate the effect of exposure input, we keep the trained checkpoint fixed and replace only the exposure input at inference. We demonstrate that this exposure reweighting can improve video-text retrieval without stronger verb-noun compositional understanding, and that the gain depends on the correct sample-level exposure correspondence. By separating exposure-driven retrieval gains from verb-noun binding, Ego-ExBind provides a diagnostic framework for evaluating whether future improvements in egocentric MIR reflect compositional understanding rather than pretraining familiarity.

</details>


### [129] [CryoGaussian: Heterogeneous Cryo-EM Reconstruction Made Explicit with Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26128)
*Ziwen Wang,Jiajun Fan,Chaoran Cheng,Ruihan Guo,Yanru Qu,Juexiao Zhou,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CryoGaussian 是一种用于不均匀冷冻电镜重建的方法，通过直接对粒子图像进行可微放像素渲染，以高斯密度表示进行拟合，解决了罕见组成状态和细微连续构象变化难以分辨的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的冷冻电镜技术在处理罕见组分状态和细微连续构象变化方面存在局限性，CryoGaussian通过在粒子图像上直接拟合高斯密度来解决这些问题，从而提供更准确的结构重建。

Method: CryoGaussian 方法使用包含可学习的粒子-图像和每个高斯的先验潜变量的放像素渲染进行训练。通过正交线积分和对比转移函数进行可微分渲染。然后根据推断出的潜变量对每个高斯进行拟合。

Result: CryoGaussian 相比其他方法在 CryoBench 数据集上表现出更高的对比度多样性和构象多样性的重建准确性。它能够重建 50S 核糖体亚单位中罕见的组成状态，分辨 rRNA 68 桥未存在于中心突出部位的情况。此外，该方法在加性冷冻电镜投影优化后，训练时间减少了 12.8-28.0 倍。

Conclusion: CryoGaussian 是一种有效的方法，能够提升罕见组成和细微连续构象变化的冷冻电镜重建精度。

Abstract: Single-particle cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) determines macromolecular structures from noisy particle images. However, rare, transient compositional states and subtle, continuous conformational variation are difficult to resolve. We present CryoGaussian, a Gaussian splatting method for heterogeneous cryo-EM reconstruction that fits an explicit Gaussian density representation directly to the particle images through a differentiable rasterizer rendering orthographic line integrals modulated by the contrast transfer function. The Gaussians are initialized randomly, with their number derived from the estimated spatial support of the molecular density and their minimum size bounded by the Nyquist frequency of the particle images. To capture the compositional and conformational variation across particle images, we learn particle-image and per-Gaussian latents that jointly modulate the base position, amplitude, and scale of each Gaussian. Each structural state is then reconstructed by fitting the Gaussians conditioned on its inferred latent. On CryoBench, CryoGaussian achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction accuracy for both compositional and conformational heterogeneity. On experimental data, it resolves a rare compositional state of the 50S ribosomal subunit (class C4, 1.3% of particles) with rRNA helix 68 docked in the absence of the central protuberance, and continuous conformational motion of the SF3b and helicase subcomplexes of the pre-catalytic spliceosome. We further optimize the rasterizer for additive cryo-EM projection, reducing training time by 12.8-28.0$\times$.

</details>


### [130] [When Foundation Models Disagree: Representational Heterogeneity for Calibrated Uncertainty in Whole-Slide Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26159)
*Yekai Shen,Yuqian Wang,Zishun Liao,Rongsheng Wang,Wenting Chen,S. Kevin Zhou,Qingsong Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CALIFUSE 是一种新颖的融合框架，通过结合多种病理基础模型来用于不确定性估计，而不像先前方法在同一特征空间内工作。该框架利用 Jensen-Shannon 散度综合滑块和块级别的证据一致性，以更准确地估计不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的不确定性估计器只在一个特征空间内工作，而忽视了基础模型表示本身带来的不确定性。通过提出 CALIFUSE 框架，作者旨在提供一种更可靠且适应性强的不确定性估计方法。

Method: CALIFUSE 通过融合多个病理基础模型并考虑每个编码器表示的异质性，利用 Jensen-Shannon 散度来衡量滑块水平决策一致性及块级别的证据一致性。方法包括不训练额外的融合模块，而是在基础模型上保持冻结训练任务特定的 nnMIL 分类器。

Result: 在七个全切片病变分类任务中，CALIFUSE 框架在校准、误分类检测和选择性预测方面表现出色，同时保持了与其他基础模型组合相当的竞争均衡准确性。

Conclusion: CALIFUSE 框架为病理学中的不确定性估计提供了一种强有力的方法，能够提高模型的可信度和适用性。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models supply strong representations for whole-slide image classification, yet the confidence attached to their predictions remains hard to trust. Prevailing uncertainty estimators act within a single feature space and thereby overlook the uncertainty that the choice of foundation-model representation itself induces. This work introduces CALIFUSE, a calibration-oriented framework that fuses several pathology foundation models for the specific purpose of uncertainty estimation. Departing from accuracy-driven fusion, CALIFUSE reads representational heterogeneity across encoders as an explicit confidence cue. Slide-level decision consensus is coupled with chunk-level evidence agreement quantified through Jensen-Shannon divergence, so that both prediction stability and fine-grained evidence consistency inform the estimate. Foundation-model encoders stay frozen. A task-specific nnMIL classifier is still trained for each FM; CALIFUSE then estimates confidence at inference without training an extra fusion module. Evaluated on seven WSI classification tasks under several foundation-model combinations, the framework sharpens calibration, misclassification detection, and selective prediction relative to both the strongest single-encoder baseline and naive ensemble averaging, all while preserving competitive balanced accuracy.

</details>


### [131] [MultiBinder: Preventing Multi-Subject Identity Leakage in Personalized Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26164)
*Wei Li,Yiheng Zhang,Zhaofan Qiu,Ting Yao,Xiaoyan Sun,Tao Mei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MultiBinder 提出了一种名为 MultiBinder 的新框架，通过引入两个阶段的条件化过程，在视频扩散模型之前将文本实体短语与视觉模式显式绑定。这有效解决了身份泄露和语义纠缠的问题，提高了多主体身份保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个性化视频生成方法由于缺乏在融合前将文本实体短语与视觉模式显式绑定的步骤，导致了严重的身份泄露和语义纠缠问题，MultiBinder 旨在解决这些问题。

Method: MultiBinder 采用了 '绑-后叙述' 的新范式，通过多阶段条件化处理，在视频扩散模型之前进行文本视觉映射。具体来说，Cross-modal Entity Binding 阶段通过 Q-Former 提取身份中心特征并将其与独特的实体短语紧密对齐，形成去纠缠的 '身份令牌'；Multimodal Large Language Model 在 Narrative-Aware Subject Arrangement 阶段解析文本提示中的动作信息，合成上下文化的主体特征。

Result: 在通用和新编纂的多主体基准测试中，MultiBinder 优于现有方法，显著提高了多主体身份保真度。

Conclusion: MultiBinder 通过引入显式的文本-视觉绑定机制，在保持多主体身份完整性方面展现了显著的优势，为未来的个性化视频生成研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Personalized video generation faces a significant scalability challenge: as the number of custom subjects increases, existing methods suffer from severe identity leakage and semantic entanglement, resulting in videos with blended attributes and incoherent narratives. We identify that this stems from a lack of explicit binding between textual entity phrases and their corresponding visual patterns prior to fusion in diffusion models. To address this, we propose MultiBinder, a novel framework built on a ``bind-then-narrate'' paradigm. Our approach introduces a two-stage conditioning process before video diffusion. First, Cross-modal Entity Binding devises an entity-aware Q-Former to extract identity-centric features from each subject's reference image and tightly align them with its unique entity phrase, yielding disentangled ``identity tokens.'' Second, a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) acts as a ``narrative director'' in the Narrative-Aware Subject Arrangement stage, capturing the action of each subject from the text prompt and synthesizes contextualized subject features that encapsulate ``who is doing what'' and preserve role-specific distinctions. These features are then injected into a video diffusion model via dedicated conditioning pathways, ensuring precise attribute allocation. Extensive experiments on both general and newly curated multi-subject benchmarks show that MultiBinder significantly outperforms existing methods in multi-subject identity fidelity.

</details>


### [132] [StreamEgo: Streaming Exocentric-to-Egocentric Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26165)
*Tianyi Xu,Boyang Lyu,Yunkai Tang,Kairan Ding,Jiahang Li,Weiyu Guo,Yandong Guo,Shuchen Weng,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamEgo 是一种实时框架，可以实现不受限制的 Exo2Ego 视频生成，它通过引入像素对齐的引导分支和区域感知损失课程，解决了复杂的操作动力学问题，并提高了多目标扩散训练的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Exo2Ego 视频生成方法存在操作区域退化、过拟合以及固定的长度限制和高延迟的问题，这些问题限制了 Exo2Ego 视频生成在实时流媒体中的应用。

Method: StreamEgo 通过引入流式双分支先验、区域感知损失课程，并将模型精简为块级自回归模型，结合区域感知的破坏机制和初始块课程，实现高质量、泛化的实时流式 Exo2Ego 生成。

Result: StreamEgo 实现了高帧率（24.3 FPS）的实时流式 Exo2Ego 生成，能够在单块 H100 GPU 上生成高保真、可泛化的视频。

Conclusion: StreamEgo 框架成功解决了实时流媒体中 Exo2Ego 视频生成面临的挑战，为沉浸式 AR/VR 体验和机器人技术提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Exocentric-to-egocentric (Exo2Ego) video generation transforms third-person recordings into first-person viewpoints, unlocking immersive AR/VR experiences and scalable demonstration synthesis for embodied robotics. Extending it to a streaming setting is expected to further enable live applications. However, existing methods suffer from degraded manipulation regions, overfitting and confinement to fixed-length clips with prohibitive latency. To address these limitations, we present StreamEgo, a real-time framework for unbounded, generalizable Exo2Ego video generation. To resolve intricate manipulation dynamics while supporting the streaming pipeline, a streaming dual-branch prior provides pixel-aligned guidance for translating appearance and skeletal kinematics. We also introduce a region-aware loss curriculum to improve generalization and stabilize multi-objective diffusion training. Furthermore, we distill the model into a chunk-wise autoregressive model, augmented with a region-aware corruption mechanism and a first-chunk curriculum, enabling high-quality, generalizable real-time streaming Exo2Ego generation at 24.3 FPS on a single H100 GPU.

</details>


### [133] [SpatialScout-V2: Can VLM Agents Actively Explore Real-World 3D Scenes and Localize Objects?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26177)
*Zhaoxi Zhang,Hao Liang,Linxuan Wu,Yiming Xu,Yifu Yuan,Kun Liang,Chenming Tang,Meiyi Qiang,Wentao Zhang,Weikang Li,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialScout-V2 提高了通过主动探索真实场景来构建准确完整的对象中心图的任务。尽管现有的前沿视觉语言模型表现不佳，但研究显示目标缺失会进一步降低准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着研究者对让智能体理解和导航真实住宅环境的兴趣增加，准确构建和理解这些环境中的物体布局成为关键需求。

Method: SpatialScout-V2 通过引入真实视觉观测、保持场景原始内容和交互式探索录制视图来扩展了 SpatialScout 的主动制图任务。

Result: 评估结果显示，当前的顶尖视觉语言模型在对象中心图的定位准确性和完整性方面表现不佳，甚至在最佳模型中也只有22.52%的对象定位准确率超过0.5。

Conclusion: 这表明在真实场景中通过主动探索创建准确、完整对象中心图的任务具有重大挑战。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence requires agents to acquire visual evidence and integrate partial observations into coherent three-dimensional representations. We introduce SpatialScout-V2, which extends SpatialScout's active mapping task from simulation to real residential scenes through three advances: real visual observations, preservation of original scene content, and interactive exploration of recorded views. Built from 100 homes in Realsee3D, the benchmark covers 3,171 target objects across 29 categories. Given a fixed target inventory, agents autonomously select recorded viewpoints and viewing directions, integrate calibrated RGB observations, and iteratively estimate and revise object centers within a fixed interaction budget. We evaluate the localization accuracy and completeness of object-center maps using mean IoU, IoU-threshold hit rates, and target coverage. Evaluating seven frontier vision-language models (VLMs), we find that six achieve an all-target mean IoU below 20\%. Even the best-performing model, Claude Opus 5.5, reaches only 22.52\%, with just 18.36\% of targets exceeding an IoU of 0.5. Further analysis shows that broad submission coverage can coexist with limited localization accuracy, while target omissions further reduce overall scores. These results highlight substantial challenges in constructing accurate and complete object-center maps through active exploration of real scenes.

</details>


### [134] [FlexVSR: Continuous-Scale Super-Resolution for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26178)
*Ji Wang,Haonan An,Jiasheng Zhang,Renlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexVSR 提出了一种 decoupled 框架，以轻量级的 ReLSA 模块实现可变尺度的生成超分辨率视频流，该模块通过无需更新扩散变压器（DiT）的参数高效适应，仅需 0.274M 可训练参数和 7.3 H100 GPU 小时，即可实现在线生成具有连续尺度的高质量视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实时流媒体生成超分辨率视频时，需要适应不同目标分辨率、显示及计算预算，而现有固定尺度的 VSR 方法难以应对尺度变化。

Method: FlexVSR 通过 ReLSA 模块对固定尺度的潜在空间进行 scale-aware 调整，利用轻量模型和少量可训练参数，在不更新扩散变压器的情况下解决跨尺度问题。

Result: FlexVSR 在实验中显示了竞争优势，包括重建质量和时间质量良好、降低延迟以及有效泛化至未见过的连续尺度，填补了实时流媒体生成超分辨率在连续尺度方面的空白。

Conclusion: FlexVSR 为可变尺度实时生成超分辨率视频提供了一种有效的解决方案，通过轻量参数和高效设计提升了系统灵活性和效率。

Abstract: Real-time streaming video generation has gained rapid momentum in live content creation and interactive media. Deploying such systems in practice requires flexible scaling factors to accommodate diverse source resolutions, target displays, and computational budgets. However, extending fixed-scale generative video super-resolution (VSR) to varying target scales poses two challenges: downsampling from a larger fixed scale incurs redundant cost, while re-optimizing the diffusion transformer (DiT) is costly and alters the shared generative prior. To address both, we propose FlexVSR, a decoupled framework for continuous-scale streaming generative VSR with supervision at only a few scales. Our key insight is that naively resampling fixed-scale latents onto a new target grid induces scale-dependent input mismatch, which can be effectively addressed through parameter-efficient, scale-aware adaptation of the latent interface without updating the DiT. To this end, FlexVSR introduces ReLSA (Refined Latent Scale Adaptation), a lightweight latent adapter that constructs and refines target-grid latents before they enter the DiT, enabling a shared frozen VSR prior to generalize across continuous scales with only 0.274M trainable parameters and an adaptation cost of 7.3 H100 GPU-hours, while introducing no additional temporal dependencies and preserving the native causal streaming protocol. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive or superior reconstruction and temporal quality, lower latency than most native-scale streaming baselines and offline methods, and effective generalization to unseen continuous scales, filling the continuous-scale gap in real-time streaming generative VSR.

</details>


### [135] [RegionAgent: Two-Stage Preference Optimization for Regional Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26179)
*Weiyu Zhou,Jiacong Tang,Tao Hu,Axi Niu,Jinqiu Sun,Peng Wu,Wei Dong,Qingsen Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为RegionAgent的自适应复原框架，使用视觉-语言模型通过两阶段偏好学习生成区域特定的复原动作，显著提升了图像在混合和单一退化下的复原性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法采用全局统一的操作，在处理具有不同退化区域的图像时可能会忽略区域差异，导致复原不完整或引入图像内容损害。此外，为同一退化区域多次复原时，早期操作可能改变残余退化特征或引入伪影，这可能影响后续的复原效果。

Method: 该模型通过两阶段偏好学习训练视觉-语言模型（VLM），首先使用步骤级直接偏好优化在每一步中选择适合当前区域的动作，其次通过轨迹级直接偏好优化考虑当前操作对未来复原的影响。

Result: 实验表明，RegionAgent框架在多数据集上的复原表现强大，特别是在混合和单一退化下。进一步的消融研究也验证了两阶段偏好学习对提高复原质量的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的方法能够有效处理具有不同退化区域的图像，且具有良好的复原性能，具有重要的应用前景。

Abstract: Images under non-uniform coupled degradations require diverse restoration capabilities for different degraded regions. Existing methods employ globally uniform operations, potentially overlooking regional differences and causing incomplete restoration or even damage to image content. Moreover, a degraded region may require successive restoration actions, and earlier actions may alter residual degradation characteristics or introduce artifacts, potentially hindering subsequent restoration. To address these challenges, we propose RegionAgent, an adaptive restoration framework that trains a Vision-Language Model (VLM) through two-stage preference learning to generate a region-specific restoration action at each iteration. Specifically, we first train the VLM to select an action suited to the current region through step-level Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), using preferences derived from immediate restoration feedback to supervise only the differing component in each action pair. To account for the effects of current actions on subsequent restoration, we then apply trajectory-level DPO, using preferences derived from complete trajectory scores to supervise the sole differing action in each trajectory pair. Furthermore, as conventional image-pair datasets do not directly provide preferences over regional restoration decisions, we construct a regional restoration preference dataset comprising 10K action pairs and 20K trajectory pairs for two-stage VLM training. Experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate strong restoration performance under both mixed and single degradations. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of two-stage preference learning in improving restoration quality.

</details>


### [136] [Backbone Matters: Benchmarking Encoder-Decoder Networks for Semantic Segmentation of Driving Scenes on BDD100K](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26191)
*Ehsan Rassekh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究对BDD100K驾驶数据集使用了多种解码器与不同家族的13种主干网络，结果表明FPN与EfficientNet-B4组合能达到最高的平均交并比（mIoU）为62.6%，同时讨论了实验结果和研究范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在通过系统地比较不同主干网络在不同解码器下的表现，选择最适合用于BDD100K驾驶数据集的主干网络和解码器组合，提高自动驾驶车辆对周围环境的理解能力。

Method: 研究通过将四种常用的解码器（U-Net, FPN, LinkNet 和 PSPNet）与六个家族中选出的13种主干网络（ResNet, VGG, DenseNet, Inception, MobileNet 和 EfficientNet）组合，总共训练了52个模型，并采用相同的训练协议进行训练。

Result: 研究结果显示，FPN与EfficientNet-B4的组合在BDD100K驱动数据集上的平均交并比（mIoU）达到了最高的62.6%，此外还达到了89.8%的准确率，训练损失为0.092。只有两种模型超过了BDD100K基准报告的56.9%的mIoU，这两者都是FPN与EfficientNet的组合。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为FPN与EfficientNet-B4的组合是目前在BDD100K驾驶数据集中表现最佳的模型，而在不同的解码器下，主干网络的效果有很大差异，对于某些特定的解码器，相同的主干网络表现非常不同。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation gives an autonomous vehicle a dense, pixel-level description of its surroundings and is a core part of scene understanding. Most segmentation networks consist of an encoder (the backbone) and a decoder, yet the backbone is often chosen without a systematic comparison. This work studies this choice on the BDD100K driving dataset. Four widely used decoders (U-Net, FPN, LinkNet and PSPNet) are combined with 13 backbones from six families (ResNet, VGG, DenseNet, Inception, MobileNet and EfficientNet), and all 52 models are trained with one common protocol. The best model, an FPN decoder with an EfficientNet-B4 backbone, reaches 62.6% mean intersection over union (mIoU), 89.8% accuracy and a loss of 0.092. Only two of the 52 models, both FPN with EfficientNet, exceed the 56.9% mIoU reported with the BDD100K benchmark. The effect of the backbone depends strongly on the decoder: the same EfficientNet-B4 gives 42.6% mIoU with PSPNet but 62.6% with FPN, and the gap between the best and the worst backbone ranges from 12.0 points (U-Net) to 42.5 points (FPN). The results and the scope of this study are discussed.

</details>


### [137] [A version of the Cloude/van Zyl decomposition for full/quad pol synthetic aperture radar data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26195)
*Allan Aasbjerg Nielsen,Paul Jacques Connetable,Henning Skriver,Knut Conradsen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Based on the Cloude/van Zyl decomposition of reflection symmetric polarimetric SAR data we present a version for full/quad polarisation data. We demonstrate both versions on X-band F-SAR data over Vejers, Denmark. Differences between the original and our version of the decomposition occur especially for very bright man-made targets. These differences come with lower entropy for our version giving an improved focus on a more pronounced dominant scattering mechanism for these objects. Interpretation of the scattering mechanism related to the full/quad pol version of the decomposition is facilitated by means of the Cloude/Pottier $\barα$ angle.

</details>


### [138] [SpecWM: Turning Verification into Refinement for Streaming World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26211)
*Jiaming Li,Lingzhen Li,Xiaze Yang,Chenyu Zhu,Kaiqi Liu,Xiaotong Zhao,Songlian Li,Toyota Li,Alan Zhao,Haobin Zhong,Yue Ma,Zhiyuan Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SpecWM的统一推理框架，能够利用目标的潜在速度场对草案部分进行验证，并提供可重复使用的校正信息，从而在提高视觉质量的同时加速生成过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的生成方法在保证生成质量的同时，可能引起验证过程的延迟，需要多次重生成草案，推高了成本。因此，本文旨在通过一种训练无依赖的在线推理框架SpecWM来优化生成过程。

Method: SpecWM框架利用目标的潜在速度场进行验证，并通过两个阈值决定草案的接受、细化或拒绝。细化分为一步细化和三步细化，一步细化用来改善感知质量，三步细化则增强摄像头跟随和轨迹一致性。

Result: 在WBench Navigation中，SpecWM实现了一倍多的加速，同时提高了视觉质量，并将NavScore从草案的75.99提升到了85.63，展现了最佳的质量、速度和控制权之间的权衡。

Conclusion: SpecWM为世界模型的交互生成提供了一种新的在线推理方法，通过验证草案和利用潜在速度场，实现了速度和质量之间的更好平衡，为后续研究提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Recently, video world models have advanced interactive generation, with large models offering higher quality and smaller models enabling faster inference. Existing speculative methods combine their strengths through fast draft generation and selective target regeneration guided by rewards on decoded frames. However, verification covers only the external scorer's evaluation dimensions and adds decoding and scoring costs. Moreover, these scores provide no correction, requiring costly target regeneration for rejected drafts. To address these limitations, we propose Speculative World Model (SpecWM), a unified, training-free inference framework for streaming world models. It verifies draft chunks using the target's latent velocity field. Verification also yields a reusable correction; two thresholds select Accept, Refine, or Reject. Specifically, one-step refinement reuses it to improve perceptual quality, while three-step refinement strengthens camera following and trajectory consistency. These findings motivate transition-aware refinement. On WBench Navigation, SpecWM improves visual quality at 1.63$\times$ speedup over the target and raises NavScore from the draft's 75.99 to 85.63, achieving the best quality-speed-control trade-off. Additionally, our LongSpecBench shows sustained quality and control over long, multi-turn rollouts, while experiments on Hy-Nomas validate SpecWM across backbones and denoising budgets.

</details>


### [139] [MixReact: Physics-Based Reactive Humanoid Control from Multimodal Cues via a Discrete-Continuous Mixture of Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26218)
*Bin Li,Xing Zhu,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixReact 提出了一种结合文本、音频和视频线索的统一框架，用于基于物理的人形反应控制，通过 DisCo-MoT 进行状态预测，并由 HAT 转换成可执行动作，从而改善了反应的准确性、多样性和物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法只能处理有限的线索模态且仅能实现基于运动学的生成，这使得反应容易出现物理上的错误。因此，需要一种能够从文本、音频和视频中生成具有物理一致性的反应的新方法。

Method: 该方法采用混合的离散-连续Transformer (DisCo-MoT) 预测状态，以及基于历史的动作Transformer (HAT) 转换预测的动作，用于生成物理上可行的反应。

Result: 实验表明，MixReact 在所有模态下都优于基线，在保真度、多样性和物理一致性方面均有改进。消融实验表明各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: MixReact 是一个集成多模态输入的物理人形反应控制框架，能够生成更准确、多样且符合物理特性的反应。

Abstract: Humanoid agents in everyday environments are expected to react to diverse cues as humans do, with semantically appropriate and physically executable behaviors. Existing cue-driven reaction generation methods support only a narrow range of cue modalities and are limited to purely kinematic generation, leaving the resulting reactions susceptible to physical artifacts. To this end, we present MixReact, the first unified framework for physics-based reactive humanoid control from text, audio, and video cues, integrating a Discrete--Continuous Mixture of Transformers (DisCo-MoT) for state prediction with a History-aware Action Transformer (HAT) for physical execution. DisCo-MoT couples a discrete masked-modeling branch with a continuous autoregressive diffusion branch via one-way mixture attention within a MoT architecture. Guidance from discrete context mitigates collapse into averaged behaviors in one-to-many reaction generation, while direct prediction in continuous state space bypasses the decoding bottleneck of discrete representations. HAT then maps the predicted next state and observed execution history to executable actions, enabling robust closed-loop control in physics-based simulation. Experiments across all three modalities demonstrate consistent improvements over the baselines in fidelity, diversity, and physical consistency, while ablations validate the complementary contributions of the components. Our code will be released to facilitate future research. Project page: https://binlee26.github.io/MixReact_Page/

</details>


### [140] [Attention Drift Regularization with Adaptive Initialization for Domain-Robust Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26234)
*Shivangi Rai,Vinod K Kurmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为DGA-CAFC的方法来解决源无监督域适应中两个关键问题：注意力漂移和领域间隙无关的初始化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的源无监督域适应方法未能有效解决注意力漂移和领域间隙处理不当的问题，导致模型鲁棒性差，泛化能力受限。对此，该论文提出了DGA-CAFC方法以提升模型的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Method: DGA-CAFC包含两个主要模块：1) Counterfactual Attention-based Feature Consistency (CAFC)模块，通过最小化不同注意力机制下特征表示的差异，确保特征表示的稳定性和领域鲁棒性；2) Domain-Gap Aware Source Model Selection (DGASS)模块，通过估计目标域预测熵并选择最适合当前域差距的源模型变体，优化模型初始化。

Result: 实验表明DGA-CAFC在多个源无监督域适应和领域泛化基准上相较于现有基线方法表现出更优的效果。

Conclusion: DGA-CAFC为解决源无监督域适应中的关键问题提供了一种有效的方法，并验证了其在多种场景下的适用性和有效性。

Abstract: Source-free domain adaptation (SFDA) adapts a pretrained source model to unlabeled target domains without source data access, yet two critical failure modes remain unaddressed. First, attention drift: source-learned attention maps shift toward spurious or background features under distribution shift, destabilizing feature representations. Second, domain-gap-agnostic initialization: applying a single fixed source model uniformly across all target domains ignores domain gap severity, causing premature overfitting to noisy pseudo-labels under large shifts. We propose DGA-CAFC to address both failures. The Counterfactual Attention-based Feature Consistency (CAFC) module enforces feature stability by minimizing the discrepancy between representations computed under learned attention and those under counterfactual-inspired normalized perturbations, thereby promoting domain-robust features independent of spurious attention shifts. The Domain-Gap Aware Source Model Selection (DGASS) module estimates domain gap severity via target prediction entropy and selects the source model variant whose confidence level best matches the estimated gap, reducing negative transfer and stabilizing pseudo-label quality. Crucially, CAFC transfers beyond SFDA: applied as a lightweight regularizer to standard domain generalization objectives without target data. Extensive experiments across SFDA and DG benchmarks demonstrate that our method consistently improves over recent baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness and generality of the proposed framework. Project page is available at https://visdomlab.github.io/DGA_CAFC

</details>


### [141] [Beyond Covers and Titles: Evidence-First Ranking for Robust Cold-Start Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26249)
*Fuyi Wang,Yuxin Cao,Yifan Liao,Renyang Liu,Lin Li,Wei Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了REAP攻击和Evidence-First Ranking (EFR)算法，通过模拟平台编解码过程，发现视频内容本身比封装编辑更能影响冷启动排名，进而提出了一种能抵御包装编辑的冷启动排序方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的内容感知推荐系统受到攻击时，攻击者通过修改像素或特征来提升视频的推荐排名，而现有防御措施只能抵御规范范围内的扰动。这种攻击方法和防御措施都存在局限性，因此研究者开发了REAP攻击和EFR算法来提升冷启动视频的推荐效果。

Method: 本文通过H.264编码、分辨率转换、裁剪和JPEG压缩来模拟平台重新编码过程。借助REAP攻击，研究者发现即使没有封装编辑，仅根据视频主体也能实现高冷启动视频推荐效果。基于上述发现，研究者提出了Evidence-First Ranking (EFR)算法，采用支持门控残差来限制包装影响，并通过合并冷启动和温启动排名的比例标准，来提供针对任何包装编辑的精确认证。

Result: 通过实验验证，EFR算法不仅有效防御了包装编辑攻击，还提高了冷启动视频的推荐效果。在MicroLens-100K数据集上，EFR将攻击带来的收益降低了81%，并将冷启动召回@100从0.1133提高到了0.170，在同时保持合并NDCG@10与未防护系统差距在4%以内。

Conclusion: 研究者展示了包装编辑对冷启动推荐的潜在危害，并提出了EFR算法来对抗这种攻击，提升了冷启动视频的推荐质量和公正性。

Abstract: Short-video platforms rank new uploads before anyone has watched them, using content towers that read a video's cover, title and frames. Existing attacks on content-aware recommenders perturb pixels or features and ignore platform re-encoding, and existing defenses train against norm-bounded perturbations that neither match real edits nor preserve recommendation quality. We show with REAP, a realizable attack through {a simulated platform channel (H.264 transcoding, rescaling, cropping and JPEG)}, that packaging edits raise a cold video by 32.0 rank-percentile points in unrelated audiences. We then observe that cold-start ranking does not need the packaging at all: a tower that sees only the video body ranks cold items best. Building on this, we propose Evidence-First Ranking (EFR), which scores cold videos from their body, admits packaging only through a support-gated residual bounded by $γ$, and merges cold and warm rankings by rank percentile, yielding an exact certificate against any packaging edit. On MicroLens-100K, EFR cuts the attack gain by 81% and raises cold-start Recall@100 from 0.1133 to 0.170, while keeping the merged NDCG@10 within 4% of the undefended system.

</details>


### [142] [Prism: Hierarchical Text Detection with Minimalist Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26254)
*Yuchen Su,Xuesheng Wang,Zhilong Ji,Jinfeng Bai,Shijian Lu,Zhineng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为Prism的简约框架，通过使用预训练的单尺度主干特征图并附加轻量级头部进行多粒度检测，以及通过高效的几何包含算法重建结构层次。实验结果表明，该模型在多个公开基准上的性能达到了SOTA水平且具有较高的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过复杂的多尺度特征融合颈部和重型头部来提高准确性，但会导致模型结构复杂且推理效率低。因此，论文旨在寻找一种更简约的方法，依赖于预训练的主干网络来替换这些复杂模块，同时使用简单的几何包含方法进行层次结构解析。

Method: Prism框架通过单尺度主干特征图附加并行轻量级头部，进行多粒度检测，并通过高效的几何包含算法重建结构层次。此外，论文系统地比较了代表性的轻量级头部架构，并表明像素级回归在这个设置中具有最佳的准确率-效率权衡。

Result: 在多个公共基准上的实验结果显示，简化后的架构达到了SOTA性能，并且效率显著提升。与LRANet相比，Prism在CTW1500上快了$1.7	imes$，且准确度更高；在HierText数据集上，Prism比当前SOTA方法OmniParser V2高出了超过6点的PQ得分。

Conclusion: Prism框架通过简化模型结构提高了检测效率，并验证了轻量级头部的潜力。该方法为视觉文本理解中的层级结构解析提供了一种新的视角。

Abstract: Hierarchical text detection aims to localize words, text lines, and paragraphs while parsing their structural relationships. It provides essential structural cues for visual text understanding. To maximize accuracy, existing methods typically rely on complex multi-scale feature fusion necks and heavy-weight heads, resulting in bloated architectures and low inference efficiency. In this paper, we argue that a sufficiently pre-trained backbone can effectively replace these sophisticated external modules, while simple geometric inclusion suffices for hierarchical text structural parsing. Thus, we propose Prism, a minimalist framework for hierarchical text detection. Prism performs multi-granularity detection by attaching parallel lightweight heads to a single-scale backbone feature map, covering words, text lines, and paragraphs, and reconstructs the structural hierarchy via an efficient geometric inclusion algorithm. In addition, we systematically compare representative lightweight head archetypes and show that pixel-level regression offers the best accuracy-efficiency trade-off in this setting. Experiments on multiple public benchmarks show that our significantly simplified architecture achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance with remarkable efficiency. Specifically, it runs $1.7\times$ faster than LRANet on CTW1500 while achieving higher accuracy, and surpasses the current SOTA method, OmniParser V2, by over 6 PQ points on the hierarchical HierText dataset. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [143] [Digitization of Analogue Gauges using Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26262)
*Erico Kayumba Mutonkole,Olasupo Ajayi,Omowunmi Isafiade*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究开发并评估了一种使用计算机视觉和深度学习技术的自动化表盘读取系统。通过不同方法对比实验，研究发现基于ResNet18的深度学习方法表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于制造行业中广泛使用模拟表盘进行物理量测量，但人工监控带来的操作效率低下和人为错误问题，该研究旨在设计并测试一种基于计算机视觉和深度学习的自动化表盘读取系统，以提高效率并减少人为错误。

Method: 研究中建立了涵盖1848张压力表图像的数据集，通过网络爬取、合成生成和图像增强技术进行构建。分别使用三种混合方法进行实验，包括传统计算机视觉方法及结合ResNet18和VGG19的深度学习方法。

Result: 实验结果显示，基于ResNet18的深度学习方法在准确度方面表现最佳，达到97.3%，而VGG19和Scikit-Image则分别仅为64.4%和63.8%。

Conclusion: 研究证实了残差学习架构在工业表盘数字化中的有效性，为制造业特别是工业5.0自动化应用提供了坚实的基础。

Abstract: Analogue gauges remain prevalent in manufacturing industries for measuring physical quantities, yet manual monitoring by operators introduces human error and operational inefficiency. This research developed and evaluated an automated gauge reading system using computer vision and deep learning techniques. Three hybrid approaches were implemented and compared: OpenCV with Scikit-Image (traditional computer vision), OpenCV with ResNet18, and OpenCV with VGG19 (deep learning approaches). A comprehensive dataset of 1,848 pressure gauge images (0-10 BAR range) was curated through web scraping, synthetic generation, and augmentation techniques. The experimental results demonstrate that ResNet18 significantly outperforms the alternatives, achieving an accuracy of 97.3%, compared to VGG19's 64.4% and Scikit-Image's 63.8%. The study validates the effectiveness of residual learning architectures for industrial gauge digitization, providing a robust foundation for Industry 5.0 automation applications.

</details>


### [144] [Tracking Objects Through Assembly and Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26269)
*Cheryl Lam,Yihong Sun,Bharath Hariharan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需提示的物体跟踪方法Atlas，可同时检测物体的分解和组装事件，而无需初始物体提示。与现有方法相比，Atlas在检测状态变化方面性能更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的物体跟踪方法需要初始物体提示，只能检测物体的分解，而不能检测由多个物体组成的组装事件。因此，本文提出了一个无需提示的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 本文引入了Atlas系统，它基于时空分区和反向时空分区来联合推断物体轨迹及其变化。通过建立正向和反向时空分区，Atlas能够同时检测物体的分解和组装事件。

Result: 在OmniST基准测试中，Atlas检测到的状态变化数量是之前方法的两倍以上，同时在现有仅评估分解的基准测试中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法为物体状态变化的检测提供了新的视角，不仅能够准确地检测分解，还能有效检测组装事件。

Abstract: Tracking objects through transformations is essential for understanding real-world dynamics. Recent work has extended traditional object tracking to detect state changes. However, existing methods require an initial object prompt and detect only its decompositions, missing assembly events, which begin with multiple objects rather than one. We therefore introduce a prompt-free formulation of this task, where the goal is to recover all object tracks and detect both decomposition and assembly events given only a video. Our key insight is that assembly is decomposition in reverse. Building on this insight, we present Atlas, a zero-shot system that constructs and jointly reasons over forward and reverse spatiotemporal partitions to recover object tracks and their transformations. We further introduce OmniST, a benchmark with dense annotations of objects and both transformation types. On OmniST, Atlas recovers more than twice as many state changes as prior methods while remaining competitive on existing benchmarks that only evaluate decomposition.

</details>


### [145] [C-LiTE: A Lightweight Foundation Model for Cellular feature learning in Histopathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26316)
*Esha Sadia Nasir,Jiaqi Lv,Gozde N. Gunesli,Shan E Ahmed Raza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: C-LiTE是一种轻量级的模型，用于HE染色组织病理学中的细胞表征学习，它通过减少参数量和改进采样策略来克服传统方法的局限性，在多个细胞级任务中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大部分病理模型对细胞级特征的捕捉不直接，而现有的细胞级模型不仅限制于特定细胞群体，也依赖于复杂的计算架构。C-LiTE旨在解决这些局限，提供一种灵活且高效的细胞级表征学习方法。

Method: C-LiTE采用了自细胞中心处切片并直接使用4x4维度的切片嵌入策略，无需进行图像上采样。此外，C-LiTE利用预训练的ViT-5-S中的切片嵌入权重，通过伪逆变换进行微调，使其在更细的切片分辨率上初始化权重。

Result: C-LiTE在多种公开数据集上的表现接近于性能较高的模型CytoFormer，但参数量仅为后者的1/30，证明其在细胞表征学习方面的高效性。

Conclusion: C-LiTE为病理学中的细胞表征学习提供了一个新的解决方案，其灵活性和高效性为相关研究提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Characterising individual cells is fundamental to computational pathology, as cellular morphology informs tasks such as tumour grading, mitotic assessment and immune profiling. However, most pathology foundation models are trained on tissue patches and capture cellular features only implicitly, while existing cell-level models are either restricted to specific cell populations or rely on computationally expensive architectures with hundreds of millions of parameters. Applying ViTs directly to small cellular crops also presents an architectural challenge: upsampling increases computational cost without adding morphological information and may introduce interpolation artefacts. To address these limitations, we introduce C-LiTE, a lightweight foundation model for cellular representation learning in H&E-stained histopathology, with only 22 million parameters. C-LiTE employs a native-resolution tokenisation strategy tailored to cellular inputs, processing 64 x 64 px cell-centred crops using a 4 x 4 patch embedding to produce a 16 x 16 token grid without image upsampling. To transfer visual knowledge from large-scale pre-training, we adapt the patch embedding weights of a pre-trained ViT-5-S using a pseudoinverse-based transformation, enabling weight initialisation at the finer patch resolution. We further assemble a pre-training dataset of 60 million cell-centred crops from TCGA, balanced across 19 cancer types and 16 tissue types. To our knowledge, this represents the largest public dataset for self-supervised pre-training on H&E images at the cellular level. We evaluate C-LiTE on seven public datasets spanning mitotic figure recognition, nucleus and cell-type classification, and immune cell phenotyping. Under frozen-feature evaluation, C-LiTE achieves a mean macro-F1 of 0.670, approaching CytoFormer (0.682) with approximately 30x fewer parameters.

</details>
