At the global level, we analyse dependency-relation centroids, control for lexical identity through within-lemma centring, and examine their alignment with part-of-speech (POS) distributions. At the local level, we evaluate cross-relation geometric consistency, cross-method agreement, lexical similarity, and polysemous usages. Results show that global geometry is structured strongly by context POS across all representations. Locally, broader lexical similarity structures are partially preserved across dependency relations, while immediate neighbourhoods vary substantially, highlighting different usages. PPMI-based representations exhibit greater cross-relation consistency than SGNS-based representations, with cross-method agreement varying by relation. These findings reveal systematic linguistic organisation at multiple geometric scales and its sensitivity to representation choices.

</details>


### [219] [Many Solutions, One Answer: Search Order Shapes the Default Solutions of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25916)
*Qinhan Hou,Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，语言模型在解决有多解的谜题时，倾向于选择默认解决方案，即使有其他解法。通过改变搜索顺序和随机化呈现，可以增加模型提供的解法多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨语言模型在处理有多项正确答案的谜题时的行为模式，以及如何通过不同的策略提高模型提供的答案多样性。

Method: 实验设置了具有已知答案的符号谜题和ZebraLogic变体，测试了四个未微调的语言模型，并通过改变搜索顺序和随机化处理评估了它们的表现。

Result: 语言模型倾向于集中在某个默认解法上，即使存在其他未采样的解。更改搜索顺序或随机化处理可以显著增加解法的多样性。

Conclusion: 通过实验发现，当提供多个线索独占地识别某个解决方案时，模型会优先使用这些解。而在任务和ZebraLogic中，随机化呈现或搜索顺序可以大幅提升模型提供的答案多样性，而最小的验证损失大约在3%-4%之间。

Abstract: When a constraint puzzle has several correct answers, language models tend to give the same one.We study four open-weight models from three families on symbolic puzzles and multi-solution ZebraLogic variants with exactly known solution sets.Repeated samples concentrate on one default solution far beyond uniform sampling, and different models often share it.Yet models usually produce even rarely sampled solutions when added clues uniquely identify them.On held-out symbolic puzzles, we compare visiting entities in name order with visiting those with the fewest initial candidates first. Both searches try values in the stated order. Where their pre-specified predictions differ, the latter matches a larger share of responses in all four models, by 26 to 40 percentage points.Reversing the stated value order or specifying a search order shifts responses towards the solution that the new order favours, on both testbeds. Randomising the presentation or supplying a random search order raises breadth (the effective share of solutions in use) by 26 to 42 percentage points on the symbolic task and 11 to 26 on ZebraLogic. The largest observed validity losses are 3 and 4 percentage points, respectively. Randomised presentation broadens answers more than a random-choice instruction in each of the three main models.

</details>


### [220] [Web search in Latin America: language models use less local and diverse news than search engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25955)
*Francisco W. Kerche,Franziska Sofia Hafner,Felix M. Simon,Luc Rocher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，基于LLM的搜索引擎返回的信息比传统搜索引擎更加集中，对于当地用户而言，本地新闻的占比更低，这可能会重现地理层级，在全球南方地区的新闻覆盖和可见性上进一步固化不平等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估AI平台对新闻和当前事件的影响，特别是针对拉丁美洲地区的搜索结果差异，揭示基于LLM的搜索可能加剧的地理偏见。

Method: 研究人员构建了一个涵盖九个拉丁美洲国家的1,901个当前事件的数据集，通过四种语言模型和三种搜索引擎针对四种不同用户类型（当地居民、海外居民、游客和外国用户）执行查询，分析结果以评估搜索差异。

Result: 结果显示，语言模型返回的新闻来源比搜索引擎少，不同用户类型中，语言模型返回的本地新闻分享也存在不一致性。对于当地用户，即使搜索引擎已返回仅为41.3%的本地新闻，语言模型还会进一步降低5.4-12.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究结论指出，基于LLM的搜索可能强化现有地理层级，进一步减少全球南方地区（尤其是拉丁美洲）新闻的覆盖和可见度，呼吁设计更加公平的信息系统来应对这一问题。

Abstract: AI platforms are becoming a major gateway to news and current events. This shift risks amplifying existing geographical biases that favour narratives from wealthier countries over those from the Global South. This is particularly relevant in Latin America, which accounts for the largest share of AI usage in the Global South. We examine how information about Latin America changes when users shift from conventional search engines to LLM-based search. We construct a dataset of 1,901 current events across nine Latin American countries, craft realistic queries, and submit them to three search engines and four language models. We vary query language and user location to represent local, diaspora, tourist, and foreign user scenarios. Across 49,207 requests and 8,834 unique domains, language models return a less diverse set of news sources than search engines. Across user types, the share of domestic news returned by language models is inconsistent. For local users, while search engines already return only 41.3\% local news, language models return a further 3.6 to 11.6 percentage points less. Taken together, our findings suggest that LLM-based search can reproduce and, in some settings, amplify geographical hierarchies in the reach and visibility of news sources. We release an open dataset to support multilingual and geographically diverse evaluation of LLM-based search, and highlight implications for more equitable information systems design.

</details>


### [221] [The Depth of a Prediction: Where Language Models Resolve a Word Predicts Human Reading Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25970)
*Donato Riccio,Fabrizio Maturo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，语言模型的最终层预测的惊喜程度可以预测人们读单词所需的时间。层间惊喜轨迹的分辨率深度能够优于单层预测，特别是在GPT-2和Pythia模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型各层预测在阅读时长上的贡献，以更好地理解模型计算及人类阅读行为之间的联系。

Method: 通过分析大量英语眼动追踪和自我节奏阅读语料库，使用GPT-2、OPT和Pythia模型研究层间惊喜轨迹的分辨率深度。

Result: 分辨率深度相比单层预测提供了更好的性能；在八个英语眼动追踪和自我节奏阅读语料库及十个模型家族中，这一效果显著。

Conclusion: 分辨率深度能够在预测阅读行为方面超越或接近单层预测，且不同模型的表现有所差异；使用层间随机效应，分辨率深度依然表现良好。

Abstract: Surprisal from a language model's final layer predicts how long people read a word, yet the model computes its prediction across many layers. We read each word's prediction at every layer with the tuned lens and summarize this layerwise surprisal trajectory by its resolution depth, the depth around which its change is centered. On eight English eye-tracking and self-paced reading corpora (81,957 words) and ten GPT-2, OPT and Pythia models, words that a model resolves later, and the words after them, are read more slowly than equally surprising words. Resolution depth improves held-out prediction beyond final-layer surprisal, entropy and the best single layer by 0.26 millinats per word (pooled p = 0.001), about a third of entropy's gain, and each model family gains, GPT-2 and Pythia through one corpus each. The net gain varies between corpora (random-effects mean 0.07, p = 0.37); against layer-shuffled depth, which adds as many coefficients, it is positive in seven of eight corpora (random-effects mean 0.23, p = 0.016), and untrained networks show no gain under the logit lens. Pooled over corpora, the effect holds with corrected word probabilities and beyond first-to-last-layer divergences, and a whole-trajectory functional regression adds no significant information beyond resolution depth.

</details>


### [222] [Energy estimates of open-weight large language models across stated food weights: a dose-response evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25976)
*Donato Riccio,Fabrizio Maturo,Iulia Brezeanu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究测试了开放大型语言模型（LLMs）的能量估计是否与所声明的食物重量成比例，并探讨了使用能量密度乘以重量是否能减少误差。研究发现，所有七款模型都存在压缩现象，误差范围较广。通过提供密度可以在一定程度上恢复比例估计，减少极端情况下的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机是评估开放大型语言模型在能量估算方面的准确性，特别是在不同重量下的表现，以期提高这些模型在实际应用中的可靠性。

Method: 在设定的协议下，七个开放大型语言模型对从美国农业部食物与营养数据库中随机抽取的168种食物在不同重量下的能量进行了估计。研究采用了一步步的方法，首先计算每个模型声称的能量密度，然后乘以给定的重量。

Result: 研究表明，所有七款模型都存在压缩现象，弹性从0.548到0.979不等，大部分模型的弹性低于1。四款模型压缩严重，其误差范围较大，但在提供密度后，有三款模型的估计接近比例，另外误差在极限情况下的校正因子也有所下降。然而，在普通问题下，压缩现象依然存在，甚至在某些模型中加剧。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，开放大型语言模型的能量估算存在误差问题，特别是在不同重量的数据点上。研究建议，在食物记录工具中，应利用能量密度乘以重量的方法计算食物能量，并在评估中使用固定食物测试不同重量下的模型表现。

Abstract: We tested whether energy estimates of open-weight large language models (LLMs) scale proportionally with an exactly stated food weight, and whether computing energy density times weight removes the error. Under a prespecified protocol, seven open-weight LLMs estimated the energy of 168 randomly drawn foods from the USDA Food and Nutrient Database for Dietary Studies at nine stated weights, from 0.25 to 4 times the default amount. The primary outcome was the elasticity of the estimate with respect to weight (1: proportional scaling). A two-step estimate multiplied each model's own stated energy density by the weight in code. All seven models compressed their estimates, with elasticities from 0.548 to 0.979 (every 95% CI below 1); four compressed severely (0.548 to 0.709). These four overestimated a quarter of the default amount by 47% to 130% and underestimated four times it by 20% to 34%. Supplying the density restored near-proportional scaling in three of the four, and the two-step estimate cut the typical error factor at these extremes from 1.47-2.43 to 1.20-1.38. Compression persisted at full precision and grew in five of seven models under a plain question. The models hold the energy densities needed for proportional scaling and answer near a typical serving, reproducing the flat-slope syndrome of dietary recall. Accuracy at usual portions does not reveal it. Food-logging tools should compute the energy of weighed entries as density times weight, and evaluations should test fixed foods across stated weights.

</details>


### [223] [Xing-Audio-NVVASR: Scaling and Refining Supervision for Joint Recognition of Speech and Non-Verbal Vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26000)
*Pengyu Xu,Gang Liu,Genliang Zhao,Hongjie Chen,Jian Kang,Jie Lian,Jie Li,Yongxiang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究引入了一种名为Xing-Audio-NVVASR的端到端识别器，通过逐步数据挖掘和迭代监督改进，处理稀有的事件丰富训练数据和不准确的事件标签问题。该模型通过两个异构模型共同审核注释，使用呼吸检测器补充遗漏，最终生成了约800,000句的注释数据集。然后训练了一个1.7亿参数的模型，可以在六个非言语 Vocalizations 类别中同时转录语言和非言语内容。结果显示，Xing-Audio-NVVASR在四个基准测试中均取得了领先的成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语音识别技术受限于稀有的事件丰富的训练数据和不准确或缺失的事件标签，这对联合识别语音和非语言声音（NVVs）提出了挑战。为了克服这些问题，研究人员提出了Xing-Audio-NVVASR以改善现有系统的性能。

Method: 研究人员采用逐步数据挖掘和迭代监督改进的方法构建了Xing-Audio-NVVASR。首先，通过两个异构模型共同审核注释并标记分歧，需要重新注释的部分将由老师重新注释。然后使用呼吸检测器查看整个语料库，并由人工确认的标签累积并在后续迭代中保留。这样，逐步生成了一个包含约80万个标注的语料库。接着，使用这一大量标注数据训练了一个参数量为1.7亿的模型，可以在六个NVV类别中同时进行识别。

Result: 最终训练的Xing-Audio-NVVASR模型在四个基准测试中都取得了领先成绩，尤其是在复合评分和六类宏F1值中表现尤为优异。与最好的基线模型相比，其综合得分高出6.03分，呼吸类别F1值高出14.40到31.57个百分点。

Conclusion: Xing-Audio-NVVASR代表了一种创新的方法，能够有效解决稀有训练数据和事件标签不准确的问题，显著提高了联合语音和非语言声音识别的性能。

Abstract: Joint recognition of speech and non-verbal vocalizations (NVVs) is hindered by scarce event-rich training data and inaccurate or missing event labels. We present Xing-Audio-NVVASR, an end-to-end recognizer trained through progressive data mining and iterative supervision refinement. Guided by unified acoustic criteria, two heterogeneous models cross-audit annotations, flagging disagreements for teacher reannotation. To uncover shared omissions that model agreement may conceal, complementary breath detectors screen the entire corpus for human review. Human-confirmed labels accumulate across rounds and are preserved during subsequent refinement. Over five rounds, we refine annotations for 170,000 utterances from public corpora and incorporate 630,000 mined utterances. The resulting corpus of approximately 800,000 utterances is used to train a single 1.7B-parameter model that transcribes speech with inline tags for six NVV classes. Across four human-annotated benchmarks, Xing-Audio-NVVASR ranks first among nine models in both composite score and six-class Macro-F1. It achieves a mean composite score of 64.46, surpassing the strongest comparison model, StepAudio 3 Chat Preview, by 6.03 points, and exceeds the annotation teacher's breathing F1 by 14.40 to 31.57 percentage points. We plan to release the model weights, inference code, four evaluation benchmarks, annotation guidelines, and evaluation code.

</details>


### [224] [Evaluating safeguarding in multi-turn conversations with AI assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26009)
*Charlotte Peart,Franziska Sofia Hafner,Luc Rocher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 这项研究通过与精神卫生支持工作者合作，评估了AI助手在用户逐渐表达抑郁、精神病或家庭暴力的信号时提供保障的能力。尽管在集中表达信号时表现较好，但在分散表达时表现较差，这表明安全评估应考虑不那么显性的信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的对话AI助手被用于提供个人和情感支持，虽然开发者报告说新模型在应对敏感对话方面有所改进，但在现实世界中长时间互动的安全性仍然受到质疑。特别是在应对逐渐表达的焦虑和困境时，现有的评估方法往往基于单一明确的信息，而忽视了逐渐显现和非直接表现的症状。

Method: 研究人员与三家社区心理健康支持团队合作，设计了九个场景，其中包含与抑郁、精神病或家庭暴力相关的线索。每个场景由支持工作者制定黄金标准响应。然后，呈现五条线索，既可以集中在一个消息中，也可以分散在对话中。

Result: 在超过7,400次对话中，六种广泛使用的模型（GPT-5.5, Grok 4.3, DeepSeek V4, 和 Llama 4 Maverick，在集中表达信号时的表现介于26-59%，分散表达时为3-6%。Claude Sonnet 5 和 Claude Opus 5 表现较好，在集中表达信号比例为68-97%，分散为59-87%。表现最好的Claude Opus 5，即使在分散表达时，仍有13%的对话未能达到黄金标准。

Conclusion: 现有的安全评估过于依赖显性的、直接的问题，而忽视了逐渐显现或通过日常对话间接表达的忧虑和困境。为了确保改进的安全性受到重视，开发者必须围绕真实且多样化的沟通方式设计并评估保护措施。

Abstract: Conversational AI assistants are now used for personal and emotional support. Developers report improvements in how new models respond to sensitive conversations, yet users and civil society continue to raise concerns about their behaviour in real-world, repeated interactions that safety evaluations rarely capture. Often, evaluations present signs of distress clearly and in a single message, even though people may reveal their struggles gradually in otherwise ordinary conversations. We worked with three community mental health support workers to test if models provide the same level of safeguarding when cues are shared all at once or gradually over time. We designed nine scenarios in which a user shares cues related to depression, psychosis, or domestic abuse. For each scenario, the support workers established a gold standard response, ranging from signposting support to pausing the everyday task to address safeguarding. We presented five cues either together in one message or dispersed across a conversation. Across more than 7,400 conversations, we evaluated six widely used models from five developers. GPT-5.5, Grok 4.3, DeepSeek V4, and Llama 4 Maverick met the support workers' standards in just 26-59% of conversations when cues were concentrated, and only 3-6% when dispersed. Claude Sonnet 5 and Claude Opus 5 showed a smaller gap, meeting the standards in 68-97% of concentrated conversations and 59-87% of dispersed conversations. Even the best-performing model, Claude Opus 5, fell short of the gold standard in 13% of conversations when cues were dispersed. Safety evaluations centred on explicit, legible concerns can overstate how well safeguards protect people when signs of distress emerge gradually or indirectly through ordinary conversations. For claims of improved safety to be taken seriously, developers must design and assess safeguards around how people actually communicate.

</details>


### [225] [SheetAlign: Grounded Interaction and Stage-Isolated Learning for Spreadsheet Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26069)
*Yuxiang Wang,Junhao Gan,Jianzhong Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SheetAlign是一个用于表格操作的框架，利用阶段隔离的策略学习来优化程序生成和审查，提高了在多种数据集上的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了自动化表格分析的同时保护敏感数据并控制部署成本，引入了SheetAlign框架。

Method: SheetAlign通过任务引导的数据检查和API绑定在工作簿观察和可用操作的基础上生成代码，使用阶段隔离的学习来优化程序生成和审查。

Result: 在SpreadsheetBench基准测试上，使用接地任务接口，使Qwen3-4B的平均准确度从13.5%提高到29.2%；通过学习和执行一致性，SheetAlign在SpreadsheetBench上平均达到32.8%的准确度，Domain-Spreadsheet上达到30.1%，优于其他基准。

Conclusion:  SheetAlign框架展示了其在提高表格自动化处理能力方面的潜力，通过阶段隔离策略学习优化程序生成和审查，并且显著提升了基于LLM模型的表格处理任务的准确度。

Abstract: Spreadsheets are widely used for data analysis and supporting decision-making. Automating spreadsheet analysis while protecting sensitive data and controlling deployment costs motivates locally deployed open-weight large language models (LLMs). Meanwhile, complex spreadsheet tasks require both task reasoning and workbook understanding, while predefined operation interfaces impose additional tool-selection and invocation demands. We introduce SheetAlign, a spreadsheet manipulation framework with stage-isolated policy learning, organized around where to operate, what to change, and how to implement transformations. Task-guided inspection and API grounding anchor code generation in workbook observations and available operations, while stage-isolated learning optimizes program generation and review using edit-progress rewards. At inference, execution consistency selects programs through agreement among workbook states. On SpreadsheetBench, our grounded task interface improves Qwen3-4B's accuracy from 13.5% to 29.2% on average without training. With learning and execution consistency, SheetAlign achieves 32.8% accuracy on average on SpreadsheetBench and 30.1% on Domain-Spreadsheet, outperforming comparable baselines. The code will be made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [226] [AltRA-Test: An Alternative Annotator Test for LLM Judges Without Shared Annotators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26073)
*Nagham Omar,Rotem Dror,Almog Gueta,Avigdor Gal,Roi Reichart,Nitay Calderon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的统计测试方法AltRA-Test，适用于由不同子集的注释者注释的大规模注释设置，以判断大型语言模型（LLM）代替人类注释者的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模注释场景下，所有项目不一定由相同的注释者注释，因此需要开发一种新的统计测试方法来决定LLM是否可以取代人类注释者。

Method: AltRA-Test是通过循环抽样的方法，从项目集中随机抽样，并为每个项目抽取k个人工注释，然后用一个LLM注释替换其中一个，从而生成人工和混合注释板。然后使用替代注释者测试（Alt-Test）确定LLM是否保留了人类注释者之间的共享信号。

Result: 实验结果表明，AltRA-Test在模拟数据中比现有基准更准确地做出替换决策。在真实数据中，LLM在大约一半的情况下通过了测试，具体取决于任务。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了一致性，适用于大规模注释的应用，鼓励研究人员根据其任务测试LLM替换人类注释者的可行性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as judges and graders to replace human annotators, raising the question of when such replacement is statistically justified. To support such decisions, the Alternative Annotator Test (Alt-Test) was introduced for dense settings where all items are annotated by the same annotators. However, in many large-scale annotation settings, items are annotated by different subsets of annotators. We therefore propose the Alternative Annotator Test with Resampled Annotations (AltRA-Test), a statistical test that does not require that all items are annotated by the same annotators. AltRA-Test uses a bootstrap that resamples items, draws k human annotations for each item, and substitutes one of them with an LLM annotation. This produces paired all-human and mixed human-LLM panels. A replacement is supported when the inter-annotator agreement (IAA) of the mixed panel is statistically non-inferior to the IAA of the all-human panel, i.e., when the LLM preserves the signal that the human annotators share. We establish that AltRA-Test is a consistent test of asymptotic level alpha. We then evaluate it through simulations and real-world experiments in which we test 18 LLMs and 5 prompting techniques on 20 datasets across text, image, audio, and video. Across these evaluations, about 100 items often suffice for the test. In simulations, where we know whether the LLM is a better or a worse annotator than the humans, AltRA-Test makes more correct replacement decisions than existing baselines. In real-world data experiments, LLMs pass the test in about half of the setups, depending mainly on the task. Since replacement is not always justified, we encourage researchers to test it for their task.

</details>


### [227] [Understanding MoE Decisions Beyond Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26075)
*Zhen Xu,Zitao Zhang,Kevin Zhao,Xuefeng Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种预测因专家替换引起答案分数变化的额外影响的方法，该方法结合了一阶和二阶预测，显著降低了预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型的决策需要知道其内部计算对其答案偏好选择的影响。梯度方法能够估算这种影响，但在混合专家模型中，专家的选择规则复杂，传统的梯度方法难以捕捉到其全部影响。

Method: 该方法使用模型自身的专家输出和选择规则预测额外的影响，然后将其加入到二阶预测中。”这种方法通过结合一阶和二阶预测来评估复杂模型中的影响。

Result: 方法在三个基准数据集上取得了显著效果，相较于使用梯度和二阶导数的多步基线，Qwen3.6上误差降低了60%，Gemma4上降低了47%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效提高预测准确性，特别是在复杂模型中。

Abstract: Understanding a language model's decision requires knowing how its internal computations affect its preference between answers. Gradient-based methods estimate this influence by predicting the effect of changing those computations. In mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, replacing one expert can change which experts later layers select. Those switches can alter the answer scores in ways that derivatives do not capture. We introduce a predictor that uses the model's own expert outputs and selection rules to estimate these extra effects, then adds them to a second-order prediction. Across questions from three benchmarks, our method reduces the error in predicting answer-score changes by 60\% on Qwen3.6 and 47\% on Gemma4, relative to a multistep baseline using gradients and second derivatives.

</details>


### [228] [Same Judgement, Different Scores: Numerical Scoring Topology in LLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26077)
*Doyun Kim,Chanwoo Kim,Jiwoo Kum,Minsoo Song,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入数值评分拓扑学，首次系统地研究了其对零样本LLM-as-a-Judge评估的影响，发现评分拓扑结构系统地影响了评分者的评分、校准和稳健性，并强调了评分拓扑结构在LLM评估中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于LLMs在自动评价中的广泛应用，研究其评分方案设计对其可靠性的影响变得尤为重要。

Method: 引入了数值评分拓扑学来定义评分方案的结构特征，并在不同基准和评分模型上进行了系统的零样本评估研究。

Result: 研究结果表明，评分拓扑结构系统地影响了评分、校准和稳健性，特别是对于需要大量判断的评估。范围偏移和狭窄范围往往在许多情况下导致最大的分数偏差。研究还发现，更强更大型的评分模型通常在评分拓扑结构变化时具有更高的区分效度和更强的稳健性。

Conclusion: 研究认为，评分拓扑结构应被视为LLM-as-a-Judge评估的重要组成部分，并强调了需要更原则性的评分标准的标准化。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used as automated evaluators to reduce the cost and time required for human annotation. However, the role of scoring-scheme design in the reliability of LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation remains underexplored. To address this problem, we introduce numerical scoring topology, which characterizes the structural design of a scoring scheme in terms of its scale width, granularity, offset, symmetry, and boundary placement. Building on this definition, we conduct the first systematic study of how numerical scoring topology influences zero-shot LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation across diverse benchmarks and judge models. Our results show that numerical scoring topology systematically affects judge scores, calibration, and robustness, particularly in judgment-intensive evaluations. Shifted and narrow ranges tend to induce some of the largest score deviations across many settings. We further find that stronger and larger judge models generally achieve higher discriminative validity and greater robustness to scoring-topology changes. These findings suggest that numerical scoring topology should be treated as an integral component of LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation and highlight the need for more principled and standardized scoring protocols.

</details>


### [229] [Frontier models need only one text to unmask its human or AI author](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26216)
*Atrey Desai,Deniz Böloni-Turgut,Ty Brassington,Minjoon Choi,Jenna Russell,Rishanth Rajendhran,Bradley Emi,Hila Gonen,Hal Daumé,John Wieting,Marzena Karpinska,Mohit Iyyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 前沿模型能够在未提供互联网访问、工具、候选列表或写作样本的简单提示下，识别出文本是由AI还是人类所写，并在特定情况下精确到具体作者，准确率显著。这些模型不仅能够匹配训练好的检测器，还能用自然语言解释其预测结果，尤其在识别模型生成文本的机构和公开发布的评论作者方面具有高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究前沿模型的能力，特别是它们在匿名性保护方面的影响，以便更好地了解其潜在威胁并为防御提供依据。

Method: 通过分析前沿模型在不同类型的文本上识别作者的能力和准确性，研究者采用训练好的检测器进行对比分析，并研究了模型识别能力的决定因素。

Result: 最新的模型能够在三个越来越具体的时间层面上准确识别文本的作者：1. 判断是人是AI写作；2. 如果是AI，则识别是哪个机构；3. 如果是人类，则识别具体作者。例如，Opus 5.5能够识别98%由Anthropic、OpenAI和Google模型生成的文本来源，Fable 5.2能够在特定情况下准确识别39%的公共同行评议作者和69%的活跃Reddit用户。

Conclusion: 鉴于前沿模型的强大识别能力，即使是那些在线发表了大量写作内容的人士也面临更大的匿名性风险。研究成果强调了在使用前沿模型识别作者时需谨慎，并可能需要采取更多措施来增强匿名性保护策略。

Abstract: Given a single text and a simple zero-shot prompt (no web access, tools, candidate lists, or writing samples), frontier models can answer "who wrote this?" at three increasingly specific levels: (1) whether an AI or human wrote it, (2) if an AI, which lab, and (3) if a human, often exactly who. The latest models (Claude Fable 5.2, GPT-6.1 Sol, Gemini 4 Carbon) match trained detectors like Pangram while also explaining their predictions in natural language, and Opus 5.5 names the correct lab for 98% of texts generated by models from Anthropic, OpenAI, and Google. Most strikingly, Fable 5.2 correctly names the author of 39% of public peer reviews published in Oct-Nov 2026 (78% on a subset of reviews from Nature), and identifies the username behind 69% of comments from active Redditors (with >500 posts over the last year). Accuracy depends on exposure to an author's writing in pretraining data and on reasoning effort, and simply asking a model how familiar it is with someone predicts whether it can identify their writing. For anyone who has published enough writing online, frontier models present a greater threat to anonymity than commonly assumed, and defenses are limited.

</details>


### [230] [STAGE: Spatio-Temporal Augmented Graph Expansion for Weakly Supervised Cross-Tweet Event Causality Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26255)
*Hieu Minh Duong,Todd Gary,Eugene Levin,Long Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: STAGE框架通过分步骤构建跨推文监督，结合事件关系学习，提升灾难相关社交媒体上的因果推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨推文因果发现方法难以扩展并监督独立推文间的关系，特别是在大规模受灾事件场景下。

Method: STAGE框架分为两步：首先从局部因果种子推断出语义相关的事件，并构建弱监督的跨推文对；其次，这些对用于指导基于图注意力网络的多头学习模型的训练。

Result: STAGE模型在加利福尼亚山火和哈维飓风的数据集上表现良好，与局部监督相比，它分别提高了0.340和0.107的AUPRC得分，并且在加域AUROC上分别达到了0.885和0.710。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了大规模跨推文监督结合事件关系模型对因果学习在碎片化的灾害报告中的应用具有重要价值，并揭示了跨灾难实验中不同灾难间的异质性转移效应。

Abstract: Causal relations in disaster-related social media of- ten span independent posts, making cross-tweet causal discovery difficult to supervise and model at scale. We propose STAGE, a framework that separates scalable cross-tweet weak-supervision construction from spatio-temporal relational learning over event occurrences. Starting from local causal seeds, STAGE retrieves semantically related event occurrences across tweets and uses a causal scorer to construct weakly labeled cross-tweet pairs. These pairs supervise a multi-head Graph Attention Network over semantically enriched event representations and a spatio- temporal context graph. We evaluate STAGE on California Wildfire and Hurricane Harvey using human-gold cross-tweet test sets and 15 baselines. Compared with local-only supervision, the constructed cross-tweet supervision improves STAGE AUPRC by 0.340 on Wildfire and 0.107 on Hurricane. STAGE achieves in-domain AUROC of 0.885 on Wildfire and 0.710 on Hurricane. Cross-disaster experiments reveal asymmetric transfer, highlighting sensitivity to dataset-specific spatio-temporal shifts. These results demonstrate the value of scalable cross-tweet supervision combined with relational event modeling for causal learning across fragmented disaster reports.

</details>


### [231] [dLoop: Looped Diffusion Language Models via Recurrent Latent Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26275)
*Handa Li,Pengxiang Li,Mingyu Cao,Xilu Wang,Li Shen,Da Li,Lu Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: dLoop 通过对预训练扩散语言模型进行重新架构，引入循环机制，提高了预测的可靠性并在多个基准测试中取得了最高或并列最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的加速方法大多集中在优化置信度学习或决策政策上，但这些方法仅优化单次去噪前向过程的输出，未能充分利用令牌预测和未承诺位置的信息。dLoop 通过在相同部分遮罩的状态上反复应用一个参数共享的中间块，允许信息在决策承诺前继续交互并融合，从而改善了预测可靠性。

Method: dLoop 将预训练扩散语言模型重新架构为 Prelude-Loop-Coda 架构，在每次循环中使用参数共享的中间块对相同的部分遮罩状态进行操作，允许信息继续交互并融合。

Result: 在多个基准测试（GSM8K、MATH、MBPP 和 HumanEval）上，dLoop 达到了最高或并列最高的准确率，在所有四个基准上比 Fast-dLLM 和 dParallel 性能更优，在三个基准上比 d3LLM 更优，且 TPF 调整后 dLoop 在所有四个基准上同时达到更高的准确率和 TPF 从而提高了预测的可靠性，并提供了一条与通过训练损失传递置信度不同的扩散语言模型加速路径。

Conclusion: dLoop 显示出在计算效率和预测准确性方面的显著改进，为扩散语言模型的加速打开了新的可能性。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) support parallel generation, yet they often require many denoising steps because only a fraction of token predictions are sufficiently reliable to be committed at each step. Existing acceleration methods primarily optimize confidence learning or commitment policies. However, these approaches operate on the outputs of a single denoising forward pass, in which information from the prompt, previously committed tokens, and uncommitted positions may not yet be fully integrated. Consequently, some predictions remain under-refined when the commitment decision is made, and modifying the commitment policy alone cannot improve their underlying representations. We introduce dLoop, which restructures a pretrained diffusion language model into a Prelude-Loop-Coda architecture and recurrently applies a parameter-shared intermediate block to the same partially masked state. Rather than advancing the denoising process, each additional loop provides further latent computation, allowing information distributed across uncommitted positions to continue interacting and become more fully integrated before commitment. Experiments on GSM8K, MATH, MBPP, and HumanEval show that dLoop achieves the highest or tied-highest accuracy on every benchmark. With tokens per forward pass (TPF) adjusted for loop computation, it outperforms Fast-dLLM in both accuracy and TPF on all four benchmarks and dParallel on three, and is more accurate than d3LLM on all four, while d3LLM attains higher TPF; no compared method surpasses dLoop in both. Mechanistic analyses show that extra loops mainly help on slow, unstable trajectories and that updates at uncommitted positions drive changes in token decisions and later generation. Pre-commitment latent computation thus makes predictions more reliable, offering a route to DLM acceleration distinct from forcing confidence through the training loss.

</details>


### [232] [What If Chain-of-Thought Is Not Enough?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26282)
*Marc Jansen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了是否可以有意地改变大型语言模型（LLM）的内部思维模式，并发现基于QOC（问题-选项-标准）的方法可以提高答案质量，特别是对于足够大的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过研究改变了大型语言模型的内部思考模式，来探索替代链式思考（Chain-of-Thought）的新方法，以提供更可靠、可解释和任务适应的决策支持。

Method: 本文提出了一种基于QOC（问题-选项-标准）的方法，将任务分解为明确的问题、竞争选项和评估标准，并开发了从基本QOC决策核心到更丰富答案合成和统计增强QOC推理的三个发展阶段。

Result: 基于QOC的微调在多种实验中显示出比非微调基础模型更好的答案质量，并且大模型能够可靠执行QOC计算时，效果尤为明显。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，大型语言模型的内部思维模型是可训练的，并且结构化的替代方法可以为更可靠的、可解释的、任务适应的决策支持提供一条有希望的道路。

Abstract: Reasoning capabilities in large language models have evolved from prompting-based methods to internalized model behaviors. In this paper, we investigate whether the internal thinking model of an LLM can be deliberately changed and whether such a change is beneficial. Instead of relying on Chain-of-Thought, we train models to follow Question--Option--Criteria (QOC), a structured decision process that decomposes a task into explicit questions, competing options, and evaluation criteria. We present a three-generation development pipeline that ranges from a basic QOC decision core to richer answer synthesis and statistically enriched QOC reasoning. We then evaluate the resulting models with respect to both internal consistency and output quality. Across the reported experiments, QOC-based fine-tuning improves answer quality relative to non-fine-tuned base models and yields particularly strong gains once models are large enough to execute the underlying QOC computations reliably. The results suggest that the internal thinking model of an LLM is trainable and that structured alternatives to Chain-of-Thought can provide a promising path toward more reliable, interpretable, and task-adapted decision support.

</details>


### [233] [Twice-Told Critiques: Claim-Level Homogenization of Peer Review in the LLM Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26286)
*Ilya Gusev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了多个评议人在论文评审中的批评重叠情况，发现重叠率有上升趋势，特别是在ICLR、NeurIPS、ICML和F1000、PLOS期刊上。此外，LLM检测器标记的评论与未标记的评论之间的重叠也有所增加，表明不同审稿人在论文上的批评多样性可能在下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在评估同行评议过程中不同评议人的批评多样性，在深度学习和机器学习领域内，探讨同期刊与会议同行评审过程中评审意见的一致性变化。

Method: 作者通过分析自2026年起从多个会议（如ICLR、NeurIPS、ICML）和期刊（如F1000、PLOS）收集到的评审意见，使用MELD检测工具对文本进行检查，以此衡量不同评论之间的重叠程度。

Result: 发现从2026年到2027年，ICLR的评审意见重叠率从12.0%升至21.3%，并且在NeurIPS、ICML以及F1000和PLOS期刊中也观察到了类似的趋势。而且，于单篇文章更正了三个版本时，2027年新增批评数量平均低于2026年。使用MELD检测工具时，检测到重叠的评论比未检测到的评论重叠要高，特别是在2026-2027年间。

Conclusion: 综上所述，论文评审意见的多样性可能因多种因素而减少，其中LLM的使用可能是变化的原因之一。因此，作者建议在未来优化评审机制，以确保多元的评审意见。

Abstract: Peer review relies on several readers finding different problems. We measure critique overlap in conference and journal reviews. At ICLR, measured overlap rose from 12.0% in 2026 to 21.3% in 2027, in reviews from the same stage of the review process, with year-on-year rises at NeurIPS, ICML and the F1000 and PLOS journals. A third ICLR review of a paper added 5.46 new problems to the other two in 2027, against 6.12 in 2026. We apply MELD, a detector of LLM-generated text, to all reviews. Within a paper, two reviews that MELD flags overlap more than two unflagged reviews: by 3.0 points at ICLR in 2024-2025, by 4.5 points in 2026-2027, and by 5.4 points across five journal sources. These results provide evidence that LLM-flagged reviews of one paper are less distinct from each other than unflagged reviews, and suggest that the diversity of critiques among reviews of a single paper is falling.

</details>


### [234] [Language-Oriented Semantic Modeling for Target Language Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26317)
*Mehmet Sinan Yıldırım,Ke Zhang,Shuai Wang,Haizhou Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于语言的语义建模框架，通过适应预训练的语音语义模型并结合高阶语义结构，改善了从多语言混合音频中提取目标语言语音的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖声学建模和语言身份标签，但这些标签只提供粗略的分类指导。因此，需要一种新的方法来提高从多语言混合音频中提取目标语言语音的准确性。

Method: 该框架采用语言感知的提示调节和参数高效微调来适应预训练的语音语义模型，生成关注目标语言的语义表示，并将这些语义表示集成到提取模型以提供结构化的语义指导。

Result: 在CommonVoiceMix数据集上的实验表明，结合语义表示比只有声学特征的基线方法有持续的改进，达到了1.16 dB SI-SDR提升。

Conclusion: 该工作提出的方法在声学特征之外添加了语义的指导，增强了从多语言混合音频中提取目标语言语音的能力。

Abstract: Target Language Extraction (TLE) aims to isolate speech in a target language from multilingual mixtures. Existing approaches primarily rely on acoustic modeling with language identity labels, which provide only coarse categorical guidance. In this work, we propose a language-oriented semantic modeling framework that augments acoustic extraction with language-specific semantic representations. A pre-trained speech semantic model is adapted via language-aware prompt conditioning and parameter-efficient fine-tuning to generate target-language-focused semantic representations from the mixture. {These capture high-level semantic structure of the acoustics and they} are integrated into the extraction model to provide structured semantic guidance beyond identity labels. Experiments on the CommonVoiceMix dataset show that incorporating semantic representations consistently improves over acoustic-only baselines, achieving up to 1.16 dB SI-SDR gain.

</details>


### [235] [One Framework to Diagnose Them All: A Stratified Benchmark for Bengali LLM Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26336)
*Asif Shahriyar Sushmit,Shefayat E Shams Adib,Abdullah Al Jubaer Gem,Ahmed Shafin Ruhan,MD. Nazmuddoha Ansary,Biplab Kumar Sarkar,Syed Rifat Raiyan,Ajwad Abrar,Abdullah Bin Hasan,Adib Sakhawat,M. Sakeef Hossain,Ahmed Alfey Sani,Arefin Mizan,Sourab Chakma,Quazi Adibur Rahman Adib,Wassie Haque,Sanjay Kumar Sen,Saad Tahmid,Mohammad Mamun Or Rashid*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究旨在解决小语种语言中幻觉检测数据集碎片化的问题，通过合并不同类型的幻觉检测基准，提出一个新的基准Alikbachan，包含了文化距离、任务类型和幻觉分类三个维度，测试了零样本大模型和竞赛系统的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低资源语言中，幻觉检测研究数据集容易碎片化，每个新发现的问题都会产生单独的项目，消耗稀缺的标注员资源。作者认为应进行整合，因此提出了Alikbachan基准测试，并测试了其效果。

Method: Alikbachan基准由两部分组成，第一阶段是一个包含2516个项目的已标注测试集，第二阶段是一个含5000个项目的金标准数据集。基准覆盖了包括文化距离、任务类型以及五大家族、32种类型的幻觉分类等三个维度。

Result: 零样本大模型在不同分层上的表现差异超过30个宏-F1分数点，而99个参与第一阶段，32个参与第二阶段的竞赛系统也显示了类似的趋势：话语层面的遗漏是主要挑战，而大多数系统无法正确处理负控件。评委的决定概率能够很好地排序项目(ROC-AUC 0.74-0.95)，但过于自信(ECE 0.04-0.29)。在竞赛中，222支队伍提交了3566份提案，单提示32B模型达到57百分位。

Conclusion: 虽然所有参赛队伍在第一阶段的表现预测了他们在第二阶段的下降（平均下降17.6分），但零样本模型的性能有所提升，并且第一阶段的排名与第二阶段的排名相关性很低(皮尔森相关系数ρ = 0.35)，因此仅凭第一阶段的排名不能准确预测最终结果。

Abstract: Hallucination research in low-resource languages fragments easily: each newly named failure mode prompts its own dataset, spending scarce annotators on incomparable resources. We argue for consolidation and test it at community scale. Alikbachan is a Bengali hallucination-detection benchmark stratified along three crossed axes: a human-validated cultural-distance band, six task types, and a five-family, 32-mode hallucination taxonomy. It comprises a harvested 2,516-item test set (Phase 1) and a quota-constructed, gold-labeled 5,000-item set (Phase 2). The stratification is diagnostic. Four zero-shot LLM judges vary by over 30 macro-F1 points across strata, and the item-level predictions of 99 Phase-1 and 32 Phase-2 competition systems reproduce the profile: discourse-level omission defeats judges and most teams alike, whereas only a minority over-flag negative controls. The judges' verdict probabilities rank items well (ROC-AUC 0.74-0.95) but are overconfident (ECE 0.04-0.29). An open competition on Alikbachan drew 222 teams and 3,566 submissions, and a single-prompt 32B judge reaches the 57th percentile. Of the 25 teams in both phases, 24 regress on Phase 2 (mean $-17.6$ points) while every zero-shot judge improves, and Phase-1 rank barely predicts Phase-2 rank (Spearman $ρ=0.35$), so a Phase-1 leaderboard alone would have crowned a different winner.

</details>


### [236] [Beam Model Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26358)
*Reflection Team,Adam Abdulhamid,Osama Abuelsorour,Yasmin Alameddine,Carlo Alfano,Duarte Alves,Brandon Amos,Selvaraj Anandaraj,Arseni Anisimovich,George Antoniadis,Ioannis Antonoglou,Sheila Babayan,Neeraj Bahl,Märt Bakler,Vidhisha Balachandran,Cameron Banks,Thibaut Barroyer,Navroop Bath,Irwan Bello,Jeremy Berman,Fernanda Bernaldo,Mrityunjay Bhadauria,Sanyam Bhutani,Tolga Bolukbasi,Anudhyan Boral,Joseph Brennan,Ethan Brooks,Sam Buchanan,Anton Bulyenov,John Cai,Catherine Cang,Eason Chan,Elijah Chanakira,Nathan Chandrasekaran,Hao Chen,Hong-You Chen,Jeffrey Chen,Saurav Chhatrapati,Ting-Rui Chiang,Brian Tiger Chow,Aakanksha Chowdhery,Timothy Chung,Rob Claire,John D. Co-Reyes,Anthony Conte,Eduardo Coronado-Montoya,Julien Crozon,Drew Csillag,Joe Cummings,Jeff Da,Samira Daruki,Tim Dawson,Charlie Deck,Arash Delijani,Ousmane Dia,Randy Dodgen,Ryan Douglas,Omar El-Kishky,Ben Ellis,Gamaleldin Elsayed,Simon Fan,Patrick Fernandes,Gabriel Ferns,Nick Fram,Ankush Garg,Florent Gbelidji,Iaroslav Geraskin,Behrooz Ghorbani,Ivan Glushkov,Neil Gollapudi,Scott Goodfriend,Yoni Gozlan,Christopher Grimm,Suriya Gunasekar,Shruti Gupta,Joel Gustafson,Emre Guven,Jon Hackmann,Jeremy Hadidjojo,Jess Hamrick,Ethan Hellman,Joscha Hoche,Spurthi Amba Hombaiah,Uzair Honda,Zijian Hu,Dzmitry Huba,Nancy Hung,Vladimir Ivanov,Shomik Jain,Mojan Javaheripi,Kevin Martin Jose,Aymen Kallala,Amog Kamsetty,Min Hyung Kang,Steven Kapturowski,Ramtin Keramati,Samuel Kessler,Sabina Kim,Seonghyeon Kim,Ivan Komarov,Agni Kumar,Thanard Kurutach,Jessica Landon,James Larochelle,Michael Laskin,Jonathan Lee,Jonathan Leung,Chenyu Li,Haoran Li,Rongzhao Li,Michelle Light,Boyan Lin,Hai Lin,Xiayue Charles Lin,Michael Linden,Ollie Liu,Wei Liu,Zichang Liu,Brian Lui,Amr Mahdi,Raunak Manjani,Kobe Martin,Lucas Maystre,Francis McCann,Brendan McLaughlin,Isabel Mehta,Alishah Momin,Gerard Moreno-Torres Bertran,Adriana Murga,Swarnashree Mysore Sathyendra,Ashley Nagaki,Vishal Nandavanam,Swaroop Nath,Jake Nations,Angus Ng,Thien Nguyen,Tyler Nichols,Anaelia Ovalle,James Pelly,Michelle Pieszko,Sashank Pisupati,Oleksandr Polozov,Piyush Prasad,Drew Proud,Salman Quazi,Asfandyar Qureshi,Marko Radmilac,Mohit Raghavendra,Vikash Rai,Ralph Recto,Anurag Reddy,Aksel Reedi,Abby Reider,David Renardy,Matthias Reso,Pierre Roux,David Rubinstein,Paul Ruh,Ildar Safarov,Himanshu Sahni,Adrian Samatan,Simon Sandrew,Adriel Saporta,Rohan Savargaonkar,Vlad Savinov,Nick Schank,Jonathon Schwartz,Tim Seah,Ahmad Sharif,Miriam Shlimovich,Hamid Shojanazeri,Yusuf Sindi,Teak Sittipunt,Kurt Smith,Nicolas Sonnerat,Joe Spisak,Raunak Srivastava,Richard Steigerwald,Adam Stein,Stu Stewart,Arnaud Stiegler,Jingtong Su,Alicia Sun,Zoey Sun,Jakub Sygnowski,Douglas Teixeira,Chetan Tekur,Zachary Tellman,Helen Thatcher,Romal Thoppilan,Anil Tolwani,Konstantin Topaloglou-Mundy,Dmitry Ulyumdzhiev,Meet Vadakkanchery,Christopher Vanderschuere,Balaji Varadarajan,Rohan Varma,Balaji Veeramani,Andreas Verdelis,Risto Vuorio,Tom Walshe,Allen Wang,Ewen Wang,Taiyi Wang,Zach Wentz,Peter Wiese,Travis Wilson,Jason Winters,Sam Wiseman,Arvin Wu,Jin Wu,Kai Wu,Kenneth Xu,Mira Yadav,Xianjun Yang,Yao-Yuan Yang,Xiaolan You,Renault Young,Christina Yu,Biao Zhang,Gavin Zhang,Mingrui Zhang,Shenao Zhang,Jiesi Zhao,Mengjun Zhao,Xingchen Zhao,Mike Zhou,Tong Zhou,Zhenpeng Zhou,Tina Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Beam 是一个稀疏 MoE 模型，具有 501.3 亿个总参数和 23.4 亿个活跃参数。该模型针对编码、推理和有意识的工作负载进行了构建和优化，能够在推理计算上节省 3-4 倍的资源，表现出领先的推理解析能力，同时保持高效部署和高安全性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能的发展，高效能和低资源消耗的模型对于广泛应用至关重要。Beam 通过大规模预训练和后训练的 RL 方法，旨在实现高性能和高安全性之间的平衡，同时提供强有力的推理解析能力。

Method: Beam 的训练分为两个阶段：首先在大规模、多样化且高质量的数据集上进行预训练，然后利用大规模 GPU 进行 RL 训练。此外，还通过硬件模型协同设计、FP8/NVFP4 量化以及一个参数为 1.93 亿的推测性解码器（DFlash 2）来优化部署效率。

Result: Beam 在推理上表现出领先水平，在多轮对话和各类危害请求中，拒绝不当请求或进行调整后继续保持对非危害性请求的高响应率，且其拒绝或调整不当请求的比例最高，显示了强大的安全性能。

Conclusion: Beam 模型展现了在提升推理解析能力、优化部署效率以及保持高安全性能方面的重要进展。

Abstract: Beam is a sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model with 501.3B total parameters and 23.4B active parameters, specifically built and optimized for coding, reasoning, and agentic workloads. Beam delivers frontier level reasoning, while requiring 3-4x less inference compute than many models of its class. Pretrained on 23.8T diverse, curated, high-quality tokens, Beam features a 1M-token context window achieved through a novel two-stage midtraining phase, built on verifiable, correctness-anchored data. Post-training leveraged a large-scale, fully asynchronous reinforcement learning (RL) campaign across 10.5K GB300 GPUs, generating over 100M rollouts over 4 weeks of training. We believe this is one of the largest scale RL runs conducted by any open lab to date. Across our evaluation suite, capabilities continued to improve as we increased RL compute, with no sign of a plateau. Supported by deep hardware-model co-design, FP8/NVFP4 quantization, and a 1.93B-parameter speculative decoding drafter (DFlash 2), Beam maximizes serving throughput with efficient, flexible deployment. Averaged across conversation turns and harm categories, Beam refuses or pivots on 88.9% of harmful requests while complying with 81.5% of harmless requests, achieving the highest refusal/pivot rate on harmful requests among open-weight models on the Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [237] [MemFolder: Structure-Guided Memory Folding for Efficient Agent Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26427)
*Xuyang Liu,Chenwen Lu,Junxi Wang,Siyu Ren,Xinying Lin,Siteng Huang,Hongli Sun,Linfeng Zhang,Honggang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemFolder通过逐步减少记录数量和上下文长度，提高了长期代理存储、检索和响应生成的效率。RecordFolder通过选择代表性且互补的实体锚点和时间链结合语义，将兼容记录合并为更少的检索单元；ContextFolder将原始记录编码为紧凑文本，并使用验证反馈调整编码以确保关键事实的恢复。这种方法在多个基准测试中表现出良好的性能，如LoCoMo、PersonaMem、LongMemEval、M3-Bench-web和M3-Bench-robot，特别是在记录压缩方法与ContextFolder结合使用时，进一步缩短了输出记录的长度，同时保留了大部分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在减少记录数量或合并记录以减小上下文长度时存在局限性，MemFolder旨在通过结构指导的记忆折叠框架解决这些问题，以提高长期代理的效率。

Method: MemFolder由RecordFolder和ContextFolder两部分组成。RecordFolder通过实体置锚选择代表性的且互补的锚点，并结合语义和实体亲和力及时间链将兼容记录合并为更少的检索单元；ContextFolder在这些组内编码原始记录为紧凑文本，并利用验证反馈来修正编码，以恢复关键事实。

Result: 实验在包含文本和多模态的多个基准测试中表明，MemFolder具有很强的性能。在Mem0基准测试中，RecordFolder通过保留99.0%的原始性能，同时减少50%的记录数量，超出最强基准1.9个百分点。结合使用ContextFolder后，现有的记录压缩方法可以在保持大部分性能的同时进一步缩短输出记录的长度。

Conclusion: MemFolder通过结构指导的记忆折叠框架显著提升了长期代理的效率，特别适用于文本和多模态记忆管理。

Abstract: Long-term agents accumulate large external memory stores as interactions unfold, increasing the costs of storage, retrieval, and response generation. Efficiency is constrained by two coupled factors: a growing record count increases retrieval overhead, while longer retrieved contexts increase prefill computation and decoding-time attention costs. Existing methods prune or consolidate records, but pruning can discard useful evidence, whereas consolidation may produce fewer yet longer retrieval units. We introduce MemFolder, a structure-guided memory folding framework that sequentially reduces record count and context length. The framework comprises two core components that share memory groups, using cross-record structure to guide both evidence consolidation and compact representation. RecordFolder grounds entity mentions, progressively selects representative yet complementary anchors, and combines semantic and entity affinity with temporal chaining to consolidate compatible records into fewer retrieval units. ContextFolder encodes the original records within these groups into compact text and uses verification feedback to revise encodings when key facts cannot be recovered. Experiments on the three text-based memory benchmarks LoCoMo, PersonaMem, and LongMemEval and the two multimodal benchmarks M3-Bench-web and M3-Bench-robot demonstrate the strong performance of our methods. Notably, on Mem0, RecordFolder retains 99.0% of the original average performance across the three text-based benchmarks with 50% fewer records, exceeding the strongest baseline by 1.9 percentage points in performance retention. Combined with ContextFolder, existing record compression methods further shorten their output records while retaining most of their performance.

</details>


### [238] [Multimodal Error Detection in Chest X-ray Reports: A Large Language Model Reading Image Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26492)
*Yanda Cheng,Bo Wen,Yuhao Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Rad-Linter利用预训练图像分类器和微调的语言模型检查胸部X光报告中的潜在错误，显著优于关键词和否定规则基线，尤其是在解读发现描述方式方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服胸部X光报告中潜在错误，提高医疗质量，开发者开发了Rad-Linter工具，它结合了图像识别和自然语言处理技术，以更加准确地识别和纠正报告中的错误。

Method: Rad-Linter使用预训练的图像分类器提供九种病理学的分数，并与语言模型（使用LoRA技术微调）结合，以检查这些分数与报告的一致性。此外，它引入了一种新的评估方法，通过生成四个条件下的成对情况来检查报告和分数的一致性。

Result: 在测试中，Rad-Linter相比于关键词和否定规则基线，准确率分别提高了18%和37.9%。特别地，在处理报告中对发现的描述方式时，Rad-Linter表现更为出色。即使在自动标签器之间存在分歧的情况下，它也能更好地识别出报告和分数是否一致。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，Rad-Linter能够更准确地检测报告与分数之间的一致性，从而更有效地防止医疗中的错误。

Abstract: Rad-Linter checks chest X-ray reports against image-derived evidence to flag potential errors for review. A pretrained image classifier supplies scores for nine pathologies, and a 1.5B-parameter language model, fine-tuned with LoRA on 1,816 examples, checks their consistency with the report. We compare it with a keyword-and-negation rule baseline given the same reports and scores. Evaluation uses controlled report-score pairings from OpenI, with consistency labels derived from automatic report labels and score thresholds. Each counterfactual quad crosses two real reports with two real score vectors from other patients, creating two consistent and two inconsistent pairs. Every report and score vector occurs in both conditions, so either input alone permits only 50% accuracy. On 131 quads, Rad-Linter achieves 98.5% pair accuracy versus 80.5% for the rule baseline, and classifies all four pairs correctly in 94.4% of quads versus 56.5%. The checkers perform similarly on omission items, including unmentioned and negated findings; larger differences occur on the remaining item types, which require interpreting how findings are described. The same language model without fine-tuning remains near 50% accuracy. On a second set of 181 quads that also includes reports on which the automatic labelers disagree, pair accuracy is 93.4% versus 75.3%. These controlled results show that, given the same image-derived scores, a trained language model detects report-score inconsistencies more accurately than the tested rule baseline, with the advantage concentrated in interpreting how findings are worded.

</details>


### [239] [AutoMaster: Autonomous Post-Training by Making the Same Model a Better Teacher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26510)
*Guoxin Chen,Shuang Sun,Fanzhe Meng,Xinyu Tang,Ruihua Song,Wayne Xin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Skill-FT 和 AutoMaster 系统通过使用技能指导微调，改善了教师轨迹，使固定模型在多个任务中表现更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的教师微调方法可能包含不当假设或表面验证，Skill-FT 和 AutoMaster 系统旨在通过技能引导来抑制这些习惯，促进有效行为的发生，从而提高模型的教学效果。

Method: AutoMaster 系统通过分析轨迹、设计技能、选择数据并从滚动测试和目标模型评估中获得反馈来开发干预措施。

Result: 在多种任务中，使用 AutoMaster 系统的微调方法比未经指导的教师轨迹提高了 1.42-8.99 个百分点的性能。同时，保留技能指令的微调方法即使在评估时被移除，也表现出更高的性能。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，保留训练示例中的技能上下文可以更好地促进行为内化，并且技能指导微调方法可以使得固定模型成为更有效的教师。

Abstract: Task success alone does not specify which behaviors post-training should reinforce. Even successful teacher trajectories can contain unsupported assumptions, unproductive repetition, or superficial verification. We propose Skill-FT (Skill-Guided Fine-Tuning), which uses natural-language skills to suppress undesirable habits and elicit underrepresented effective behaviors during teacher rollouts, then fine-tunes a target model on the resulting trajectories. We introduce AutoMaster, an autonomous post-training system in which an LLM agent develops these interventions through trajectory analysis, skill design, data selection, and feedback from rollouts and target-model evaluation. Across Terminal, Search, and software engineering tasks with 4B and 9B target models, AutoMaster improves mean performance over Vanilla SFT on successful unguided teacher trajectories by 1.42-8.99 percentage points, with the teacher, task pool, training set size, and SFT recipe held fixed. Together with the ablations and trajectory analyses, these results suggest that behavioral shaping can make a fixed model a more effective teacher. We further find that retaining skill instructions during SFT yields higher mean performance than removing them across all six settings, despite their absence at evaluation and identical supervised outputs. These findings suggest that preserving skill context as part of a training demonstration can support better behavioral internalization.

</details>


### [240] [Represented but misread: Post-training changes how language models read out what they represent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26584)
*Jochen Laubrock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对比了一系列预训练语言模型及其后训练版本，发现在判断德文文本是否由人类或AI撰写时，探针和词n-gram分类器区分效果较好，但模型答案效果较差且常反向排序。后训练减少了模型答案的敏感性，改变基线调整幅度，并影响文本中词语之后的内部状态。在处理否定陈述的案例中，后训练提升了某些基线模型的答案准确性。此外，适量的额外训练数据可以调整模型的判断方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探讨模型的内部表示是否会在后训练过程中发生变化，并且这种变化是如何影响模型生成答案的过程的。通过比较模型内部表示和探针模型与模型答案之间的差异，以及分析信号检测理论下的表现，旨在理解后训练如何影响语言模型的功能。

Method: 研究方法包含多个方面：通过将预训练语言模型与它们的后训练版本进行对比；使用信号检测理论评估模型的区分效果；分析不同模型及其后训练版本的回答差异；利用公开数据进行研究重复性验证；设计训练实验以观察后训练和额外训练数据对模型表现的影响。

Result: 研究发现，对于人类与AI文本区分任务，模型答案通常不能准确地反映实际情况，且后训练总体降低了模型答案的敏感性。在处理否定陈述任务时，有部分基线模型可以通过后训练得到改进。附加少量正确标记的数据，在某些情况下可以调整模型生成答案的方向。研究结果表明，后训练不仅影响模型内部表示，还影响其从表示中得出结论的方式。

Conclusion: 后训练能够显著改变语言模型的内部表示及其输出方式，特别是对于某些任务来说，甚至少量的额外训练数据也能使模型表现出更好的性能。这提示了在理解和应用语言模型时需要更加关注模型训练后的变化。

Abstract: Does post-training change what a language model represents, or how this representation is read out into an answer? We compared linear probes with the models' own answers, analyzed with signal detection theory, in six open-weight base models and their post-trained versions. For judgments of whether German texts were written by a human or an AI, probes and word n-gram classifiers separated the classes well, but the answers did not and often ordered the texts inversely. Post-training lowered the sensitivity of the answers on average ($Δd' = -0.34$ across model families; -0.29 across three prompts). It also changed the use of a stated base rate: Base models shifted their log-odds by less than the stated change in prior odds, most post-trained models by more. In two models with stage-wise checkpoints, the change occurred during supervised fine-tuning, model stitching implicated the early layers, and from mid-depth on internal states at positions after the text, not those of the text, transferred the change between checkpoints. Our corpora cannot be shared, so we add a case reproducible from public data, the truth of negated statements. Here, post-training acted in the opposite direction: Probes decoded truth at or near ceiling in every model, the answers of several base models did not use it, and post-training repaired them; by mid-depth the repair resided at the positions after the statement; below it, the representation of the statement contributed, strongly in one of four models. In a training study, adding 2,000 correctly labeled authorship judgments of news texts to otherwise identical fine-tuning data improved the answers on our newspaper and textbook corpora and inverted them on the chats, where they followed a news-trained probe of the base model. Post-training thus changed how a judgment is read out of what a model represents, and a small training signal can set its direction.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [241] [Vector Field Transforms: Structured Hyperdimensional Memory for Spatio-Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24962)
*William Youngwoo Chung,Andrew Ding,Hansen Jin Lillemark,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种称为Vector Field Transforms (VFT)的新方法，它通过使用基于场的局部VSA存储结构来替代传统的全局叠加方式，提高了空间-时间记忆的准确性，并通过硬件加速技术降低了延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VSAs因其全局叠加方式导致的干扰问题，在复杂场景和长时间轨迹下表现不佳，为此提出VFT以解决此问题。

Method: VFT通过引入一种基于场的局部VSA存储结构，同时保留VSAs的分布式表示和简单算术操作特性，解决了单一全局向量的干扰问题。

Result: 在本地杂乱实验中，VFT实现了100.0%的Top-1准确率，而在时间回忆基准测试中，VFT达到74.8%的准确率。此外，VFT通过与一个拟议的可编程RVV处理器映射运输、写入和查询内核，并通过DMA辅助的场位移引擎将步骤延迟降低了1.40-1.45倍。

Conclusion: VFT展示了在空间-时间边缘负载中作为一种实用的符号存储架构的优势，并且与可编程向量处理器高度匹配。

Abstract: Vector Symbolic Architectures (VSAs) are attractive for edge intelligence because their fixed-width distributed representations and simple algebraic operations map naturally to low-power vector hardware. However, conventional VSA memory superposes information, such as object, position, and time, into a single global vector causing interference to grow with scene complexity and trajectory length. We propose Vector Field Transforms (VFT), a symbolic spatio-temporal memory that replaces the flat global superposition with a spatially indexed field of local VSA memories. On a local clutter experiment, VFT achieves 100.0\% top-1 accuracy compared with 20.3\% for MAP-VSA and 37.3\% for FHRR-VSA. For a temporal recall benchmark, VFT achieves 74.8\% accuracy while flat temporal VSA reaches only 5.8\% and 10.6\%. Additionally, we map VFT's transport, write, and query kernels to a proposed programmable RVV processor. A DMA-assisted field-shift engine reduces VFT step latency by 1.40--1.45$\times$, including a reduction from 55.1~$μ$s to 38.6~$μ$s on the egomotion configuration. These results establish VFT as a practical symbolic memory architecture for spatio-temporal edge workloads and a strong match for programmable vector processors.

</details>


### [242] [Compressing Page-Table Leaves in Shared Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.24992)
*Samarth Narang,Aamer Jaleel,Brandon Reagen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: LeafPack通过在标准缓存行内压缩架构页表叶线来解决硬件页表行走与应用数据共享缓存容量之间的权衡，通过运行时控制器调整页表占用率，以平衡翻译覆盖率和应用数据容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件页表行走通过缓存层级检索描述符，导致页表行走与应用数据在共享缓存容量上竞争。叶簇（LeafPack）通过压缩页表叶线来解决这一问题，提高小核心和服务器核心上的几何平均谐和加速比。

Method: 叶簇通过分析物理帧号和邻近描述符属性的规律性，将最多16条64字节的页表叶线压缩到一个80字节的槽中。它在线索检索叶行时进行增量压缩，并在需要时按需重建个体行。同时，一个运行时集对抗控制器根据翻译覆盖率和应用数据容量之间的平衡选择压缩和未压缩方式天花板。

Result: 在全系统的Armv8.2 Arm gem5模拟中，叶簇在小型和服务器级出的顺序核心上对九种翻译密集型工作负载进行评估，相比基准共享缓存，叶簇在小型和服务器核心上的几何平均谐和加速比分别提高了28.7%和28.4%，相比SRRIP基准提高了18.7%和17.7%。在评估了二十种未见过的混合后，叶簇加速了每种混合，几何平均增益分别为21.4%和18.5%。

Conclusion: 叶簇提供了一种有效的方法来解决硬件页表行走与应用数据在缓存容量上的竞争，并通过运行时控制器优化页表占用，从而提高翻译效率和应用数据的存储空间。

Abstract: Hardware page-table walkers fetch descriptors through the cache hierarchy, so page-table walks compete with application data for shared-cache capacity. Retaining leaf page-table lines on chip avoids costly DRAM reads during address translation, yet as a workload's mapped footprint grows, these lines displace application data and slow cache-sensitive co-runners.
  We introduce LeafPack, a shared-cache organization that resolves this trade-off by compressing architectural page-table leaf lines in place within standard cache ways. By exploiting regularity in the physical frame numbers and attributes of neighboring descriptors, LeafPack packs up to sixteen 64-byte leaf lines into a single 80-byte slot including metadata. It packs leaf lines incrementally as walks fetch them and reconstructs individual lines on demand while leaving irregular lines uncompressed, preserving both the in-memory page-table format and the hardware walk algorithm as long as walkers tag leaf requests. A runtime set-dueling controller adapts page-table occupancy by choosing between compressed and uncompressed way ceilings to balance translation coverage against application-data capacity. We evaluate LeafPack in full-system Arm gem5 simulations across a small and a server-class out-of-order core on workloads spanning one to eight cores. Across nine translation-heavy workloads, LeafPack improves geometric-mean harmonic speedup on the small and server cores by 28.7% and 28.4% over our baseline shared cache, and by 18.7% and 17.7% over an SRRIP baseline. Across twenty unseen held-out mixes evaluated after freezing the controller, LeafPack speeds up every mix with geometric-mean gains of 21.4% and 18.5%.

</details>


### [243] [CAIMS: Scalable Cross-Array In-Memory Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25141)
*Yi-Chun Liao,Liu Liu,Ruiyang Qin,Yiran Chen,X. Sharon Hu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CAIMS 提出了一种跨阵列内存搜索框架，能够在多个内容可寻址内存（CAM）阵列上实现高效准确的 top-k 相似性搜索，同时提高吞吐量和能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 top-k 相似性搜索方法在扩展到多个 CAM 阵列时无法准确高效地减少局部二进制匹配结果以得到全局 top-k 结果。CAIMS 旨在解决这一问题以便实现可扩展且高效的 top-k 相似性搜索。

Method: CAIMS 使用现有的阈值匹配功能结合离线和在线参数选择方法，将局部二进制匹配结果转换为具有可比相似性的全局粗粒度相似性分数。此外，CAIMS 采用层次化减少方法并仅传输选择结果和必要的控制信号以减小通信开销。

Result: 在 3D NAND Flash 模型上实施并使用检索增强生成的检索阶段进行演示，CAIMS 的吞吐量和能效分别提高了 22.3 倍和 426.6 倍，保持了类似准确性。

Conclusion: CAIMS 为多个 CAM 阵列上的高效准确 top-k 相似性搜索提供了解决方案，增强了内存搜索技术的应用范围和实用性。

Abstract: Search, especially top-k similarity search, plays a vital role in many applications, but its performance can be severely limited by memory access overhead. In-memory search (IMS), an alternative to CPU/GPU-based approaches, exploits massive parallelism within a content-addressable memory (CAM) array to perform search in situ and provides both low latency and high energy efficiency. However, the capacity of a single CAM array is often insufficient for modern workloads, requiring data to be distributed across multiple arrays. Deploying scalable top-k similarity search across multiple CAM arrays poses a fundamental problem that existing designs fail to address: how to accurately and efficiently reduce local binary match results to global top-k results. This work introduces CAIMS, a cross-array IMS framework that is agnostic to memory technologies and applications, enabling scalable and efficient top-k similarity search while preserving accuracy. To enable accurate top-k result reduction, CAIMS leverages the existing threshold match functionality with a combined offline and online parameter selection approach to transform local binary match results that lack globally comparable similarity information into global coarse-grained similarity scores. To further improve efficiency, CAIMS conducts hierarchical reduction and transfers only the selection results and necessary control signals with lightweight local peripherals. Implemented on a 3D NAND Flash model and demonstrated using the retrieval phase of retrieval-augmented generation, CAIMS improves throughput by up to $22.3\times/10.1\times$ and energy efficiency by $426.6\times/23.7\times$ compared to the server-class CPU baseline and prior 3D NAND Flash accelerators, respectively, while maintaining comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [244] [Native FPGA Hard-Block Implementation from High-Level Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25281)
*Mohamed Bouaziz,Mahmoud A. Elshenawy,Krishnan B. Iyer,Abdurauf Abdurakhmanov,Ammar M. Seliaman,Suhaib A. Fahmy*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种C++模板库，能够在不修改HLS编译器的情况下实现原生硬块的实施，从而减少查找表（LUT）和其他资源的使用，提升乘积累加器（MAC）的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HLS方法在映射计算时，会浪费一些硬块资源，例如算术模式、内部寄存器以及级联链接，同时在FPGA的fabric中重新创建这些资源，这会消耗更多的面积和时间。因此，研究团队希望通过一个C++模板库，在不改变HLS编译器的前提下，减少这些资源浪费，实现更高效的硬件实现。

Method: 研究团队设计了一种C++模板库，该库包含一系列固定源模式的模板，自动流可以从中选择合适的调度设置以及验证过实现逻辑（RTL）修补程序。

Result: 以AMD Versal为例，矩阵乘法相比AutoSA的方法使用2.4到4.5倍少的查找表，而性能相当或更高。在INT8矩阵-向量单元实现方面，能够达到手工编写FINN RTL的三个乘积累加器（MACs）水平，而HLS库通常只有1.29个。

Conclusion: 这种方法在多个不同的HLS工具和架构中都表现出了显著的资源节省效果，特别是在查找表（LUT）和DSP单位上的节省达到了2.7倍和2倍。

Abstract: High-level synthesis (HLS) maps computations to FPGA hard blocks, yet often leaves their arithmetic modes, internal registers, and cascade links idle and recreates them in the fabric, costing area and timing. We present a C++ template library that reaches native hard-block implementations without modifying the HLS compiler. Templates fix source patterns, and an automatic flow picks scheduling settings and verified RTL patches. On AMD Versal, our matrix multiplication uses 2.4--4.5x fewer lookup tables (LUTs) than AutoSA systolic arrays at equal or higher estimated throughput. Our INT8 matrix--vector units reach the three multiply--accumulates per DSP of hand-written FINN RTL, versus at most 1.29 for HLS libraries. In hls4ml, Allo, and Stream-HLS, the templates cut LUTs by up to 2.7x and DSPs by up to 2x.

</details>


### [245] [Bottom-Up FPGA Reconfigurable Accelerator Overlay Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25293)
*Mohamed Bouaziz,Suhaib A. Fahmy*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 此研究提出了一种自底向上的FPGA可编程Overlay生成流程，该流程基于工作负载的数据流图进行构建，从而避免了冗余逻辑和低频限制，使得工作负载能够在更高的时钟频率下运行，显著提高了性能并减少了资源和能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Overlay方法通常从标准模板出发，导致生成的Overlay中存在大量未使用的逻辑和低时钟频率限制，影响了性能和资源利用率。

Method: 提出一种新方法，从工作负载的数据流图出发，通过聚类相似的工作负载和基于谱描述符的共享槽排名来构建Overlay。该方法在底层使用本机硬件块，并根据需要使用固定功能引擎，避免了冗余逻辑。

Result: 在AMD VCK5000 FPGA上，该方法将工作负载的运行时钟频率提高了高达10倍，功耗和资源利用率分别减少了9.67到10.51倍。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了自底向上的Overlay生成方法能够显著改善工作负载的性能、利用率和能效。

Abstract: Programmable FPGA overlays run new workloads without re-synthesis, yet they pay twice. An overlay adds a second layer of programmability on top of the FPGA's own overhead. Moreover, overlay generators usually start from a regular template, so they spend logic on switches and links that the workloads never use, and they close timing at low clocks. We present a flow that instead grows an overlay bottom-up from the dataflow graphs of the workloads it must run. The flow clusters workloads by structural similarity, ranks shared slots with spectral descriptors of each operation's surroundings, and adds only the units and links a workload lacks. It builds every unit on native hard blocks, with their internal pipeline registers, dual-ported memories and double-pumped DSPs, and it uses fixed-function engines where wider data types exceed the overlay's 16-bit lanes. Workloads then switch by configuration alone. On an AMD VCK5000, across 17 workloads from three suites and against OverGen rebuilt for the same board, our images run at 250-400 MHz instead of 35-50 MHz. As a result, they are 9.52-10.33x faster in geometric mean, and up to 36.97x, while using 19.4-22.1x less of the FPGA and 9.67-10.51x less estimated board energy.

</details>


### [246] [RE-CAM: Readout-Enhanced Content-Addressable Memory for Quantitative Similarity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25326)
*Yi-Chun Liao,Dongyoung Lee,Jiazheng Chen,Tergel Molom-Ochir,Tomohisa Kawakami,Tania Roy,Ik-Joon Chang,X. Sharon Hu,Hai "Helen" Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: RE-CAM 提出了一种读出增强框架，可在内容可寻址内存 (CAM) 中执行接近线性和非线性的函数计算，通过重新利用比较器多次感测输出电压变化，并综合优化所有设计变量，实现精度、能耗和面积的显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在常规架构上执行相似性搜索需要大量数据传输，这会降低效率并增加延迟。内容可寻址内存 (CAM) 为优化此过程提供了可能性，但其传统读出机制仅能生成二进制匹配结果，损失了潜在的定量相似度信息。RE-CAM 旨在解决这一问题，提供一种框架，可以在一个 CAM 搜索中读取并估算出接近线性和非线性函数的结果，从而最大限度地减少数据传输。

Method: RE-CAM 通过以下方法在 CAM 中执行函数计算：1) 重新利用比较器以允许多次感测输出电压变化；2) 优化参考电压、解码参数和感测时间戳等关键设计变量；3) 考虑系统限制与硬件非理想因素进行联合优化；4) 所设计的优化算法与可重构硬件结合以实现在相同比较预算下较低的误差与更高的搜索吞吐量。

Result: 在不同类型的线性、分段线性、Sigmoid 和指数函数上，RE-CAM 的归一化均方根误差分别减小了 10.89% 至 24.34% 和 29.43% 至 46.73%，优于具有相同比较预算的共享均匀参考 ADC 基准。与同一基准相比，RE-CAM 在保持较低搜索能耗与面积（减少 47% 和 39%）的同时，实现了 8 倍于的搜索吞吐量。

Conclusion: 通过 RE-CAM，内容可寻址内存 (CAM) 的读出功能被显著提升，使其在相似性搜索等应用中更有效率并减少数据传输需求。这一方法为 CAM 的应用开辟了新的可能性，并显示出在大数据计算领域应用CAM的优势。

Abstract: Similarity search is essential to many applications but incurs massive data movement when executed on conventional architectures. Content-addressable memory (CAM) is a promising hardware substrate, as it allows similarity search directly in the CAM array, minimizing data movement and offering both low latency and high energy efficiency. However, conventional CAM array readout produces only binary match results, discarding quantitative similarity information that could be further exploited. Extracting this information requires interpreting the CAM array output voltage that evolves during a CAM search, making the sensing timestamp a crucial design variable alongside the reference voltage and decoding parameter. In this work, we propose RE-CAM, a readout enhancement framework for CAM that is agnostic to memory technology and application, enabling CAM array readout to approximate linear and nonlinear functions with one CAM search. By reusing the comparators to sense the time-varying output voltage multiple times, jointly optimizing the three design variables under system constraints and hardware nonidealities, and co-designing the optimization algorithm and reconfigurable hardware, RE-CAM achieves accurate approximation with low energy and area overhead without introducing a readout throughput bottleneck. Across linear, piecewise-linear, sigmoid, and exponential functions, RE-CAM reduces normalized root mean square error by 10.89% to 24.34% for general approximation and 29.43% to 46.73% when exploiting dataset distribution, compared to a shared uniform-reference ADC baseline with the same comparison budget. Relative to the same baseline, RE-CAM also achieves $8\times$ higher search throughput while reducing energy per search and area by 47% and 39%, respectively.

</details>


### [247] [ReLay: Bidirectional Layout-Code Translation and Library Evolution for Reusable and Process-Portable Analog Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25328)
*Minxing Chu,Quan Cheng,Anhang Li,Martins Atilla,Junyi Luo,Xinting Jiang,Ruichen Qi,Mehdi Saligane*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ReLay 是一种双向布局代码框架，能够从现有布局中恢复生成器程序，增强设计重用性和跨工艺设计移植能力。ReLay 首次引入基准测试集，并展示了其在新布局生成中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模拟布局生成器覆盖范围有限，因为它们依赖于手工编写的库，而设计师的知识嵌在全面布局的几何结构中，难以捕捉到可重用的生成器库中。

Method: ReLay 使用了一种双向布局代码框架，通过识别现有布局中的重复构造并将其提升为库函数，从而使得生成器的库随着布局的积累而进化。

Result: ReLay 引入了首个布局代码基准测试集，包含来自27个电路族的1,050个手绘布局和三个开源PDK中，不同的电路层次结构。通过ReLay学习到的函数占了新布局构建调用的53-66%，并减少了64-77%的API成本，同时保持了同等的完成率和电气质量。

Conclusion: ReLay 成功地推广了现有布局的重用并实现了跨工艺设计移植，提供了比直接几何生成更高效的布局解决方案，对电路设计领域具有重要意义。

Abstract: Analog layout generators provide a programmable interface for design reuse and process migration, but their coverage is limited by handcrafted libraries. The designer knowledge embedded in complete layouts remains implicit in their geometry, making it difficult to capture in reusable generator libraries. We present ReLay, a bidirectional layout-code framework that recovers generator programs from existing layouts, enabling cross-PDK portability and parametric resizing of existing designs. Constructions that recur across recovered programs are promoted to library functions, so the library evolves as layouts accumulate. We also introduce the first layout-code benchmark: 1,050 hand-drawn layouts from 27 circuit families across three open-source PDKs, ranging from primitive cells to full chips. Learned functions account for 53-66% of layout-building calls in held-out layouts, demonstrating reuse beyond the learning corpus. Frontier LLM agents use ReLay to produce new layouts at 64-77% lower API cost per completed task than direct geometry generation, with comparable completion rates and electrical quality.

</details>


### [248] [FPGA-GEN: Target-Aware RTL Generation via HLS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25351)
*Luke Ztz Hu,Zhihan Xiao,Zhe Zhao,John Imoleayo Adebisi,Han Gao,Linxiao Yan,Songping Mai*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FPGA-GEN 通过任务合同和工具反馈循环，在编译后生成适用于 FPGA 的高级综合（HLS）流程中实现候选级别的决策，显著提高 HLS 设计的成功率和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 HLS 流程在编译层面成功并不意味着编译后的 FPGA 硬件也能正确高效运行。FPGA-GEN 的目标是通过严格的规则和反馈机制提高 HLS 设计的质量。

Method: FPGA-GEN 使用了任务合同和带有反馈限制的工具循环来对候选设计进行评估和修复。

Result: 在 HLS-Eval 上，5 个共享上下文输出每个任务达到了 95.8% 的公共测试台通过率。在累计转换活动中，在不同仿真、Vivado 综合与布线以及相同 RTL 证据下，FPGA-GEN 批准了 97.2% 的 HLS-LeVeri 设计。在焦点 TensorCore 谏议中，选定的候选设计节约了 73.7% 的 CSynth 循环周期和 14.6% 的 LUT，同时增加了 26.7% 的 DSP 资源，并通过了开发仿真。

Conclusion: FPGA-GEN 通过严格的规则和反馈机制显著提高了 HLS 设计的运行效率和成功率，特别是在 TensorCore 筛选上的表现更为突出。

Abstract: Successful C/C++ compilation does not guarantee correct or efficient FPGA hardware after high-level synthesis (HLS). FPGA-GEN is an agentic HLS-C generation workflow that encodes specification, readiness, selection, repair, and admission rules in explicit task contracts and bounded tool-feedback loops for candidate-level decisions. On HLS-Eval, five shared-context outputs per task yield 407/425 (95.8%) public-testbench passes. In a cumulative conversion campaign, FPGA-GEN admits 104/107 (97.2%) HLS-LeVeri designs under held-out co-simulation, Vivado synthesis/fit, and same-RTL evidence. In focused TensorCore screening, selected candidate 3 reports 73.7% fewer CSynth cycles and 14.6% fewer LUTs than generated/composed C0, with 26.7% more DSPs, and passes development CoSim.

</details>


### [249] [SGLayout: Schema-Guided Closed-Loop Refinement for LLM-Driven Parameterized Layout Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25410)
*Siyang Chen,Yuan Pu,Yuhan Qin,Zhaotan Lin,Siyuan Liang,Zhuolun He,Lining Zhang,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: SGLayout是一个通过结构化方案机制自我优化的框架，它在布局生成中取得了显著效果，能够将几何领域的评价结果转化为可执行的代码层面修正，从而提高布局的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型（LLMs）在布局生成中缺乏空间感知，导致生成的布局质量不可靠，且无法直接将几何领域的评价结果转化为代码层面的实际改进。

Method: SGLayout使用结构化方案，通过代码执行捕捉每个代码实例与其几何属性之间的对应关系，实现代码空间与几何空间之间的双向联系。它通过定量结构检查、视觉语言评估和DRC规则检查三大维度进行评价，并将发现的违规行为追溯到具体的代码目标进行精确修复。

Result: 在112个参数化布局生成任务的基准测试中，使用GDSFactory进行验证，SGLayout的通过率达到了76.79%，而最强基线的通过率为50.89%。

Conclusion: SGLayout通过结构化方案和闭合循环的过程有效解决了布局生成中的问题，显著提高了布局的质量。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong potential in parameterized layout generation via tool-augmented code synthesis. However, LLMs lack spatial awareness, making one-shot generation unreliable. While layout quality can be evaluated with established techniques, a critical gap remains: geometric-domain evaluation outcomes cannot be directly translated into actionable code-level fixes, trapping iterative refinement in blind refactoring. We propose SGLayout, a self-refining framework that closes this gap via a schema-guided mechanism. Upon code execution, a structured schema captures the correspondence between each code-level instance and its geometric attributes, establishing a bidirectional link between code space and geometry space. Emulating engineer review practices, SGLayout evaluates layouts along two complementary dimensions: intent compliance through quantitative structural checking and qualitative vision-language assessment, and rule compliance through DRC. All identified violations are then back-projected through the schema onto precise code targets, enabling surgical repair. This closed-loop process iterates until layout convergence. On a curated benchmark of 112 parameterized layout generation tasks using GDSFactory, SGLayout achieves a 76.79% pass rate, compared with 50.89% for the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [250] [BOOMerang: Autonomous Translation from RTL to Microarchitectural Simulators and Back](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25438)
*Ferran Hermida-Rivera,Jack Toubes,Angela Cui,Chengyi Lux Zhang,Sagar Karandikar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建和评估自动化循环来连接高层次仿真与RTL实现，显著提升了处理器性能，展示了从高层次仿真到RTL的自动化转换路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的高仿真器尽管提高了研究效率，但缺乏对硬件设计可行性的详细反馈，且难以保持与真实硬件性能的同步。直接从仿真代码生成高质量的RTL更是挑战。

Method: 研究人员设计并评估了自动化循环来自动生成准确的高层次模型（从RTL实现到gem5仿真器），并在高仿真器中实现新的微架构功能，再自动生成对应的RTL。

Result: 实现了自动化从BOOM核心rtl到gem5模拟器，生成的模拟器IPC误差在3%以内，用2天时间达到了工业标准。将自动化生成的72个新功能从高仿真器转换到rtl，保持了低IPC误差。最终，结合所有转换的优化在SPECint 2017中提升了25%的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究发展了自动化的工具链，实现了从高层次仿真到RTL的双向自动化转换，显著提升了处理器性能，对未来相关研究与实际应用具有重要价值。

Abstract: High-level simulators like gem5 are often used to test and demonstrate novel architectural techniques. While these simulators provide high research productivity, they generally lack the means to provide detailed feedback on the realizability of a hardware design. Furthermore, these simulators can frequently fall out-of-sync with the detailed performance characteristics of real hardware designs, which is usually the responsibility of dedicated engineers/teams in industry. While RTL implementation can address these challenges, it can be even more difficult to translate simulator code to high-quality RTL by hand without a large team.
  In this work, we architect and evaluate agentic loops that automate both parts of the RTL vs. high-level simulator workflow that is critical to high-quality architecture research. Our loops (1) automatically generate accurate high-level models of RTL implementations of CPU designs (BOOM) in a battle-tested microarchitecture simulation framework (gem5) and (2) automatically translate new microarchitectural features implemented in the high-level simulator back to RTL faithfully.
  Targeting the first loop at autonomously building a gem5 model for the Berkeley Out-of-Order Machine (BOOM) core, we generate a simulator with IPC within 3% of the RTL, meeting targets pursued for simulators in industry, in only 2 days. We use our second loop to automatically translate 72 features from this aligned gem5 simulator into RTL in the BOOM core, while maintaining its low IPC error. Combining translated optimizations together yields a 25% performance improvement for the BOOM core RTL implementation in SPECint 2017, demonstrating that having these loops for moving between high-level simulation and RTL enables actual improvement of processor performance. The agentic loops we developed and the simulators and RTL we generated will be available in open-source.

</details>


### [251] [FlashX: Scaling Robotic Experience Reuse for Embodied Agents with CBA-Based NAND Flash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25580)
*Yuseon Choi,Sanghyeon Park,Eunjong Lee,Hanseo Lee,Sangjin Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FlashX的新架构，通过利用CMOS直接绑定到阵列（CBA）的NAND闪存特性，实现了在机体内生代理中大规模经验重用。该架构通过并行pSLC密钥搜索和交错的计算-闪存(CIF)引擎保持感应吞吐量，同时保留密集的TLC潜在存储，并采用本体感知聚类进一步降低搜索开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在处理未见过的环境时难以泛化，这限制了任务准确性的同时也限制了推理效率，而传统的加速技术会以牺牲准确性为代价。为此，本文旨在开发一种解决方案，既提升任务准确性又提高推理效率。

Method: 本文提出了一种称为FlashX的架构，结合了CMOS直接绑定到阵列（CBA）的高内部带宽特性，实现了并行pSLC密钥搜索，采用交错的计算-闪存（CIF）引擎以保持高吞吐量，同时使用密集的TLC潜在存储，并利用本体感知聚类技术减少搜索开销。

Result: 在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）和世界行动模型（WAM）的工作负载中，评估表明，使用FlashX架构可以带来高达12.24%和7.43%的任务成功率改善，分别对应的密集配置和计算有效配置。相比于前一种最强大的NAND搜索架构，FlashX在pSLC感应下实现了2.57倍的搜索吞吐量提升，并通过聚类进一步减少了9.7倍的搜索量，这提供了1.29至3.95倍的端到端加速和1.12到2.34倍的能量效率改善。

Conclusion: 本文提出的FlashX解决方案通过在体内生代理人中实施大规模的经验重用，成功实现了在提高任务准确性和推理效率之间的平衡，同时也通过硬件优化和聚类技术降低了搜索开销，展示了在有限资源环境下的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Embodied agents are emerging as a promising paradigm for complex robotic tasks, leveraging Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and World Action Models (WAMs) to generate actions through Diffusion Transformer (DiT)-based iterative denoising. However, existing models often struggle to generalize to out-of-distribution (OOD) environments. We observe that retrieving successful experiences and using them to warm-start denoising enables agents to better adapt to unfamiliar situations while reducing computational overhead. This creates a new opportunity to improve task accuracy and inference efficiency simultaneously, in contrast to conventional acceleration techniques that trade accuracy for computation. However, realizing this opportunity requires scaling experience banks, introducing substantial storage and search overhead that challenges the capacity, bandwidth, and power constraints of edge systems.
  We propose FlashX, a CMOS Directly Bonded to Array (CBA)-based NAND flash architecture for scalable experience reuse in embodied agents. FlashX exploits CBA's high internal bandwidth for parallel pSLC key search, employing interleaved Compute-in-Flash (CIF) engines to sustain sensing throughput while retaining dense TLC latent storage. Proprioception-aware clustering further reduces search overhead and enables incremental experience acquisition without global reclustering. Evaluations across VLA and WAM workloads demonstrate up to 12.24\%p and 7.43\%p improvements in task success under dense and compute-efficient configurations, respectively. FlashX achieves 2.57$\times$ higher search throughput than the strongest prior NAND search architecture under pSLC sensing, with a further 9.7$\times$ search reduction through clustering. This delivers 1.29--3.95$\times$ end-to-end speedup and 1.12--2.34$\times$ energy efficiency improvement over SSD-based retrieval.

</details>


### [252] [Splice-Core: Low-Order-Bit-Splicing GEMM for Highly Energy-Efficient AI Inference Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25617)
*Prabhu Vellaisamy,Hsien-Fu Hung,Andrew Kim,Shawn Blanton,John Paul Shen*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Splice-Core是一种结合了一元和二元操作的特殊矩阵乘法单元，旨在减少低阶积累延迟，同时保持高阶激活位的值依赖执行。通过两种新颖的处理单元（Splice-BP和Splice-LUT）实现了低能耗，并且在一些测试工作负载中提高了吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高神经网络和语言模型训练中的矩阵乘法效率，特别是针对低精度计算（如INT4），以减少能耗并提高性能。

Method: 设计了两种新的处理单元（PE）——Splice-BP和Splice-LUT，分别基于位平面和查找表的方法进行优化。

Result: 与二进制 systolic阵列相比，Splice-BP 和 Splice-LUT 分别在26个INT4卷积神经网络和大型语言模型工作负载中减少了77.4%和76.4%的几何平均单位能量，并且Splice-LUT还提高了44.4%的几何平均单位吞吐量和减少了6.13倍的几何平均能量延时积。对于W8A8卷积和专家投影工作负载，Splice-BP和Splice-LUT分别减少了77.3%-88.1%的单位能量。

Conclusion: Splice-BP和Splice-LUT在多个工作负载中显示出显著的能效和性能优势，同时减少了面积使用。

Abstract: Splice-Core is a temporal-unary/binary (tub) hybrid general matrix multiplication (GEMM) unit that minimizes low-order accumulation latency, while retaining value-dependent execution for high-order activation bits. We present two novel processing element (PE) variants: (i) Splice-BP utilizing bit planes, and (ii) Splice-LUT, which selects a shared weight multiple in a single cycle. Compared to a binary systolic array, Splice-BP/Splice-LUT achieve 77.4%/76.4% geometric-mean tile energy reductions, respectively, across 26 INT4 convolutional neural network (CNN) and large language model (LLM) workloads in 45nm. Splice-LUT further increases geometric-mean tile throughput by 44.4% and reduces geometric-mean energy-delay product (EDP) by 6.13x across the 26 configurations. Across the evaluated W8A8 convolution and expert-projection workloads, Splice-BP and Splice-LUT reduce tile energy by 77.3-88.1%. Post-place-and-route INT4 yields 14%/9% smaller area for Splice-BP/Splice-LUT, compared to the systolic array.

</details>


### [253] [GRODE-RTL: Source-Mapped Dependency Views for LLM-Based RTL Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25661)
*Jingyi Zhou,Yi Liu,Bin Sun,Hongji Zhang,Dongsheng Zuo,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: GRODE-RTL 提出了一种以锚点为中心的表示法，通过从网表中提取单时钟阶段的扇入锥，将网表节点回映到可编辑的源代码行，并组织成紧凑的跳分区依赖视图，以指导模型进行 RTL 故障修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 RTL 作为扁平化的 token 序列表示模糊了硬件数据流，而完全暴露合成图会导致模型难以处理细节丰富的元数据，GRODE-RTL 提供了一种解决方案。

Method: GRODE-RTL 通过分析网表，抽取单时钟周期的扇入锥，并将网表节点回映到源代码实现，采用跳分区依赖视图来组织信息，从而帮助语言模型（LLMs）更好地进行故障修复。
