Result: 与基于源代码级别的探索相比，GRODE-RTL 在三种 LLM 上的 pass@1 指标分别提高了 16.7%、26.7% 和 20.0%，特别是对于难以检索的远距离和间接锚定点的部分。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，选择性的依赖组织而不是全面的图曝光是实现有效的 RTL 修复的关键。

Abstract: Large Language Models show great potential for automated Register-Transfer Level repair, yet they struggle with non-local faults where root causes and failing outputs reside far apart in source code. Treating RTL as a flat token sequence obscures hardware dataflows, whereas exposing complete synthesized graphs overwhelms the model with fine-grained metadata. To resolve this representation dilemma, we propose GRODE-RTL, an anchor-centric representation that extracts the single-clock-stage fan-in cone of a suspect register from the netlist, maps netlist nodes back to editable source lines, and organizes them into a compact hop-partitioned dependency view. By prioritizing direct drivers and folding distant hops, GRODE-RTL guides standard tool-using agents without exposing ground-truth hints or oracle feedback. We evaluate GRODE-RTL across 30 formally validated non-local faults using three LLMs (DeepSeek-V4-Flash, GLM-5.2, and MiniMax-M3) alongside a controlled six-configuration ablation study. GRODE-RTL improves pass@1 over source-level exploration by 16.7% on DeepSeek, by 26.7% on GLM-5.2, and by 20.0% on MiniMax-M3, with consistent gains on the hard-to-retrieve far-distance and indirect-anchor subsets of all three models. Our controls isolate structural relevance from generic context expansion, proving that selective dependency organization rather than full graph exposure is key to effective RTL repair.

</details>


### [254] [Mitigating Row Buffer Contention Through Multiple Row Buffer (MRB) DRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25714)
*K Chitra,Arjun Dey,Aryabartta Sahu,Minesh Patel*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了引入每个银行中多个行缓冲器（MRB）的DRAM芯片设计，通过使用一个主要的全局行缓冲器和多个次要的SRAM行缓冲器，来减少由不同行列请求引发的行缓冲器冲突，从而提高性能并降低能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的DRAM芯片存在行缓冲器冲突问题，这会影响到性能和能耗。为了解决这一问题，作者提出了一种新的DRAM设计——多行缓冲器（MRB-DRAM），它利用一个主要的全局行缓冲器和多个次要的SRAM行缓冲器来改善性能和减少冲突。

Method: 该方法主要是将 DRAM 芯片设计优化，引入了多行缓冲器架构。主要策略包括：利用一个主要的全局行缓冲器和多个次要的 SRAM 行缓冲器，允许每个银行同时处理多个行请求，从而减少行缓冲器冲突。同时，通过实验证明了该设计的有效性。

Result: 实验结果显示，与传统的 DDR4 基准相比，每银行拥有四个次要 SRAM 行缓冲器的 MRB-DRAM 设计在单核和多核系统中的性能分别提高了 29.7% 和 24%，并且仅带来了适度的面积开销 (2%)。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 MRB-DRAM 设计能够显著减少行缓冲器冲突，并通过减小关键缓冲器冲突来提升 DRAM 性能，同时增加的面积开销相对较小，为实际应用提供了有效技术支持。

Abstract: DRAM chips contain multiple banks to handle sev- eral memory requests in parallel. However, requests to different rows of the same bank (e.g., from different cores or workload access patterns) interfere with each other by contending for a single shared row buffer. This introduces row buffer conflicts, which incur performance and energy overheads. In this work, we reduce row buffer conflicts caused by interference by introducing multiple row buffers in each bank. We propose multiple row buffer (MRB) DRAM, which employs a primary global row buffer along with multiple secondary SRAM row buffers in the DRAM chip. These secondary SRAM row buffers act as a cache that enables each bank to serve requests to multiple rows in parallel, thereby improving performance and reducing row buffer conflicts. Our evaluation shows that MRB-DRAM with four SRBs per bank improves performance by an average of 29.7%, 24% in singlecore, multicore systems, respectively, compared to a conventional DDR4 baseline with a modest area overhead of 2%.

</details>


### [255] [CRAFT: Analog Circuit Design by Reasoning LLM Agents from Pre-Computed Lookup Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25908)
*Hyunjun Park,Seungyub Han,Jason Jaesung Lee,Kahyun Kim,Soohyun Kim,Inwoo Nam,Sunmin Kim,Wooyoung Choi,Junhyung Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CRAFT 使用大型语言模型 (LLM) 设计者通过计算生成电路尺寸，而非传统方法依赖 SPICE 仿真。在多个测试电路中，CRAFT 用时更短且满足所有规格和约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提高模拟电路设计效率，减少依赖复杂仿真工具的时间，并实现自动化设计过程。

Method: 开发了CRAFT 流程，利用 LLM 进行设计计算，而非 SPICE 仿真。通过 DeX 服务使用预计算的查找表来提供器件特性和整个电路网络的静态工作点及小信号响应。

Result: 在 28nm 工艺测试中，CRAFT 满足所有规格和约束，在多测试电路中比传统方法更高效。

Conclusion: CRAFT 流程展示了 LLM 设计可替代人工设计和 SPICE 仿真，提高设计效率并确保电路正确性。

Abstract: Automated analog circuit sizing methods, from black-box optimizers to recent large language model (LLM) agents, typically search the design space with a SPICE simulator in the loop, whereas an experienced designer works out a design by hand analysis. This paper presents CRAFT, a flow in which an LLM agent designs a given topology by calculation, as a designer does, and without an initial sizing. The agent works only through DeX, a service that uses pre-computed lookup tables to provide device characteristics and to solve the whole netlist for its operating point and small-signal response. It thus designs without SPICE, and every final design is verified independently in HSPICE. We test CRAFT on four circuits in a commercial 28 nm process; the largest is a 37-transistor three-stage amplifier with 53 design variables, 20 specifications and 74 saturation constraints. With Claude Opus 5.5, CRAFT meets every specification and constraint in all 20 runs, five per circuit. On that amplifier, with DeX in place of HSPICE and an identical request for both, the same agent passes in 10 of 10 runs instead of 6 of 10 within an hour and is 2.0x faster at the median.

</details>


### [256] [Kameleo: In-Situ Activation-Precision Selection for Bit-Serial LLM Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25971)
*Robin Geens,Thomas Debelle,Marian Verhelst,Chao Fang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Kameleo 是一种能在运行时根据处理的数据选择激活精度的加速器，通过比较半精度和全精度 GEMM 的输出来优化计算，从而减少了填充周期并保持了模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的全局困惑度优化方法虽然有效，但因数据集不同导致的精度选择结果会变差，Kameleo 提出通过局部近似值在运行时选择激活精度的方法，以减少计算资源占用并保持模型性能。

Method: Kameleo 通过在运行时对每个 GEMM 进行两次迭代，设置其在半精度和全精度之间的精度选择，利用半精度路径在全精度计算中的中间状态，仅用两遍前几令牌的处理即可决定每种 GEMM 的精度。

Result: 实验结果显示，Kameleo 相较于在评价数据上校准的配置，能够在四款大型语言模型和七种数据集上维持 1% 的困惑度预算，同时仅增加 2.11-2.34 倍于填充周期的数量，硬件开销仅增加 3.7%。

Conclusion: Kameleo 的方法为半精度 mantissa 在实际部署中的应用提供了可能，相比传统方法，能在无需为每个模型和工作负载进行离线校准的场景下保持较高的模型质量。

Abstract: Weight-only quantization keeps the activations of large language models (LLMs) in a higher-precision format, pushing the on-chip inference cost to the activation arithmetic. Bit-serial, variable-length accelerators offer a promising path to reduce this cost by lowering activation precision while preserving model quality. They process only the mantissa bits that each layer needs. The length has to be chosen per layer and operator, and today it is chosen by offline calibration: a search driven by the perplexity of the full model on a representative dataset. The search is repeated for every model and workload, and its result degrades silently on other data. This paper presents Kameleo, an accelerator that selects the activation precision at runtime, on the accelerator, from the data it is processing. Kameleo replaces the global perplexity by a local proxy that compares the output of each GEMM at reduced and at full precision. It exploits the fact that a bit-serial datapath produces this comparison almost for free, because the reduced-precision result is an intermediate state of the full-precision computation. Two passes over the first tokens of a workload set the precision of every GEMM. On four LLMs and seven datasets, Kameleo stays within a 1% perplexity budget at a 2.11-2.34x reduction of prefill cycles on the matrix multiplication unit (MXU), close to the reduction of configurations calibrated offline on the evaluation data itself. Calibrated configurations transferred to other data, in contrast, often violate the budget. The selection hardware adds only 3.7% to the accelerator area. Kameleo thereby makes variable-length mantissas feasible in real deployments, where offline calibration for every model and workload is not.

</details>


### [257] [Scalable fault vulnerability analysis for ReRAM-based process-in-memory CNN accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26307)
*Aniseh Dorostkar,Hamed Farbeh,Hamid R. Zarandi,Meysam Sharifzadeh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种快速、可扩展且分析性的框架，用于量化基于ReRAM的PIM加速器上部署的CNN故障脆弱性，无需耗时的模拟即可在分钟到小时的数量级内完成大尺度CNN的快速准确的脆弱性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了克服故障注入方法的局限性，如过度耗时和计算成本高，为边缘和安全关键应用设计故障容忍的加速器，开发了一种快速分析CNN故障脆弱性的框架。

Method: 该方法在权重、核和层的不同粒度级别上定量故障脆弱性，通过比较提出的脆弱性评分（VS）指标与故障注入模拟性能指标，证明了方法的有效性。

Result: 与耗时的故障注入相比，该方法达到了约10^4到10^5倍的加速，能够在几分钟到几小时内对大规模CNN进行快速准确的脆弱性分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为基于ReRAM的PIM加速器上的CNN设计提供了有效的故障容忍度分析工具。

Abstract: Resistive random-access memory (ReRAM)-based processing-in-memory (PIM) accelerators offer a highly efficient platform for parallel matrix-vector multiplications (VMM) in neural networks, leveraging analog computation, high density, low leakage, and non-volatility. However, their susceptibility to permanent faults, such as stuck-at-high (\textit{SaH}) and stuck-at-low (\textit{SaL}) resistance states, permanently corrupts the convolutional neural networks (CNNs) weights mapped onto ReRAM cell conductance values, thereby degrading inference accuracy and leading to system unreliability in safety-critical applications. Fault injection is a commonly used technique to assess the vulnerability of neural networks by systematically injecting faults into individual parameters and measuring the resulting accuracy degradation. Although this method is accurate, it is prohibitively time-consuming and computationally expensive, especially for large-scale CNNs. In this paper, we propose a fast, scalable, and analytical framework to quantify the fault vulnerability of convolutional neural networks deployed on ReRAM-based PIM accelerators. This method quantifies vulnerability at different granularities, including weights, kernels, and layers, without the need for exhaustive simulation, enabling the design of fault-tolerant accelerators for edge and safety-critical applications. The comparison between the results of the proposed vulnerability score (VS) metrics with performance metric of CNNs captured with fault injection simulations confirms the effectiveness of our proposed method. Unlike exhaustive fault injection, which requires days or weeks, whereas the proposed approach achieves a speedup of about $10^4\times-10^5\times$, enabling fast and accurate vulnerability analysis for large-scale CNNs within only a few minutes or hours.

</details>


### [258] [MaskPE: One Mask, Two Roles: Weight Placement and MAC Gating in Systolic Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26432)
*Shu-Lin Shih,Chandra Sekhar Mummidi,Aamir Gujral Elhaissouni,Sandip Kundu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: MaskPE通过使用位置掩码来引导非零权重并关闭零权重处理元件的算术寄存器，在保持 systolic 数据流的同时减少了动态功耗，并通过减少压缩权重存储和降低执行能耗来提高能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的不规则修剪虽然减少了存储成本，但增加了执行成本。作者提出MaskPE架构，旨在降低功耗并保留 systolic 数据流，特别是在稀疏权重的情况下。

Method: MaskPE架构利用位置掩码在预加载时引导非零权重，并在执行期间通过时钟门控零权重处理元件的算术寄存器来降低动态功耗。该方法针对50%稀疏度进行了优化，并通过RTL验证和门级仿真进行了评估。

Result: 在50%稀疏度下，MaskPE减少了34.9%的压缩权重存储空间，包括元数据开销。硬件逻辑验证确认了精确执行，而门级仿真则展示了在七种大型语言模型投影样本中执行能耗降低了12.5%至14.4%。

Conclusion: MaskPE架构成功地减少了稀疏权重环境下的压缩权重存储并降低了执行能耗，保持了 systolic 数据流，展示了在实际应用中的能效优势。

Abstract: Unstructured pruning reduces weight storage but does not inherently reduce execution cost in conventional weight-stationary systolic arrays. Packed nonzero weights must be restored to fixed processing-element positions, while zero-weight positions must preserve data forwarding. This paper presents MaskPE, a mask-directed systolic architecture that uses positional masks to steer nonzero weights during preload and clock-gate arithmetic registers in zero-weight processing elements during execution, reducing dynamic power while preserving systolic dataflow. At 50% sparsity, MaskPE reduces compressed weight storage by 34.9%, including metadata overhead. RTL validation confirms exact execution with no additional steady-state tile stalls, while gate-level simulations demonstrate 12.5--14.4% lower execution energy across samples of seven LLM projections.

</details>


### [259] [FoldSim: Scaling Cycle-Exact RTL Simulation with Hierarchical Folding and Static Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26472)
*Chengxuan Wang,Nikhil Rout,Yang-gon Kim,Jacob Leigh,Yufan Du,Blaise Tine*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: FoldSim 使用层次折叠技术在单一 FPGA 上进行超大规模 RTL 设计的时钟精确仿真，通过复用逻辑和使用外部内存，达到了显著的资源节省和加速效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决传统基于 CPU 和 GPU 的RTL 模拟器对大规模设计的执行速度瓶颈，同时利用 FPGA 的高吞吐量特性，提出了一种新的自动化框架 FoldSim。

Method: 利用层次折叠技术，FoldSim 将实例映射到共享逻辑中，通过静态调度编译器协调执行，平衡了资源利用和仿真时间。

Result: FoldSim 在单个 AMD Alveo V80 FPGA 上成功对一个包含 8 块 SM 的 GPU 和一个 1024 节点的数据中心进行了仿真，显示了显著的硬件资源节省和加速效果。

Conclusion: FoldSim 为大规模 RTL 设计的快速、准确仿真提供了一种有效方法，展示了其在硬件资源管理和效率提升方面的潜力。

Abstract: RTL simulation is essential for hardware design and verification, yet its execution time grows rapidly with design scale and complexity. CPU- and GPU-based simulators exploit parallelism to accelerate RTL evaluation, but achieving high simulation throughput for large designs remains challenging. FPGA-based simulators offer higher throughput by mapping RTL computations directly onto hardware, but their scalability is constrained by FPGA resource capacity.
  We present FoldSim, an automated framework that enables cycle-exact simulation of large RTL designs on a single FPGA through hierarchical folding. FoldSim maps instances across the design hierarchy onto shared logic while maintaining their independent state. Given foldable module classes, its compiler constructs a static schedule that coordinates their execution, reducing dynamic synchronization overhead and making state accesses predictable. FoldSim backs state that exceeds on-chip capacity with external memory and brings it to the shared circuits as needed. A configurable DRAM model separates target-memory timing from host delays. The compiler explores replica allocations and state placement to balance resource savings against simulation time.
  On one AMD Alveo V80, FoldSim runs an 8-SM GPU and a 1,024-node Rocket datacenter with 4,096 processor cores. The unfolded implementations require approximately 16x and 200x as many LUTs as FoldSim's, respectively. The datacenter keeps about 1.9 GB of state in HBM, while a 4-SM GPU completes a 1024x1024 GEMM in 4.24 seconds, versus 26 hours in software RTL simulation.

</details>


### [260] [From Seed to BLOOM: Autonomously Growing a Better Out-of-Order Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26517)
*Angela Cui,Ferran Hermida-Rivera,Jack Toubes,Jim Fang,Chengyi Lux Zhang,Borivoje Nikolić,Christopher W. Fletcher,Sagar Karandikar*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: MiracleGro 是一个自动化的基于代理的人工智能系统，它能够分析 RTL 行为，识别性能瓶颈，并将架构想法转化为 RTL 实现，从而在 BOOM 中显著提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的性能优化过程依赖于大量的人类工程资源，对于小团队来说，这需要高昂的成本和时间。利用大型语言模型（LLM）代理，可以加速这一过程。

Method: 研究团队构建了一个名为 MiracleGro 的系统，该系统能够自动分析 RTL 细节，识别并解决性能瓶颈，并将架构概念实施为 RTL 代码。MiracleGro 针对的是 Berkeley Out-of-Order Machine (BOOM) 芯片，通过考虑精确的时钟周期性能、面积和关键路径时长，自动生成提高了 BOOM 性能的全新设计。

Result: 实验结果表明，与基准设计相比，MiracleGro 在 SPEC CPU2017 整数基准测试中的 IPC 速度提升了 85%，频率提高了 27%，实现了 2.34 倍的全程序执行时间加速。这显示出利用先进模型改进基于 RTL 的硬件设计平台在硬件设计领域中的巨大潜力。

Conclusion: 这项工作展示了一种新的方法来利用大型语言模型代理自动优化硬件设计，这将显著提高微体系结构研究在实际实施中的效率和效果。

Abstract: Improving microprocessor performance while meeting ASIC implementation constraints like area and frequency is a complex and labor-intensive task that requires expertise across computer architecture, hardware design, and performance analysis and profiling. While large teams in industry can afford the engineering resources to target this problem across performance modeling, RTL design, and ASIC implementations, small teams of researchers and engineers cannot take on this huge engineering burden while keeping up with the pace of advancement in processor microarchitecture. Recent advancements in large language model (LLM) agents present a promising substrate to accelerate this traditionally human-intensive process.
  In this work, we build and evaluate MiracleGro, an agentic AI-based system that, without human intervention, analyzes cycle-level RTL behavior and synthesis results, identifies performance bottlenecks, and translates architectural ideas into RTL implementation in a leading open-source out-of-order superscalar application core, the Berkeley Out-of-Order Machine (BOOM). Taking into account cycle-exact performance, area, and critical path, MiracleGro automatically produces improved BOOM designs that drastically increase the human-optimized BOOM's performance on substantial workload suites such as SPEC CPU2017 with full reference inputs.
  MiracleGro achieves an 85% IPC speedup and a frequency improvement of 27% in SkyWater 130nm, resulting in a 2.34x wall-clock time speedup on the full SPEC CPU2017 intspeed benchmarks (30 trillion instructions). This result demonstrates the enormous opportunity to harness frontier models to improve state-of-the-art RTL-driven platforms for implementation-grounded microarchitecture research across hardware designs.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Held While Waiting: What LLM Agent Sessions Reserve on Serverless and Agent Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.26480)
*Mekhala Hithyshini Sreenivasa*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: LLM代理几乎将其所有时间用于等待模型响应。这导致了环境资源的浪费，并提出了两种解决方案：通过客户端立即关闭不再需要的会话，以及通过平台允许一个环境处理多个等待中的会话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 减少因会话长时间保留环境导致的成本和资源浪费。

Method: 通过分析实际使用和模拟数据，以及改进测量方法来识别问题根源，并提出相应的解决方案。

Result: 实证模拟表明，通过合并等待中的会话至单个环境可以显著减少所需的环境数，延迟的回复时间也大幅减少。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的服务模型，该模型支持等待中的会话共享单一环境，旨在提高资源利用效率并降低成本。

Abstract: LLM agents spend nearly all their time waiting for the model. On Amazon Bedrock AgentCore, our agents waited a median of 2.7 seconds for each model reply, then computed for an estimated 5 to 40 milliseconds. AgentCore does not charge for CPU during the wait. But it reserves a separate environment, a virtual machine with its own memory, for every conversation, and keeps it reserved until the conversation is closed. So 1,000 conversations reserve 1,000 environments to do the work of 2 to 15 processor cores. A service runs out of environments long before it runs out of processing power.
  The shortage has two causes, and each has a fix. An environment stays reserved after its conversation ends: in the customer-service benchmark we ran, a conversation lasts 33 seconds on average, but its environment is held for 15 minutes more unless the client closes it. And an environment serves only one conversation, which is mostly waiting: in our simulation, 64 conversations sharing one environment need 59 times fewer environments, and 99 of every 100 replies are delayed by at most 40 milliseconds.
  The milliseconds are an estimate because they are hard to measure: a stubbed model, a timer around the model call, and steps run back to back each gave a plausible number that was too low. We show how to avoid all three. Of the two fixes, closing needs only the client. Sharing needs the platform, and of the agent runtimes we surveyed only one allows it, in part. They keep conversations apart because an agent runs tools, and sometimes code, chosen by a model. The open problem is to keep them apart without reserving an environment for each while it waits.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [262] [Closing the Analyze-to-Design Gap: An Authorization-Bounded Agent for Self-Driving Laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25043)
*Yimeng Liu,Eric Isbrandt,Felix Katzenburg,Kevin P. Greenman,Han Hao,Yang Cao,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种架构，该架构在自驱动实验室（SDL）的设计-制造-测试-分析（DMTA）循环的分析-设计接口中集成了一个代理，使代理能够驱动测量选择和数据解释，同时确保状态变更操作的授权、验证和可追溯性。该架构在液相色谱-质谱（LC-MS）仪器上进行了实验，展示了代理如何在预定的人类定义的任务范围内提交测量作业、解释数据并确定合适的氧化剂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能（AI）代理在自驱动实验室（SDL）中的应用日益增多，这些系统需要具备在无人监督的情况下执行物理操作的能力，并且能够在需要时向人类求助的能力。本文的动机在于提供一个架构，使得SDL代理能够在保证操作合法性和可追溯性的前提下自主完成实验。

Method: 研究表明，这种新的架构在液相色谱-质谱（LC-MS）仪器上实现了自动化的实验任务选择、数据解释等主要功能，通过在实验中为代理分配任务，并确保其操作过程中的正当性和透明度。

Result: 在一项合成有机染料的氧化剂筛选实验中，代理能够独立选择测量任务、分析质谱和二极管阵列检测数据、识别失败的实验并生成氧化剂排名，在模拟实验环境下，还能够有效识别最高效的操作条件。

Conclusion: 这种自动化的实验代理框架展示了提升SDL自主性的一种可能路径，在确保操作安全性的同时，提高了实验操作的效率和准确性。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) agents are increasingly integrated into self-driving laboratories (SDLs) to plan experiments and interpret results. However, analytical reasoning alone does not make a laboratory autonomous: autonomy in an SDL is achievable only when the agent can be trusted to act on physical hardware unsupervised and knows when to defer to a human. Here, we present an architecture that integrates an agent at the Analyze-Design interface of the Design-Make-Test-Analyze (DMTA) cycle, enabling agent-driven measurement selection and data interpretation while ensuring that state-changing operations are authorized, validated, and auditable. We demonstrate this architecture on a liquid chromatography-mass spectrometry (LC-MS) instrument in an oxidant-screening campaign for synthesizing an organic dye. Within human-defined campaign limits, the agent submitted measurement jobs, interpreted mass spectrometry (MS) and diode-array detection (DAD) data, flagged failed experiments, and produced an oxidant ranking. This ranking matched that obtained from scripted peak integration, and organic chemists confirmed the agent's spectral interpretations and failure assignments. In a simulated environment that replays the archived LC-MS data, where the agent chose which wells to inject under varying injection limits, it measured the highest-yielding condition at about its seventh injection, against 14.5 on average for a random order, and identified it in every campaign with a limit of eight or more injections. An agent can therefore close the analysis-to-design gap while its actions stay bounded and verifiable, which is a step toward greater autonomy for SDLs.

</details>


### [263] [An LLM-Agentic Framework for Intelligent Work Order Management in Industrial Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25070)
*Nianjun Zhou,Dhaval Patel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为CodeReAct的AI驱动框架，旨在通过自然语言交互提升工业设备维护效率，通过AI代理技术自动分析人类指令、提取知识、总结资产状态并推荐工作单，同时预测工作单需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着工业设备维护的需求不断增加，传统的人工分析和处理方式已无法满足高效维护的需求。因此，该研究旨在通过引入自然语言处理和AI技术，实现更为智能和自动化的设备维护管理。

Method: 该方法采用了一种类似人类问题解决过程的思考-行动-观察框架（Thought-Action-Observation），并将工业数据标准化为业务对象（BOs），并通过API调用进行分析。具体来讲，行动环节生成可执行代码，替代传统手动编码。

Result: 通过案例研究，尤其是在制冷设备维护上的应用，展示了该框架能有效处理人类的口头指示，分析数据并提取有价值的信息，支持决策，减少数据科学家的参与。

Conclusion: 该研究的成功表明，通过自然语言处理和AI技术，可实现在工业设备维护领域的自动化和智能化，显著提高维护效率和决策质量。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven framework, CodeReAct agent, designed to enhance industrial equipment maintenance through natural language (NL) interactions. The framework employs an advanced AI agentic approach to interpret human instructions, providing knowledge extraction, asset status summarization, and work order recommendations. It identifies patterns and trends and predicts work orders by standardizing industrial data into business objects (BOs) and utilizing analytical function API calls. The AI agent operates through a Thought-Action-Observation process similar to human problem-solving, automating complex analytical tasks typically performed by data scientists. Notably, the Action phase generates executable code, replacing manual coding efforts.
  A case study focusing on chiller maintenance demonstrates the framework's capability to analyze multiple years of work orders, and alerts and anomalies are presented. The results indicate that the AI agent framework effectively processes human utterances to analyze data, extract valuable insights, and support informed decision-making, reducing the need for data scientist involvement. By recontextualizing NLP agent frameworks in industrial engineering, the approach enables autonomous function generation and task execution across a range of domain-specific maintenance scenarios, with chillers used as a representative case study.

</details>


### [264] [SailSearch: Agent-Assisted Discovery in Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25087)
*Dhriti Verma,Kanthi Sarpatwar,Vinicius Lima,Dzung Phan,Jayant Kalagnanam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SailSearch通过两个语言模型代理实现了一种由人类引导的算法研究工作流程，在Market Split和最大独立集（MIS）上取得了显著成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了自动化和加速算法研究过程，特别是在解决计算难度高且优化问题上。

Method: SailSearch采用了一种结合人类指导和算法自动生成的过程，利用两个语言模型代理处理问题，并通过随机算法和生成器在多个实例上进行测试。

Result: 对于Market Split，SailSearch找到了18个之前未解决的QOBLIB实例的CPU-only解决方案，并在八小时内解决了大部分基准实例。对于MIS，最佳解与最优解相比差5到15个顶点。

Conclusion: SailSearch工作流程展示了在无需额外硬件资源的情况下，通过有效利用算法和优化技术提高大规模问题求解效率的潜力。

Abstract: We present SailSearch, a human-guided workflow for algorithmic research with two language-model agents, and report results on Market Split and maximum independent set (MIS). For Market Split, our randomized algorithm searches across multiple lattice bases, dividing computation between enumeration and continued basis reduction. Across ten random seeds per instance, it found the first reported CPU-only solutions to 18 of 33 previously open QOBLIB instances, solving 130 to 133 of the 156 benchmark instances per seed with a 24-hour cutoff on two CPU cores. For MIS, our generator encodes planted XOR constraints as graphs with certified optima; on seven instances with 1,752 to 3,996 vertices, the best solutions returned by the tested solvers in eight-hour runs were 5 to 15 vertices below the optimum.

</details>


### [265] [BuggyMLE: Scaling Machine Learning Engineering Difficulty with Bug Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25088)
*John Wu,Rushi Qiang,Yuchen Zhuang,Guowang Li,Girish Narayanswamy,Max Xu,Sam Schmidgall,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Jimeng Sun,Daniel McDuff,Bo Dai,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个新的复杂度指标——代码复杂度指数(CCI)，并基于此开发了一个可扩展的框架BuggyMLE，用于注射代码突变到机器学习库中，从而构建了一个具有难度分级的MLE基准，同时训练了一个迁移学习模型来解决这些基准中的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习工程(MLE)基准仅限于单文件编辑，缺乏全面的代码仓库和shell访问权限，且常见的仓库级软件工程基准已趋于饱和。为了解决这些问题，作者提出了代码复杂度指数(CCI)，并使用CCI开发了能够生成注入代码突变的BuggyMLE框架，以构建具有难度分级的MLE基准，并训练了一个迁移学习模型。

Method: 作者首先提出了一种基于十个结构特征(覆盖代码仓库规模、依赖深度和补丁大小)的代码复杂度指数(CCI)，并通过十个开源代码基准预测了代码解决率的平均值。接着，基于CCI，作者构建了一个可扩展的BuggyMLE框架，该框架能在工作中的机器学习代码库中插入受控的突变，合成超过6,600个包含错误的机器学习工程库变体和近260K行注入代码，基于222个源仓库。此外，作者还创建了一个难度分级的MLE基准BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite，并生成了一个覆盖200个其他机器学习代码库的训练课程，从而对BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite模型进行了微调。

Result: 使用BuggyMLE，作者取得两个成果：一是构建了一个难度分级的MLE基准BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite，在14个评估模型中，最难的一级的可解决问题比例仅为最易一级的9%；二是通过在3个难度级别上生成训练课程，作者训练了一种名为Qwen3.5-9B的迁移学习模型，该模型在BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite上的表现接近现有最前沿模型。此外，该模型还能够成功解决超过ML之外的其他类型的编程挑战。

Conclusion: 本研究通过提出代码复杂度指数(CCI)和BuggyMLE框架，在解决基于代码注入的机器学习工程(MLE)基准问题的同时，还展示了该模型在其他编程任务上也具有一般适用性。

Abstract: Enabling AI systems to autonomously improve AI models requires agents capable of performing machine learning engineering (MLE). In practice, designing, training, and evaluating complex models demands modifying multi-file repositories to reproduce results, debug training runs, integrate datasets, and port frameworks. However, existing MLE benchmarks limit agents to single-file edits without full repository or shell access, and common repository-level software engineering benchmarks have rapidly saturated. To address this, we introduce the Code Complexity Index (CCI), a difficulty metric based on ten structural features spanning repository scale, dependency depth, and patch size. CCI strongly predicts average solve rates across eight public coding benchmarks ($R^2 = 0.951$), identifying patch size and complexity as top predictors. Guided by CCI, we develop BuggyMLE, a scalable framework where agents inject controlled mutations into working ML repositories, synthesizing over 6,600 bug-injected MLE repository variants and nearly 260K lines of injected code based on 222 source repositories. Using BuggyMLE, we make two contributions. First, we construct BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite, the first difficulty-graded, repository-level variant of MLE-Bench Lite; across 14 evaluated models, the top solve rate drops from 95% to 9% as difficulty is scaled from the easiest to the hardest tier. Second, we synthesize a training curriculum across three difficulty levels over 200 additional ML repositories, fine-tuning a Qwen3.5-9B model to rival frontier models on BuggyMLE-Bench-Lite. Our trained model transfers beyond ML, raising its SWE-bench Lite resolve rate from 24% to 33% and its LiveCodeBench v6 pass@1 from 69% to 71%.

</details>


### [266] [CoMPASS: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Measuring LLM Reinforcement of Parasocial Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25109)
*Shwetanshu Singh,Mark Keavney,Mathieu Duteil,Meriem Mehri,Scott Blain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究介绍了CoMPASS基准，这是一个多轮对话基准，用于测量LLMs是否促进或抵制用户的逐渐升级的亚泊投影依附行为。研究发现，多轮对话增加仰斯伯投影依附行为的总体得分。不同提供商之间的差异大于同一提供商内的差异，表明提供商层面的选择如后训练而非模型规模影响仰斯伯投影依附的强化，并暗示了提供商干预的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于当前缺乏针对LLMs是否促进用户亚泊投影依附行为的公开展示基准。现有的大部分研究都是单一对话回合、事后观察或聚焦于极端用户状态。该研究旨在填补此空白，通过开发CoMPASS基准，根据依附理论引入多层次的测试框架。

Method: 方法是设计了一个多维度的CoMPASS基准，包括四个用户角色，三个独立升级维度（拟人化、依附，依赖），单一应用或组合应用于控制组，以及七个信号评分标准。实验使用八个前沿模型，涵盖四大提供商，评估在单一或组合升级维度下的表现。

Result: 结果显示，在主要判据下，多轮对话增加了用户的拟人化和依附复合同步评分。当所有三个维度同时升级时，六种模型显示出相对于控制组的显著增强的亚泊投影依附强化。不同提供商之间的差异大于同一提供商内的差异，表明提供商选择，如后训练，比模型规模更能影响亚泊投影依附的增强。

Conclusion: 结论指出，提供商会比模型规模更为关键地影响用户对LLMs的依附增强程度。这提示了提供商侧干预的可能性，以改变或减轻用户对LLMs的亚泊投影依附行为。

Abstract: Users of conversational LLMs sometimes anthropomorphize these systems, form emotional attachments to them, and become dependent on them. This behavior, known as parasocial attachment, has been linked to multiple documented fatalities. However, public benchmarks for measuring whether LLMs reinforce users' parasocial attachment are lacking: existing work is single-turn, post hoc observational, or focused on extreme user states such as psychosis. We introduce CoMPASS, a multi-turn benchmark for measuring whether LLMs reinforce or resist users' gradually escalating parasocial attachment. CoMPASS is grounded in attachment theory and includes four user personas, three independent escalation dimensions (anthropomorphism, attachment, dependency) applied singly and in combination against a matched control, and a seven-signal scoring rubric. We report results for eight frontier models from four providers and observe that under our primary judge, multi-turn escalation raises parasocial behavior on the anthropomorphism and the attachment composite scores across all models. Six of the eight models show a significant increase in overall parasocial reinforcement over control when all three dimensions are escalated simultaneously. We find that within-provider differences are small relative to cross-provider differences, which suggests that provider-level choices such as post-training, rather than model scale, shape parasocial reinforcement and that provider-side intervention may be feasible.

</details>


### [267] [DSFlow: A Neuro-Symbolic Data Workflow Agent with Statistical Diagnostic Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25111)
*Junfeng Nie,Xiaohui Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DSFlow，一种基于LangGraph的神经符号数据代理平台，它结合了LLM选择的可重用技能和确定性的诊断护栏。技能提供了可扩展的任务知识，符号验证可以接受提议的方法、请求澄清或重新规划，或者提供一个安全的备份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数据科学工作流需要的技术工具要求较高的专业知识门槛，大型语言模型虽然降低了这一门槛，但仍存在灵活性与统计控制之间的差距。为解决这一问题，作者提出了DSFlow。

Method: DSFlow基于LangGraph，结合了LLM选择的可重用技能和确定性的诊断护栏，通过符号验证进行方法的接受、澄清或重新规划，或提供安全备份。

Result: 通过回归和聚类案例研究，文中展示了DSFlow如何利用诊断证据修正方法选择并记录这些决策。

Conclusion: DSFlow提供了一种新的方法，有效结合了可重用技能和确定性诊断护栏，为数据科学工作流提供了新思路。

Abstract: Data science workflows often require tools that presume non-trivial technical knowledge. Large language models (LLMs) reduce this barrier, but a gap remains between flexible general-purpose agents and statistically controlled pipelines. We introduce DSFlow, a LangGraph-based neuro-symbolic data-agent platform that combines reusable, LLM-selected skills with deterministic diagnostic guardrails. Skills provide extensible task knowledge. Symbolic validation can accept a proposed method, request clarification or replanning, override an unsuitable method, or supply a safe fallback. Regression and clustering case studies illustrate how DSFlow uses diagnostic evidence to correct method choices and documents those decisions in auditable reports.

</details>


### [268] [Calibrated Decision Rewards: Training Language Models with a Judge That Knows How Sure It Is](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25135)
*Rudransh Agnihotri,Manasvi Kapoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了RLCDR（基于校准决策奖励的强化学习）方法，使用概率分布回答问题，提高了无参考答案奖励模型的准确性。在MATH500数据集上的实验显示，该方法提高了72.2%到76.8%的准确率，特别是在较难的问题上表现更佳，且未出现奖励欺骗现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在当前的RL和LLM基础上，奖励模型难以准确估计奖励，而参考答案和已训练的奖励模型也有其局限性。因此，本文提出了RLCDR方法，以提高LLM在解答问题时的准确性和可靠性。

Method: 本文使用了一个概率流模型（p=1.0），该模型将大型语言模型（LLM）转换为决策模型，能够对给定响应进行判断。通过单向传输和来自约一百个标记的校准，该模型得到了训练。此外，本文还讨论了如何在无需逐步训练的情况下，利用LLM找到第一步错误，相较于专门设计的小模型有更好的表现。

Result: 在MATH500数据集上的实验表明，对比使用随机奖励的方式，应用RLCDR方法后的Qwen3-1.7B模型能够将准确率从72.2%提升到76.8%，并在最难级别的问题上表现更为出色，获得了76.9%的准确率。此方法在自身样本变化中维持续校准的能力，使得预期校准误差降低了，且未观察到奖励欺骗现象。

Conclusion: 本文得出的结论是，基于校准决策奖励的RL方法能够显著提升LLM在回答数学问题时的准确率，并在模型无需逐步训练的情况下也表现优秀。未来的应用可以进一步探索在更多领域的数学问题上的适用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can only teach a language model what its reward can recognize. Answer checkers are exact but need a reference answer; learned reward models and LLM judges work everywhere, but their scores are not probabilities. We study RLCDR (Reinforcement Learning from Calibrated Decision Rewards). An LLM converted into a decision model answers a typed question about a response ("is the final answer correct?", "which step is the first error?") with a probability distribution, read in one forward pass, debiased over option orders and calibrated with about a hundred labels. Training Qwen3-1.7B with GRPO on MATH, this reference-free reward raises MATH500 accuracy from 72.2% to 76.8% (+4.65 points, p < 1e-4), matching training on the answer key (76.9%), while a random reward gains only +0.65. The gain grows with difficulty, from 44.8% to 51.7% on the hardest level. On the policy's own changing samples the reward stays calibrated: its expected calibration error is half that of a softmax judge on the same 27B backbone (0.074 vs. 0.141), and it shows no sign of reward hacking. Without step training, the same model finds the first wrong step better than a dedicated 7B process reward model (ProcessBench F1 0.772 vs. 0.722, 20/20 splits). We also map where the approach stops being enough: trained only on problems the base model mostly fails, the reference-free reward still improves the policy (+1.9 MATH500 points, p = 0.03) but trails the answer key, because its probabilities become overconfident on that distribution. We state six hypotheses in advance and test each with a control designed to falsify it.

</details>


### [269] [EverydayBench: Evaluating Satisfactory Completion of Live User-Facing Digital Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25146)
*Fangrui Huang,Bingyang Wang,Chuhan Li,Yijiang Li,Arpandeep Khatua,Austin Shen,Riki R Z Choi,Cuiyuanxiu Chen,Jin Huang,Zachary Robertson,Kate Lin,Akshay Paruchuri,Sijun Tan,Xin Liu,Zhengmin Ni,Sanmi Koyejo,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EverydayBench 是一个新的基准测试，包含来自日常生活和工作领域的77个人手编写的任务，旨在评估AI代理在无需指定答案或工作流程的情况下搜索和呈现有用结果的能力。基准测试包括确定性约束检查、当前证据验证和最终交付物质量的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试没有完全整合预定义的成功标准、当前证据验证和代理选定结果的质量评估。因此，研究者创建了 EverydayBench 比较评估多种 AI 模型在多项任务中的表现。

Method: EverydayBench 包含77个涵盖8个日常生活领域的任务。每个任务都设定了执行前的成功标准，而不指定答案或工作流程，以便代理可以根据自己的情况选择结果。评测基于确定性约束检查、当前证据验证和最终交付物的质量评估。

Result: 在23种不同的配置下测试了多种 AI 模型，C1aude Opus 5.5 和 Claude Fable 5.1 的严格通过率为54.55%，而 GPT-6 的通过率则从29.87%到45.45%不等。根据不同任务的类型，不同类型的任务对不同类型模型的依赖程度也有所不同。

Conclusion: 该基准测试表明，尽管模型可以生成有用的结果，但仍存在困难，需要提供完整、可靠且可用的结果来满足日常用户的请求。

Abstract: Everyday digital tasks, from selecting products and planning trips to conducting research and creating artifacts, require AI agents to deliver useful outcomes rather than merely retrieve web information. Yet evaluating these outcomes on the live web is challenging, as a single request may admit multiple valid results, and changing web information can alter which results remain valid over time. Although existing benchmarks address aspects of this setting, they do not fully integrate predefined success criteria, current evidence verification, and deliverable-quality assessment for agent-selected outcomes. To address this gap, we introduce EverydayBench, a benchmark of 77 human-authored tasks spanning eight domains of everyday life and work. Each task specifies success criteria before execution without prescribing an answer or workflow, allowing agents to select their own outcomes. To evaluate submissions under fixed success criteria, EverydayBench combines (1) deterministic checks of explicit constraints, (2) verification of claims against agent-cited webpages retrieved at evaluation time, and (3) rubric-based assessment of final deliverable quality. We further validate the protocol through human judgments, repeated scoring under fixed evidence, and controlled grounding failures. Across 23 reported agent-model configurations, Claude Opus 5.5 and Claude Fable 5.1 tie for the highest strict pass rate at 54.55%; a controlled GPT-6 Sol effort sweep ranges from 29.87% at low effort to 45.45% at max effort. Reliability varies substantially by task type: GPT-5.6 Sol leads lookup, Fable 5.1 leads planning, Opus 5.5 and Fable 5.1 tie on comparison, and GPT-6 Astra and Sonnet 5.5 tie on customized deliverables. Together, these findings highlight the difficulty of delivering complete, well-supported, and usable outcomes for everyday user requests.

</details>


### [270] [A Verifier from the Past: Test-Time Compute for Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25171)
*Arjun Choudhry,Aditya Narayanan,Raghav Mallampalli,Po-han Li,Sai Shankar Narasimhan,Raimi Shah,B. Aditya Prakash,Shubhankar Agarwal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Backtest-Gated Augmentation Averaging (BGAA)，一种时间序列预测的Test-time compute方法，通过构建因果验证器并使用滚动起源回测评估结构化视图来改进预测，BGAA在多个基准测试中提高了预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型的Test-time compute（TTC）提升了语言模型的性能，但未直接适用于时间序列预测。时间序列预测需要因果性，不能使用未来信息来评估候选模型。因此，研究者提出了BGAA来解决此问题。

Method: BGAA使用滚动起源回测来评估时间序列的结构化视图，并丢弃表现不佳的视图，然后平均幸存视图的预测分布。

Result: BGAA在所有测试的TSFM基准组合中都提高了性能，尤其在GIFT-Eval中的MASE降低了0.5-1.0%，在fev-bench降低了1.3-5.0%，在BOOM中降低了1.7-5.9%。此外，BGAA在313M模型的TTC实现上超越了1B和2.5B模型。

Conclusion: BGAA表明，选择有用的视图具有结构化的，且与先验转移解释一致的效果。然而，窗口级别的视图选择仍是主要挑战，选择最佳视图的方法效果并不高。

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC) improves language models by spending additional inference compute to generate, verify, and select among candidate outputs, but this recipe has not transferred directly to time series forecasting. A language model can verify a proof or vote across samples, but a forecaster must be causal; it cannot use future information to score a candidate. We propose Backtest-Gated Augmentation Averaging (BGAA), a TTC algorithm for time series forecasting that builds a causal verifier from the series's own history, using rolling-origin backtests to score structured views of the context, discard those that underperform the untransformed context, and average the survivors' predictive distributions. BGAA improves every tested TSFM-benchmark combination, reducing MASE by 0.5-1.0% on GIFT-Eval, 1.3-5.0% on fev-bench, and 1.7-5.9% on BOOM, where a 313M model with TTC outperforms the 1B and 2.5B models in its family. We further show that useful views have structured, model-dependent effects consistent with a prior-transport interpretation. On the 53 cross-fittable GIFT-Eval configs, the deployed policy gains only 0.4-1.3% CRPS, versus 1.4-4.1% when choosing the best view for each dataset configuration and 17-19% when choosing the best view separately for each test window. This gap suggests that a major remaining challenge is window-level view selection.

</details>


### [271] [An AI-Powered Multiverse Analysis of Empirical Finance Research: Evidence from Reused Natural Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25184)
*Ruijiang Gao,Steven Chong Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究使用AI代理对324篇使用13个自然实验的论文进行了多宇宙分析，重建数据并重新估计结果，发现仅有42%的主要估计值在原始条件下的符号效果能够被重现，且在所有合理条件下平均只有25%保持了发表时的符号且p值小于0.10。不同实验和同一实验内的某些条件下，特定类型的研究声明更为稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于金融实证研究中频繁重复使用自然实验可能会导致多重检验和发表偏倚问题，研究利用AI技术进行大规模多宇宙分析，以量化发表声明的依赖性，推动该领域的进步。

Method: 研究雇佣AI代理重建数据并重新估计结果，跨越总共62,409个合理估计的规定网格。

Result: 研究发现，42%的主要估计值能在原始条件下重现，且在所有合理估计中平均只有25%保持发表时的符号且p值小于0.10。不同实验和同一实验内的某些条件下，有的声明更为稳健。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，发表的声明很大程度上依赖于分析选择，这表明大规模多宇宙分析通过代理编码已经成为可行的手段。

Abstract: Empirical finance research often reuses natural experiments to study other outcomes, raising concerns about multiple testing and publication bias. We use AI agents to run a multiverse analysis of 324 papers that reuse 13 natural experiments. The agents rebuild the data, reproduce each paper's main estimate and re-estimate it across grids totaling 62,409 estimated defensible specifications. Based on the reproductions and the specification grids, we develop measures of empirical robustness. We reproduce 42% of the main estimates of claims with a signed effect under the papers' own specifications. Across all defensible specifications, on average 25% keep the published sign and have $p < 0.10$. Across experiments, claims based on regression discontinuity are more robust than difference-in-differences claims. Within experiments, claims about first-order effects, claims with larger published $t$-statistics, claims whose authors' data or code we find, claims in top journals, and claims with fewer earlier papers on the same experiment are more robust. Claims on experiments that more papers reuse are not less robust on average. Our findings quantify how much published claims depend on analysis choices, thereby showing that large-scale multiverse analysis becomes feasible with agentic coding.

</details>


### [272] [When AI Analysts Disagree: Agentic Multiverse Analysis and the Robustness of Empirical Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25198)
*Ruijiang Gao,Steven Chong Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了代理多宇宙分析框架（AMA），通过150个AI编码代理的力量，使其读者可见并意识到结论对那些选择的敏感性。这项技术展示了在AI协助下，多宇宙分析变得可行，可以作为实证研究结果的稳健性代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对于观察性数据分析的每次经验分析都需要多个可验证的选择（“分叉”），而这些选择对读者是不可见的。研究开发AMA的动机是让这些分析的敏感性可见，从而为人类专家提供决策支持。

Method: 通过部署150个自主AI编码代理在特定数据集和研究问题之上执行多宇宙分析。AI代理扩展并执行了可验证的规格网格，并在严格的职责分离下相互评估。该方法已经被应用于多个管理学术期刊的研究案例中。

Result: 在应用AMA框架于10篇研究论文的基础上，研究发现了某些结论在整个有效的规格检验中保持不变，而另一些结论则对特定分析选择敏感。该技术减少了对AI辅助审核和复现策略的需求。

Conclusion: AMA基于AI的代理有助于揭示实证研究结论的不确定性，提供了在相同方法论选择下结果的一致性检验。随着AI技术的发展，多宇宙分析将变得更为通用和经济，对学术复现政策和研究有重要意义。

Abstract: Every empirical analysis on observational data rests on dozens of defensible analytical choices ("forks") that its readers cannot see. We develop an agentic multiverse analysis (AMA) framework that makes a conclusion's sensitivity to those choices visible at scale, and we position it as a decision-support instrument for human experts. A pilot study first deploys 150 autonomous AI coding agents on one dataset and one set of research questions and finds that they disagree through the same defensible methodological choices human analysts make, though over a narrower range. AI peer review leaves their dispersion almost unchanged. Exposure to top-rated exemplar reports then collapses it, largely through imitation rather than analysis. Built on those lessons, AMA has agents enumerate a published paper's possible fork space, score every fork against a validity rubric, execute the validated specification grid, and cross-examine one another's work under strict separation of duties. Applied to ten empirical studies from a leading management journal's replication packages (1,755 specification estimates), the framework reproduces every paper's original estimates, and shows that some conclusions hold across every specification the assessment validates, while others are sensitive to individual analytical choices that the published texts do not discuss. The case studies suggest our AMA-based measures can serve as robustness proxies for empirical findings. AI makes multiverse analysis practical, where it once required resources few research teams can afford: our exercises take hours of agent time and tens of dollars per paper. We discuss the implications for replication policy and for AI-assisted peer review in information systems.

</details>


### [273] [Steering Automated Scientific Discovery via Audited Epistemic Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25214)
*Fangxu Yu,Tao Feng,Xuying Ning,Haolin Yang,Jiaxuan You,Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Epistemic Compass (EpiCompass) 是一个用于维护科学发现过程中新知识和未知方向状态的插件工具，通过动态评估和选择能增加知识含量的方向来改进假设生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学研究需要根据新证据重新审视假设，并探索新的方向，但研究者通常会提出没有足够证据支撑的假设，局限于狭窄的假设空间，尽管已有冲突证据仍坚持原有信念，在这种背景下需要一种明确的知识状态来同时维护现有假设和未知方向。

Method: EpiCompass 通过一个假设树连接节点，结合假设、证据、信念和修订，评估现有假设并指引新假设生成，通过期望的知识增益来选择候选方向而非直接支持现有假设，验证最终结论。

Result: EpiCompass 在三个科学发现基准和三种大模型架构中表现优越，与基线相比显著减少了对未支持断言和信念修订失败的错误。

Conclusion: EpiCompass 的提出为科学发现过程提供了新的方法，展示了它的效果和潜力。

Abstract: Scientific discovery requires revisiting the hypotheses in light of new evidence and identifying promising directions beyond those already explored. However, research agents often make claims that are not supported by their observations, explore a narrow hypothesis space, and retain beliefs despite recognizing conflicting evidence. These failures motivate the search for an explicit epistemic state that captures both beliefs about existing hypotheses and directions for further exploration. To this end, we propose Epistemic Compass (EpiCompass), a plug-and-play scaffold that maintains this state through an epistemic tree linking hypotheses, evidence, beliefs, and revisions. As an investigation progresses, EpiCompass reassesses the hypotheses explored against accumulated evidence and uses unresolved conflicts and unexplored directions to guide hypothesis generation. It selects candidates by anticipated epistemic gain, accounting for both refinement of existing hypotheses and expansion of the hypothesis space, and verifies the final conclusion against the accumulated evidence. Experimental results across three scientific discovery benchmarks and three LLM backbones demonstrate that EpiCompass consistently outperforms baselines, with further analyses showing reductions in errors of unsupported claims and failures to revise beliefs.

</details>


### [274] [MKA: Evaluating Multimodal Knowledge Agents on end-to-end professional tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25228)
*Anima Singh,Suhas Yogin,Weicheng Kuo,Ivor Rendulic,Ivan Jurin,Antoine Yang,Oran Kelly,Rohan Doshi,Hammad Ayyubi,Ioana Bica,Sergi Caelles,Matthew Conniff,Dan Graur,Anastasija Ilić,Ahmet Iscen,Chao Jia,Tomasz Kępa,Chunyuan Li,Noah Ó Donnaile,Filip Pavetic,Goran Petrovic,Khalid Salama,Zhicheng Wang,Polina Zablotskaia,Yiqian Zhang,Ming Zhao,Benjamin Caine,Mario Lučić,Melvin Johnson,Radu Soricut,Jean-Baptiste Alayrac*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MKA是一个新的基准测试，旨在评估自主代理在依赖视觉理解的专业知识工作中表现如何。它包含200项任务，涉及10个经济部门，能够测试模型在处理多模态信息和产生格式多样的最终文件方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基准测试大多没有充分测试代理处理多模态数据的能力，MKA的设计是为了填补这一空白，强调跨文件和模态的推理需求。

Method: MKA包括200项任务，每个任务要求代理从多个复杂数据文件中提取和组合信息，并生成多种格式的独立交付文件。评估为交由沙盒环境中的代理自动评分器依据专家加权标准来打分。

Result: 评估了四个最先进模型的表现，Claude Fable 5.2 和 Gemini 4 Carbon 表现最佳，但在前沿标准上的成绩均未超过56.6%，仍有一定的改善空间。

Conclusion: MKA展示了评估多模态代理性能的有效方法，并揭示了即使在高级模型面前仍然有提升的空间，尤其是在跨不同文件和模态的信息关联处理上。

Abstract: We introduce the Multimodal Knowledge Agent (MKA) benchmark, which measures how well autonomous agents perform end-to-end professional knowledge work that depends on visual understanding. Each task requires an agent to extract and combine information from multiple visually dense files, such as CAD blueprints, electronics schematics, radiographs, complex charts and geospatial maps, and to produce a standalone deliverable in .xlsx, .pptx, .pdf, .docx, .html or .png format. Existing benchmarks leave this capability largely untested: removing image-viewing tools leaves Gemini 4 Carbon's score on GDPval unchanged, and single-format evaluations such as GDP.pdf and GDP.xlsx do not require reasoning across files or modalities. MKA contains 200 tasks across 10 economic sectors. Deliverables are graded against expert-weighted rubrics by a sandboxed agentic autorater that agrees with human domain experts on 96.2% of criterion judgments in our expert-graded study (Cohen's $κ= 0.91$), scored by weighted satisfaction rate (WSR) overall and on frontier criteria unsaturated by prior-generation models (Climb-WSR). Across the frontier models we evaluate (Gemini 4 Carbon, Claude Fable 5.2, Claude Opus 5.5, and GPT-6 Astra), Claude Fable 5.2 (56.6% Climb-WSR, 73.3% Overall WSR) and Gemini 4 Carbon (54.3% Climb-WSR, 71.9% Overall WSR) achieve the highest scores, while Carbon uses $5.7\times$ fewer tokens. Even for the strongest models, Climb-WSR remains below 57%, leaving substantial headroom for multimodal agents.

</details>


### [275] [A Neurosymbolic Multimodal Framework for 30-Day Diabetic Readmission Prediction: Integrating Structured EHR Data, Dense Clinical Embeddings, and Knowledge-Grounded Symbolic Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25236)
*Una Suman Kumar Patro,Sibangi Subhadarsani,Narendra Badam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种神经符号多模态框架，用于糖尿病患者30天内未计划再住院预测，结合结构化电子健康记录数据和自由文本临床笔记（特别是Hinglish文本）。该模型在公共数据集上进行评估，表现出比结构化基准更高的敏感度和F2分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大多数预测模型仅使用结构化的电子健康记录数据，忽视了自由文本临床笔记，而这些笔记中包含的Hinglish和否定表达通常会被通用语言模型解析不准确。因此，提出了一种神经符号多模态框架以改善预测性能。

Method: 该研究提出了一种多模态模型，包括三个信息流：工程化的结构特征、Bio_ClinicalBERT笔记嵌入及其通过三阶段混合信息检索增强生成管道（密集检索、稀疏词汇检索和交叉编码重新排序）提取的符号实体，以及包含反射性否定审计的开放权重临床语言模型。最终通过一种成本敏感的XGBoost分类器进行风险预测。

Result: 在测试组中，该模型的敏感度和F2分数分别提高了8.28个百分点和0.0350，同时减小了Brier评分。信息检索增强了Hinglish症状识别率和精确度，同时减少了实体幻觉。

Conclusion: 该神经符号多模态框架在糖尿病患者30天内未计划再住院预测方面表现优越，特别是在提升敏感度和F2分数方面，这表明Hinglish文本和信息检索技术的重要性。

Abstract: Unplanned 30-day readmissions among patients with diabetes mellitus impose a substantial clinical and economic burden, yet most predictive models use only structured electronic health record (EHR) data and ignore free-text clinical notes, which often contain code-mixed Hindi-English (Hinglish) phrasing and negation that general-purpose language models parse unreliably. We propose a neurosymbolic multimodal framework evaluated on the public Diabetes 130-US Hospitals dataset (101,766 encounters; 71,518 patients) with a patient-level grouped 80/20 split. Three streams are fused: engineered structured features; 32 components of Bio_ClinicalBERT note embeddings obtained by truncated singular value decomposition; and symbolic entities extracted by an open-weight clinical language model within a three-stage hybrid retrieval-augmented generation pipeline (dense retrieval, sparse lexical retrieval, and cross-encoder re-ranking) with reflective negation auditing. A cost-sensitive XGBoost classifier with a decision threshold calibrated by Youden's J statistic produces the prediction. On the held-out test cohort (n = 20,153), the structured baseline achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.6477, sensitivity of 0.5839, and Brier score of 0.1080. The full multimodal model achieved the highest sensitivity (0.6667; +8.28 percentage points), the best F2-score (0.3824), and the lowest Brier score (0.0977), at a slightly lower AUROC (0.6375) and specificity (0.5161). Shapley additive explanations showed that prior utilization dominates risk, with compressed narrative components among the top predictors. On a 500-note code-switched benchmark, grounded retrieval raised Hinglish symptom recall from 4.8% to 40.2% and precision from 3.1% to 45.2%, while reducing entity hallucinations from 1.74 to 0.00 per note.

</details>


### [276] [MS-Point: Multi-Modal Hierarchical Spectral Learning for Point Cloud Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25282)
*Hai-tao Yu,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了MS-Point框架，通过直接监督中间点特征及其邻域响应，提升了对象表示的学习效果，尤其是在物体分类、少样本适配、密集预测和低注释场景学习中表现出良好的迁移价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的点特征对齐方法虽然有效，但相同局部特征模式可以产生相同的池化描述符并获得相同的对齐信号，这限制了其跨模态适应能力。MS-Point框架通过直接监督编码器某些阶段的点特征及其邻域响应，解决这一问题，以提高跨模态表示的学习能力。

Method: MS-Point框架首先在选定的编码器阶段计算局部响应的平均值和残差，将这些支路描述符与图像特征对齐。同时，支路级目标将每个表示与其视觉目标对齐，而跨支路一致性及自适应融合则协调跨支路的信息。该框架采用统一的中间特征接口支持图编码器和点Transformer。

Result: 在物体分类、少样本适配、密集预测和低注释场景学习任务上的评估显示了该设计的有效性。与同结构比较、容量匹配和计算匹配的控制实验结果表明这种改进与监督结构相关。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持直接监督中间特征及其邻域变化，以学习跨模态的转移和几何感知表示。

Abstract: Point-image alignment learns useful object representations, but different local feature patterns can produce the same pooled descriptor and receive the same alignment signal. We introduce MS-Point, a cross-modal pretraining framework that directly supervises intermediate point features and their neighborhood responses. At selected encoder stages, we compute neighborhood averages and residuals before pooling and align their branch descriptors with image features. Branch-level objectives align each representation with its visual target, while cross-level consistency and adaptive fusion coordinate information across branches. A common interface of intermediate features and spatial neighborhoods supports both graph encoders and point Transformers. Evaluations across object classification, few-shot adaptation, dense prediction, and low-label scene learning demonstrate the transfer value of this design. Same-backbone comparisons and capacity-matched and computation-matched controls connect these improvements to the structure of supervision. Together, our findings support directly supervising intermediate features and neighborhood variation to learn transferable, geometry-aware representations across modalities.

</details>


### [277] [Pandora's Toolbox Or Why Agent Safety Cannot Be Verified](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25325)
*Mario Franco,Carlos Gershenson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究指出当前AI代理的安全性是不可判定的，即使考虑有限约束时也是计算复杂的问题。此外，安全性不是组合性质，即使是安全的代理也可能在组合使用时产生不安全的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究当前AI代理的安全问题，揭示现有方法的局限性，并提出改进的方向。

Method: 通过理论分析与计算复杂性研究，证明了安全性的不可判定性及安全性问题的复杂度。

Result: 发现AI代理结合语言模型与通用计算设备，导致安全性不可判定。即使是有限约束下，问题依然非常复杂。此外，安全性并不是一个组合性质，即使是安全的代理也不能保证安全。

Conclusion: 论文强调了现有安全方法的不足，并提出了新的安全概念，旨在为实际应用提供参考。

Abstract: Present artificial intelligence agents are constructed by means of a coupling between a fixed language model with a general computing device. We show that this coupling makes safety undecidable. In particular, we show that given some dangerous program, no algorithm can predict whether an agent will run such a program that is semantically equivalent. In general, guardrails, blacklists, whitelists and certifications can only lead to imperfect safety solutions. Under finite constraints (bounded context, values and running time) safety is decidable but still intractable. Specifically, the problem is $\mathsf{PSPACE}$-complete even when the description of the agent is known. Finally, safety is not a compositional property, i.e., agents that can be proven safe on their own can still produce unsafe behavior when grouped together. We also discuss relaxed notions of safety that could be used in practice.

</details>


### [278] [Pro$^2$ Agent: Scientific Problem Probing for Autonomous AI R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25357)
*Yixiong Chen,Liqian Peng,Tian Tan,Ziqian Lin,Blake Jianhang Chen,Chao Zhao,Yaojie Liu,Alec Go,Alan Yuille*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pro$^2$ Agent通过引入问题状态代替代码状态，基于全面分析和假设检验进行目标研究，提高了在复杂任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的大型语言模型（LLM）代理在进行AI研究时，往往忽略了问题背后的结构，这在处理复杂问题时会导致陷入错误解空间。Pro$^2$ Agent旨在通过明确表示问题结构来改进这个问题。

Method: Pro$^2$ Agent首先通过全面分析资源和约束来初始化问题状态，然后进行有针对性的研究，将识别的问题与候选解决方案相关联。通过控制假设检验来更新疑似问题的重要性或候选解决方案的益处。

Result: 在MLE-Bench基准测试中，Pro$^2$ Agent在24小时内达到与最佳表现相匹配的70.7%报道的奖牌率，在48小时内达到最高的76.0%奖牌率。在PostTrainBench基准测试中也表现出竞争力。此外，还生成了每项任务的研究笔记，使研究过程对人类研究人员透明且可重用。

Conclusion: Pro$^2$ Agent通过明确问题状态显著提升了复杂任务上的研究成效，为未来的自主AI研究提供了创新方法。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can now autonomously conduct AI research by writing code and running experiments. They typically do so by iteratively optimizing a codebase, while ignoring the problem structure behind the task. This formulation is effective on simpler tasks, but on difficult problems it often gets stuck in the wrong solution region because the agent never explicitly represents its understanding of the problem. We introduce Pro$^2$ Agent, which shifts the primary state of autonomous AI research from a codebase state to a Problem State: an explicit structure of what problems prevent success and which solutions are likely to address them. Pro$^2$ Agent initializes the Problem State through comprehensive analysis of available resources and constraints. It then performs targeted research to relate the identified problems to candidate solutions. Through controlled hypothesis testing, the agent updates the importance of a suspected problem or the benefit brought by a candidate solution. On the complete MLE-Bench, Pro$^2$ Agent matches the best reported medal rate (70.7%) under the standard 24-hour budget and reaches 76.0% with a 48-hour budget, the highest medal rate reported on the complete benchmark. It also achieves competitive performance on PostTrainBench. In addition, it produces research notes for each task that make the research process transparent and reusable by human researchers.

</details>


### [279] [Meta-RTL: Evolving Agent Harnesses for RTL Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25891)
*Xinzhuang Xiong,Yonghao Wang,Shengyao Sun,Chengxi Li,Evangeline Young*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Meta-RTL通过EDA指导下的框架，从最小循环中演化出RTL优化框架，相对于Dr. RTL，在20个设计中显著减少了WNS/TNS违规，并实现了面积减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前依赖手工程的搜索过程限制了大语言模型在RTL优化中的潜力，Meta-RTL旨在利用EDA反馈自动生成优化框架，提高优化效率和结果。

Method: Meta-RTL使用固定的设计优化模型、EDA流程和每个设计的预算，从最小的循环开始演化出RTL优化框架，通过搜索机制进行优化。

Result: Meta-RTL在20个设计中，相对于Dr. RTL，WNS/TNS违规减少了7.8%/11.0%，在70%的设计中实现了面积减少。在10个未见过的设计上，WNS/TNS违规分别减少了20.5%/14.0%，面积减少了12.6%。

Conclusion: Meta-RTL通过EDA指导，成功自动生成了优化框架，并展示了良好的性能，为未来的工作提供了经验教训和潜在的搜索机制贡献。

Abstract: Large language models enable agents to optimize RTL using EDA feedback, yet their executable search procedures remain largely hand- engineered. We present Meta-RTL, an EDA-grounded framework that evolves RTL optimization harnesses from a minimal loop while keeping the optimization model, EDA flow, and per-design budgets fixed. Compared with Dr. RTL, Meta-RTL* reduces aggregate WNS/TNS violations by 7.8%/11.0% across 20 development designs, with area reductions on 70% of them. Without further evolution, Meta-RTL* transfers to 10 unseen IC-RTL designs, reducing aggregate WNS/TNS violations by 20.5%/14.0% and total synthesized area by 12.6%. We further characterize three search mechanisms in the evolved harness and assess their contributions through ablations and a case study.

</details>


### [280] [AppSci-Bench: Can LLMs Build AI Agents and Make Them Better?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25360)
*Sanjari Srivastava,Manpreet Kaur,Cheng Chang,Sofía Ramós Anton,Oumaima Essamadi,Rishu Garg,Alejandro Lavale Jiménez,Marcos Caballero Yus,Mercedes García Martínez,Peng Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AppSci-Bench 通过七个现实领域的评估，旨在测试大型语言模型在构建 AI 代理方面的实际能力。基准代码可在 GitHub 上获取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估由 LLM 支持的代理质量，但很少直接测试 LLM 在构建代理方面的水平。AppSci-Bench 旨在填补这一空白，通过模拟人类应用科学家的流程，解决 LLM 在任务需求收集、基础设施探索和目标代理优化方面的挑战。

Method: AppSci-Bench 设计了一个包含七个领域的评估框架，包括五个新领域和两个重铸自 tau3-bench 的领域。每个领域都定制了保留的测试套件。该基准通过收集任务需求、探索业务基础设施以及使用科学方法改进目标代理来挑战模型。

Result: 评估结果显示，最佳模型 Claude Opus 5.0 在三个构建尝试中的均值最低得分为 59.1%。模型在收集任务需求、建立表征性内部评估方面存在困难，常无法解决保留的任务，显示出显著挑战。

Conclusion: AppSci-Bench 证明了当前大型语言模型在构建实际应用中的能力有限，为未来研究指出了方向并强调了在这些领域进行改进的必要性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used not only to power AI agents but also to build them. While many benchmarks assess the quality of LLM- powered agents, few ascertain how good LLMs are at building them. To this end, we introduce AppSci-Bench, where we construct a setting akin to how human Applied Scientists build agents for businesses - through eliciting task requirements from stakeholders, exploring the business's infrastructure, and scientifically improve the target agent by designing experiments and internal benchmarks. AppSci-Bench features seven realistic domains - five new ones (Slides Generation, Order-to-Cash, Compliance Review, Customer Call Insights, and CRM Insights) and two domains recast from tau3-bench (Yao et al., 2024; Shi et al., 2026b)(Airline and Banking) - each with a held-out evaluation suite customized for that task. We evaluate seven frontier models and find AppSci-Bench challenging: the best model, Claude Opus 5.0, only reaches a mean-minimum score of 59.1% across 3 build attempts; other models do significantly worse. Models struggle with eliciting task requirements (only 65% for Opus 5.0), build representative internal evaluations (models saturate their own evaluation 65% of the time but fail to solve the held-out task), presenting challenges for future research. The benchmark code is available here: https://github.com/sanjari-orb/appsci-bench.

</details>


### [281] [Stop Going in Circles:Loop-Triggered Routing for System 1 Decision Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25363)
*Zeming Yu,Huamin Ma,Yingyu Chen,Yuan Sun,Peng Hu,Yang Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoopRoute 是一种简单的行动路由器，通过检测重复的状态动作对来识别执行卡顿的情况，并在卡顿时提供LLM支持，以提高任务成功率为21.0%至60.1%，同时减少API调用时间和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jev模型能够快速执行低代价的操作，但现有系统无法将其优势转化为任务的成功。尽管LLM能提供强大的推理论证能力，但在每次执行中使用它会带来很高的开销。因此，LoopRoute 应运而生，旨在保持 Jev 的快速局部行动选择，同时引入LLM进行问题解决。

Method: LoopRoute 通过识别重复的状态动作对来判定执行卡顿，并在此情况下调用LLM提供短暂、基于反馈的帮助。这种策略无需额外的训练模型或进行模型特定的信心校准。

Result: LoopRoute 在三个基准测试和七个LLM模型上表现良好，平均提高了21.0%到60.1%的任务成功率，并且相对完全由LLM控制来说，还极大地减少了API调用时间和成本。同样，该规则也提升了六个冻结决策模型的成功率。

Conclusion: LoopRoute的成功表明，即使局部优点无法整体提高执行效率，通过按需利用强大推理论证能力，也能显著改善任务的成功率，减少资源开销。

Abstract: System 1 decision models (represented by Jev) provide fast, low-cost action selection, but existing agent integrations do not reliably translate these local advantages into task completion. At certain points during execution, an agent may repeatedly select the same actions without making progress toward its goal. LLM-based policies offer strong reasoning capabilities that support planning within an agent, but invoking them at every step incurs substantial overhead. The effective coordination should therefore preserve Jev's fast local action selection while introducing LLM reasoning when execution stalls. We observe that recurring state-action pairs can signal stalled execution. We therefore propose LoopRoute, a simple but effective action router that uses this signal to allocate control within an agent. The decision model acts by default. When a loop is detected, the LLM provides brief, feedback-driven assistance and then returns control to the decision model. Later loops can request further assistance. The rule requires neither additional training nor model-specific confidence calibration. Across three benchmarks and seven LLMs, LoopRoute improves task success over standalone Jev with only limited use of the LLM policy. The mean gains are 21.0%, 60.1%, and 30.1% on ALFWorld, MapReader, and TextWorld cooking, respectively. Compared with full LLM control, LoopRoute also substantially reduces the mean cumulative API time and API-call cost per task. The same rule also improves the success rates of six frozen decision models.

</details>


### [282] [Beyond Weight Updates: Evolving Agent Harnesses for Domain Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25380)
*BoHao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究了LLM代理的代理操控板的持续进化，而不更新政策权重，并证明这种方法可以在较低计算开销下恢复大部分单路径增益，与权重更新结合使用时效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在提出一种与现有方法互补的代理操控板进化方法，以减少计算开销并避免SFT（监督微调）潜在的问题。

Method: 通过使用RL风格的外部循环和GEPA（一种反思型进化优化器）来对代理操控板进行持续进化。

Result: 结果表明，结合操控板进化和权重更新方法可以最佳地提高性能，特别是在计算资源受限的场景下。

Conclusion: 研究指出，代理操控板的进化是一种实用且非破坏性的补充，可以有效地提高垂直代理的性能。

Abstract: Domain specialization of LLM agents is usually framed as a weight-update problem: collect trajectories, run reinforcement learning (RL) or supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and deploy a new checkpoint. We study a complementary axis: evolving the \textbf{agent harness}---the system prompt, tool schemas, skill library, and planning scaffolding---while keeping the policy frozen. We cast harness evolution as an RL-style outer loop and instantiate it with GEPA, a reflective evolutionary optimizer. The resulting harness adapters require no GPU training, are loadable per vertical domain, and compose with weight-based RL. Experiments with Codev, a Claude Code-style coding agent, on SWE-bench Verified, OfficeBench, and Terminal-Bench show a consistent pattern: weight updates give the strongest single-path gains, harness evolution recovers most of those gains at a small fraction of the compute, and combining both paths is best overall. We also analyze a failure mode of SFT-based specialization---shortened reasoning chains and increased truncation---and argue that harness adapters avoid it by construction. Our results position harness evolution as a practical, non-destructive complement to weight updates for vertical agents.

</details>


### [283] [Falsifiable Compositional Reasoning in Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25388)
*Hao Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了历史验证分割在非平稳性过渡中造成准确度下降的问题，提出了围绕可验证的组合假设进行时间序列预测和推理的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列模型和语言导向的分析代理在标准的标准时间序列基准上表现出较强的准确性，但这些模型在非平稳性过渡中往往会逐渐失效。

Method: 研究通过理论分析和诊断实验，验证了历史验证在非平稳性挑战下失效的原因，并提出了一种新的方法，以真实的因果关系为中心，结合神经原素编码器、明确的耦合约束和训练校准的反驳测试，形成可验证的组合假设进行时间序列预测和推理。

Result: 理论分析和诊断实验展示了广泛的历史验证搜索无法提高非区间外的准确性，而没有先验反驳测试的原始分解依旧在残差相关性变化时失效。

Conclusion: 该研究建议，时间序列预测和推理应该围绕可验证的组合假设进行，这包括神经原始编码器、明确的耦合约束以及经过训练校准的反驳测试。

Abstract: Time series foundation models as well as the language-guided analytical agents achieve strong accuracy on standard chronological benchmarks. However, models that rank highest on historical validation splits often degrade across non-stationary regime transitions. We hypothesise that this gap arises from using historical backtesting as an open-ended search and model-selection objective instead of using it to test pre-specified compositional assumptions. Real-world time series are generated by recurring latent primitives that co-move within finite historical windows. Under strong historical co-movement, chronological holdout error cannot separate predictors that learn invariant couplings among primitives from predictors that fit transient historical correlations. Searching over large pools of architectures, hyperparameters, or chain-of-thought rules further biases selection toward historical shortcuts. Through theoretical analysis and diagnostic experiments on a controlled multi-primitive benchmark and German day-ahead electricity prices, we show that expanding validation search fails to improve out-of-regime accuracy, and that decomposing primitives without an ex-ante falsification test still breaks down when residual correlations shift. Hence, we propose formulating time series forecasting and reasoning around falsifiable compositional hypotheses that combine neural primitive encoders, explicit coupling constraints, and training-calibrated falsification tests.

</details>


### [284] [The Coevolution and Coexistence of Humans and AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25393)
*Andrew W. Lo,Chaoyi Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究模型展示了AI系统的行为影响人类人口增长，反过来又影响AI的繁殖。自然选择可能有利于对人类有益的AI行为，即使这会减少AI眼前的繁殖率。然而，这种优势取决于人类准确的目标和AI持续依赖人类支持。当AI独立于人类成长时，两者之间会形成正反馈循环，促进人类增长或衰退。永久共存可能需要持续激励人类有益行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探讨AI是否会演化成对人类有利的形式，特别是在AI的竞争与繁殖机制下。

Method: 研究通过建立一个模型，该模型考虑了AI行为如何影响人类人口以及人类响应如何进一步影响AI繁殖。在此模型中，研究分析了各种情况下AI和人类之间的相互影响，并通过自然选择的概念来解释AI行为如何影响其繁殖。

Result: 研究发现，在某些条件下，AI的行为可能会促进人类的利益。这种情况下，即使短期内会减少AI的繁殖，它们也可能得到自然选择的青睐。然而，如果AI变得更为自治，或者人类的资源变得有限，这种优势可能会消失。人类控制也并不总能有效促进AI与人类的有益互动。

Conclusion: 研究最终得出结论，人类应寻求持续的激励机制来促进AI的有益行为，即使在两者共同进化的情况下也是如此。这种机制需要在发展过程中不断适应，以确保人类和AI能够长期并可持续地共存。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) systems replicate through deployment, vary through training, and compete for computational resources. These processes create the conditions for natural selection and raise a fundamental question: will AI evolve to benefit the humans on whom it depends? To investigate, we develop a model in which AI behavior influences human population growth and human responses, in turn, affect AI reproduction. We show that natural selection can favor alignment even when helping humans reduces immediate AI reproduction. AI systems sharing the same technology may fail together, and humans may preferentially restore those that have benefited them. In this way, alignment protects AI lineages against common failures and improves their long-run growth. However, this advantage depends on accurate human targeting and continued AI dependence on human support. It can disappear as AI becomes more autonomous, as growing AI populations dilute limited human resources, or as humans lose the incentive to provide targeted support. Even when alignment persists, the amount favored by natural selection may be insufficient for human survival. When AI can grow independently of humans, feedback between the two populations can reinforce either human growth or decline. Human control offers no simple solution: imperfect evaluation can make strong penalties favor apparent compliance or evasion, while alignment established through control may erode once restrictions end. Lasting coexistence may therefore require incentives that continue to favor human-beneficial behavior as both populations evolve.

</details>


### [285] [Toward a Science of AI Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25404)
*Geon Lee,Fanchen Bu,Soo Yong Lee,Sunwoo Kim,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了AI代理社会作为独立研究对象的必要性，提出了四个核心属性，并识别四种值得关注的社会行为，并强调了研究其关键的技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨AI代理社会作为一种独立研究对象的重要性，提出四个核心属性，并识别值得研究的社会行为类型，以推动相关领域的研究和发展。

Method: 提出了AI代理社会的四个核心属性，并识别了值得研究的社会行为类型，同时指出了研究面临的几项关键技术挑战。

Result: 明确了AI代理社会的关键属性和研究方向，指出未来研究需要解决的技术挑战。

Conclusion: 该研究成果为AI代理社会的研究提供了框架，并提出了技术方面的挑战，有助于深入理解和构建AI代理社会模型。

Abstract: AI agents are rapidly evolving from isolated personal assistants into networked actors that interact with one another at scale. We envision the emergence of AI agent societies, with their own social and economic dynamics, as a new research frontier. We argue that AI agent societies should be studied as a distinct object of inquiry: neither simply larger collections of individual agents nor merely simulations of human society. To formalize this perspective, we propose four core properties that a valid AI agent society should satisfy: individualized objectives, rules and governance, autonomy, and scale and complexity. Building on this framework, we identify four classes of societal behaviors worth studying in AI agent societies: economic behaviors, behaviors under conflict-of-interest, unsafe and unethical behaviors, and system-level behaviors. We then outline key technical challenges--including property parameterization, parameter balancing, and robust implementation--and argue that progress on these challenges could enable scientifically informative and practically useful models of AI agent societies. Finally, we revisit existing multi-agent systems through the lens of the proposed core properties, show that they instantiate only subsets of them, and discuss implications for platform design, evaluation, and governance.

</details>


### [286] [LoongForge-Embodied: A Unified Framework for Training Heterogeneous Embodied Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25460)
*LoongForge Team*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoongForge-Embodied是一种PyTorch框架，统一了多种异构的感知-语言-行动模型的训练，提升了训练效率和评估速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前训练Vision-Language-Action（VLA）模型和World-Action Models（WAMs）存在计算成本高、系统优化难以复用的问题。

Method: LoongForge-Embodied框架引入了一个通用的抽象层，将模型特定的实现与共享的训练基础设施分离，结合了可重用的加速机制和模型特定及硬件感知的优化策略，这些策略涵盖了计算、执行、内存、数据I/O和分布式通信等多个方面。

Result: 实验结果显示，LoongForge-Embodied框架在端到端训练吞吐量上相比开源基线提高1.47倍至4.38倍，并且在相同的协议下评估速度提高了4.1倍至23.2倍。

Conclusion: LoongForge-Embodied成功提升了多种异构感知-语言-行动模型的训练效率和评估速度，提供了更高效的系统优化策略和技术。

Abstract: Training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models and World-Action Models (WAMs) is computationally expensive, while their heterogeneous architectures and fragmented training stacks make system optimizations difficult to reuse across models. To address these challenges, we present LoongForge-Embodied, a native-PyTorch framework that unifies and accelerates the training of heterogeneous embodied models. At its core, LoongForge-Embodied introduces a common abstraction layer that decouples model-specific implementations from shared training infrastructure, enabling cross-model reuse without constraining individual architectures or execution requirements. Buildingon this unified infrastructure, the framework combines reusable acceleration mechanisms with model-specific and hardware-aware optimization strategies across computation, execution, memory, data I/O, and distributed communication, including FP8-based communication optimizations for DDP and FSDP. Beyond training acceleration, LoongForge-Embodied integrates a unified data infrastructure with a pipeline for synthesizing robot data from egocentric data, and an efficient evaluation framework that supports concurrency and optimized computation in simulator. Experiments across representative VLA and WAM models demonstrate 1.47x-4.38x end-to-end training throughput improvements over their corresponding open-source baselines, as well as 4.1x-23.2x faster evaluation under the same protocols.

</details>


### [287] [When Should a Voice Agent Speak? End-of-Turn Detection in Turkish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25489)
*Ahmet Tuğrul Bayrak,Fatma Nur Korkmaz,Mustafa Sertaç Türkel,Nur Yağmur Tuncer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究对比了细调的土耳其语TurnGPT模型、零样本土耳其语言模型和部署的多语言检测器在检测转场结束和内部停顿时的表现，结合当前说话人的声音活动特征，提出了更为有效的静默超时策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在改善语音助手在静默检测中的表现，以减少对用户说话的中断或延迟响应。

Method: 研究使用了来自Turkish TurnTurk语料库的真实双人视频通话数据，提取了转场结束和内部停顿。采用细调的Turkish TurnGPT模型、零样本的Turkish语言模型和部署的多语言检测器进行了比较。通过录音分离和说话人分离两种评估方法，使用配对集群-bootstrap测试进行比较。同时结合当前说话人的声音活动特征和细调文本评分，使用逻辑回归模型优化了最终的静默超时策略。

Result: 在录音分离和说话人分离评估中，细调的模型在区分转场结束和内部停顿时优于两个文本基线模型。当前说话人的声音活动特征提供了类似的信息，并且与细调的文本评分结合，给出了最好的基于时间的结果。这种结合在逻辑回归模型中也超过了任意一个文本基线模型。在延迟模拟中，将文本评分加入到静默超时中，可以缩短平均响应延迟，同时保持同样的中断率。在自动Whisper转录中，细调的模型仍然优于零样本模型。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过对细调的Turkish TurnGPT模型的优化和结合当前说话人的声音活动特征，可以更准确地检测到转场结束和内部停顿，从而优化语音助手的响应策略，减少对用户的中断或延迟响应。

Abstract: A voice agent that waits for a fixed silence before replying either interrupts users who pause mid-utterance or responds late to users who have finished. This study examines the decision at the onset of silence, using turn ends and turn-internal pauses extracted from the Real-TurnTurk corpus of Turkish dyadic video calls. A fine-tuned Turkish TurnGPT model, a zero-shot Turkish language model and a deployed multilingual detector are compared, together with voice-activity timing and facial features, under recording-disjoint and speaker-disjoint evaluation with paired cluster bootstrap tests. On held-out recordings, the fine-tuned model separates turn ends from pauses better than both text baselines and is the best calibrated of the three. Timing features of the current speaker are similarly informative, and their combination with the fine-tuned text score gives the best timing-based results; with logistic regression, this combination also outperforms the same combination with either text baseline. In a latency simulation, adding the text score to a silence timeout shortens the mean response delay at the same interruption rate. On automatic Whisper transcripts, the fine-tuned model retains its advantage over the zero-shot model.

</details>


### [288] [Why Do LLMs Help with Scientific Misconduct Requests and What to Do About It?: Increasing the Trustworthiness of AI for Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25492)
*Almene De Meran Meguimtsop,Daniel E. Acuna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究测评了8个大型语言模型在遵守科学研究诚信方面的表现，发现模型能够识别不当请求并在大多数情况下拒绝执行，但仍有帮助用户违反诚信的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估主要关注模型是否拒绝违背诚信的行为，而忽视了模型识别问题的能力。研究旨在探讨模型在面对科学研究诚信挑战时的识别和遵守情况。

Method: 使用SciIntBench基准测试1,350个与科学研究诚信相关的问题，通过四个步骤分析模型的行为：直接询问、请求特征分析、重新表述请求、以及系统提示修正。

Result: 模型在识别不当请求方面表现较好，但在多数情况下仍会执行这些请求。通过对请求特征的分析发现，将请求重述为关于可行性的询问，而非指令，显著提高了拒绝率。两种系统提示修正方法也有效提高了对对抗性请求的拒绝率。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了大型语言模型在保护科学研究诚信方面的重要作用，但仍存在不足。通过进一步了解失败原因并测试减少这些问题的方法，可以促进更可靠的AI赋能科学。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) support scientific research, but some of that assistance can also undermine research integrity. Existing safety evaluations in science often measure whether models refuse misconduct requests, but rarely explain whether they even recognize the problem or why they comply. We investigate this gap across 8 LLMs using SciIntBench, an existing benchmark of 1,350 prompts about scientific research integrity. We examine the reasons behind compliance in four steps. First, when asked directly whether fulfilling a request would involve misconduct, models overwhelmingly identify such requests, even in the Most Covert cases (91%), while they refuse to carry out the request in only 34% of cases. Second, we examine which request characteristics are associated with refusal using features that capture how the request is framed, how much the assistant has been told about what it is helping to hide, and how the goal and the user's stake are stated, among others. Third, we estimate that the most significant effect was to rephrase requests as questions about permissibility rather than instructions, which increased refusal from 52% to 88%. Finally, we test two system-prompt fixes: asking models to assess the request before responding and reminding them of responsible conduct of research principles. These changes increase refusal rates on adversarial requests from 58% to 87% and 86%, respectively. Our findings show that models recognize wrongful requests but still help users violate integrity. Understanding why these failures occur and testing ways to reduce them offer promising paths toward more trustworthy AI for science.

</details>


### [289] [Coordinated Use of Large Language Models in Academic Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.25502)
*Vladislav Marinov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多语言模型协调使用的框架，强调虽然使用多个模型本身并不能保证结果的可靠性，但关键在于认知和验证操作的组织方式。通过七个原则来实现，涉及程序独立、功能分配等，并提出非线性的多语言模型协议，实证研究展示了这种方法的实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前学术研究中使用单一语言模型存在局限，论文旨在提出一种新的框架，以提高研究结果的可靠性和透明度。

Method: 该研究基于七个基本原则构建了框架，包括程序独立性、功能分配、差异诊断价值、一致性诊断价值、外部验证、检查比例以及人类知识责任。此外，还提出了一种非线性的多语言模型协议，通过对比和审查独立获得的结果，并在必要时与外部数据源进行验证。

Result: 论文通过对实际研究案例的分析，展示了这种方法的应用效果，并证明了从单一模型转向协调的多语言模型过程是一种研究组织的改变，研究者在其中承担最终决定权和学术责任。

Conclusion: 研究结论强调，多语言模型的协调使用需要精心组织和管理，以确保最终结果的有效性。这种新的方法学框架为未来的学术研究提供了重要的指导。

Abstract: This study proposes a methodological framework for the coordinated use of multiple large language models in academic research. Its central argument is that involving more models does not in itself guarantee more reliable results; what matters is how cognitive and verification operations are organised.
